\mathcal{D}_{\tau}
, where
\tau
represents the index of total time steps. Then the algorithm determines the cluster and makes a cluster-based recommendation. This process is similar to RCLUMB. After updating the information (Algo.
15
Line12), the agent checks if a split or a merge is possible (Algo.
15
Line13-17).

By our assumption, users in the same cluster have the same vectors. So a cluster can be regarded as a good cluster only when all the estimated user vectors are close to the estimated cluster vector. We call a user is consistent with the cluster if their estimated vectors are close enough. If a user is inconsistent with its current cluster, the agent will split it out. Two clusters are consistent when their estimated vectors are close, and the agent will merge them.

RSCLUMB maintains two sets of estimated cluster vectors: (i) cluster-level estimation with integrated user information, which is for recommendations (Line
12
and Line
10
in Algo.
15
); (ii) the average of estimated user vectors, which is used for robust clustering (Line
3
in Algo.
16
and Line
2
in Algo.
17
). The previous set-based CB work
[
142
]
only uses (i) for both recommendations and clustering, which would lead to erroneous clustering under misspecifications, and cannot get any non-vacuous regret bound in CBMUM.

Algorithm 15

Robust Set-based Clustering of Misspecified Bandits Algorithm (RSCLUMB)

1:

Input:
Deletion parameter
\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2}>0
,
f(T)=\sqrt{\frac{1+\ln(1+T)}{1+T}}
,
\lambda,\beta,\epsilon_{*}>0
.

•

\bm{M}_{i,0}=0_{d\times d},\bm{b}_{i,0}=0_{d\times 1},T_{i,0}=0
,
\forall{i\in\mathcal{U}}
;

and the single cluster
\bm{S}_{1}
by
\bm{M}_{1}=0_{d\times d}
,
\bm{b}_{1}=0_{d\times 1}

,

r_{\tau}
.

\displaystyle\bm{M}_{i_{\tau},\tau}

\displaystyle=\bm{M}_{i_{\tau},\tau-1}+\bm{x}_{a_{\tau}}\bm{x}_{a_{\tau}}^{\mathrm{T}},\bm{b}_{i_{\tau},\tau}=\bm{b}_{i_{\tau},\tau-1}+r_{\tau}\bm{x}_{a_{\tau}},

\displaystyle T_{i_{\tau,\tau}}

\displaystyle=T_{i_{\tau},\tau-1}+1,\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i_{\tau},\tau}=(\lambda\bm{I}+\bm{M}_{i_{\tau},\tau})^{-1}\bm{b}_{i_{\tau},\tau}

\displaystyle\bm{M}_{V_{j},\tau}

\displaystyle=\bm{M}_{V_{j},\tau-1}+\bm{x}_{a_{\tau}}\bm{x}_{a_{\tau}}^{\mathrm{T}},\bm{b}_{V_{j},\tau}=\bm{b}_{V_{j},\tau-1}+r_{\tau}\bm{x}_{\tau},

\displaystyle T_{V_{j},\tau}

\displaystyle=T_{V_{j},\tau-1}+1,\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{V_{j},\tau}=(\lambda\bm{I}+\bm{M}_{V_{j},\tau})^{-1}\bm{b}_{V_{j},\tau},

\displaystyle\tilde{\bm{\theta}}_{V_{j},\tau}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sum_{i\in V_{j}}\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i},\tau}{[V_{j}]}

if

i_{\tau}
is unchecked

Split

Algorithm 16

Split

1:

Define
F(T)=\sqrt{\frac{1+\ln(1+T)}{1+T}}

2:

then

Split user

from cluster

of user

\displaystyle\bm{M}_{V_{j},\tau}

\displaystyle=\bm{M}_{V_{j},\tau}-\bm{M}_{i_{\tau},\tau},\bm{b}_{V_{j}}=\bm{b}_{V_{j}}-\bm{b}_{i_{\tau},\tau},

\displaystyle=T_{i_{\tau},\tau},C_{j^{\prime},\tau}=\{i_{\tau}\}

for

\displaystyle\bm{M}_{V_{j_{1}}}

, delete
V_{j_{2}}

4:

end

for

A.3.12
Main Theorem and Lemmas of RSCLUMB

Theorem A.3.5

(main result on regret bound for RSCLUMB)
.

With the same assumptions in Theorem
6.4.3
, the expected regret of the RSCLUMB algorithm for T rounds satisfies:

\displaystyle R(T)

\displaystyle\leq O\bigg(u\left(\frac{d}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}(\gamma_{1}-\zeta_{1})^{2}}+\frac{1}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}^{2}}\right)\log T+\frac{\epsilon_{*}\sqrt{d}T}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}^{1.5}}+\epsilon_{*}T\sqrt{md\log T}+d\sqrt{mT}\log T+\epsilon_{*}\sqrt{\frac{1}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}}}T\bigg)

(A.149)

\displaystyle\leq O(\epsilon_{*}T\sqrt{md\log T}+d\sqrt{mT}\log T)

(A.150)

Lemma A.3.6
.

For RSCLUMB, we use
T_{1}
to represent the corresponding
T_{0}
of RCLUMB. Then :

\displaystyle T_{1}

\displaystyle\triangleq 16u\log(\frac{u}{\delta})+4u\max\{\frac{16}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}^{2}}\log(\frac{8d}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}^{2}\delta}),\frac{8d}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}(\frac{\gamma_{1}}{6}-\epsilon_{*}\sqrt{\frac{1}{2\tilde{{\lambda}}_{x}}})^{2}}\log(\frac{u}{\delta})\}

\displaystyle=O\bigg(u\left(\frac{d}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}(\gamma_{1}-\zeta_{1})^{2}}+\frac{1}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}^{2}}\right)\log\frac{1}{\delta}\bigg)

Lemma A.3.7
.

For RSCLUMB, after
2T_{1}+1
rounds: in each phase, after the first
u
rounds, with probability at least
1-5\delta
:

\displaystyle\left|\bm{x}_{a}^{\top}(\bm{\theta}_{i_{t}}-\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{\overline{V}_{t},t-1})\right|

\displaystyle\leq(\frac{3\epsilon_{*}\sqrt{2d}}{2\tilde{\lambda}_{x}^{\frac{3}{2}}}+6\epsilon_{*}\sqrt{\frac{1}{2\tilde{\lambda}_{x}}})\mathbb{I}\{\overline{V}_{t}\notin V\}+\beta\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a}\right\rVert_{\overline{\bm{M}}_{\overline{V}_{t},t-1}^{-1}}+\epsilon_{*}\sum_{s\in[t-1]\atop i_{s}\in\overline{V}_{t}}\left|\bm{x}_{a}^{\top}\overline{\bm{M}}_{\overline{V}_{t},t-1}^{-1}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\right|

\displaystyle\triangleq(\frac{3\epsilon_{*}\sqrt{2d}}{2\tilde{\lambda}_{x}^{\frac{3}{2}}}+6\epsilon_{*}\sqrt{\frac{1}{2\tilde{\lambda}_{x}}})\mathbb{I}\{\overline{V}_{t}\notin V\}+C_{a,t}\,

A.3.13
Proof of Lemma
A.3.7

\displaystyle\lvert\bm{x}_{a}^{\mathrm{T}}(\bm{\theta}_{i}-\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{\overline{V}_{t},t})\rvert

\displaystyle=\lvert\bm{x}_{a}^{\mathrm{T}}(\bm{\theta}_{i}-\bm{\theta}_{V_{t}})\rvert+\lvert\bm{x}_{a}^{\mathrm{T}}(\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{\overline{V}_{t},t}-\bm{\theta}_{V_{t}})\rvert

(A.151)

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a}^{\mathrm{T}}\right\rVert\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{i}-\bm{\theta}_{V_{t}}\right\rVert+\lvert\bm{x}_{a}^{\mathrm{T}}(\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{\overline{V}_{t},t}-\bm{\theta}_{V_{t}})\rvert

\displaystyle\leq 6\epsilon_{*}\sqrt{\frac{1}{2\tilde{\lambda}_{x}}}+\lvert\bm{x}_{a}^{\mathrm{T}}(\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{\overline{V}_{t},t}-\bm{\theta}_{V_{t}})\rvert

where the last inequality holds due to the fact
\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a}\right\rVert\leq 1
and the condition of "split" and "merge". For
\lvert\bm{x}_{a}^{\mathrm{T}}(\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{\overline{V}_{t},t}-\bm{\theta}_{V_{t}})\rvert
:

\displaystyle\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{\overline{V}_{t},t-1}-\bm{\theta}_{V_{t}}

\displaystyle=(\sum_{s\in[t-1]\atop i_{s}\in\overline{V}_{t}}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\ \bm{x}_{a_{s}}^{\top}+\lambda\bm{I})^{-1}(\sum_{s\in[t-1]\atop i_{s}\in\overline{V}_{t}}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}r_{s})-\bm{\theta}_{V_{t}}

\displaystyle=(\sum_{s\in[t-1]\atop i_{s}\in\overline{V}_{t}}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\ \bm{x}_{a_{s}}^{\top}+\lambda\bm{I})^{-1}\bigg(\sum_{s\in[t-1]\atop i_{s}\in\overline{V}_{t}}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}(\bm{x}_{a_{s}}^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{i_{s}}+\bm{\epsilon}_{a_{s}}^{i_{s},s}+\eta_{s})\bigg)-\bm{\theta}_{V_{t}}

\displaystyle=\overline{\bm{M}}_{\overline{V}_{t},t-1}^{-1}\sum_{s\in[t-1]\atop i_{s}\in\overline{V}_{t}}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\bm{\epsilon}_{a_{s}}^{i_{s},s}+\overline{\bm{M}}_{\overline{V}_{t},t-1}^{-1}\sum_{s\in[t-1]\atop i_{s}\in\overline{V}_{t}}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\eta_{s}+\overline{\bm{M}}_{\overline{V}_{t},t-1}^{-1}\sum_{s\in[t-1]\atop i_{s}\in\overline{V}_{t}}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{i_{s}}-\bm{\theta}_{V_{t}}

\displaystyle=\overline{\bm{M}}_{\overline{V}_{t},t-1}^{-1}\sum_{s\in[t-1]\atop i_{s}\in\overline{V}_{t}}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\bm{\epsilon}_{a_{s}}^{i_{s},s}+\overline{\bm{M}}_{\overline{V}_{t},t-1}^{-1}\sum_{s\in[t-1]\atop i_{s}\in\overline{V}_{t}}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\eta_{s}+\overline{\bm{M}}_{\overline{V}_{t},t-1}^{-1}\sum_{s\in[t-1]\atop i_{s}\in\overline{V}_{t}}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}^{\top}(\bm{\theta}_{i_{s}}-\bm{\theta}_{V_{t}})

\displaystyle+\overline{\bm{M}}_{\overline{V}_{t},t-1}^{-1}(\sum_{s\in[t-1]\atop i_{s}\in\overline{V}_{t}}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}^{\top}+\lambda\bm{I})\bm{\theta}_{V_{t}}-\lambda\overline{\bm{M}}_{\overline{V}_{t},t-1}^{-1}\bm{\theta}_{V_{t}}-\bm{\theta}_{V_{t}}

\displaystyle=\overline{\bm{M}}_{\overline{V}_{t},t-1}^{-1}\sum_{s\in[t-1]\atop i_{s}\in\overline{V}_{t}}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\bm{\epsilon}_{a_{s}}^{i_{s},s}+\overline{\bm{M}}_{\overline{V}_{t},t-1}^{-1}\sum_{s\in[t-1]\atop i_{s}\in\overline{V}_{t}}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\eta_{s}+\overline{\bm{M}}_{\overline{V}_{t},t-1}^{-1}\sum_{s\in[t-1]\atop i_{s}\in\overline{V}_{t}}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}^{\top}(\bm{\theta}_{i_{s}}-\bm{\theta}_{V_{t}})

\displaystyle-\lambda\overline{\bm{M}}_{\overline{V}_{t},t-1}^{-1}\bm{\theta}_{V_{t}}\,.

Thus, with the same method in Lemma
5.4.4
but replace
\zeta=4\epsilon_{*}\sqrt{\frac{1}{2\tilde{\lambda}_{x}}}
with
\zeta_{1}=6\epsilon_{*}\sqrt{\frac{1}{2\tilde{\lambda}_{x}}}
, and with the previous reasoning, with probability at least
1-5\delta
, we have:

\lvert\bm{x}_{a}^{\mathrm{T}}(\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{\overline{V}_{t},t}-\bm{\theta}_{V_{t}})\rvert\leq C_{a_{t}}+\frac{3\epsilon_{*}\sqrt{2d}}{2\tilde{\lambda}_{x}^{\frac{3}{2}}}

(A.152)

The lemma can be concluded.

A.3.14
Proof of Lemma
A.3.6

With the analysis in the proof of Lemma
A.3.2
, with probability at least
1-\delta
:

\left\lVert\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t}-\bm{\theta}^{j(i)}\right\rVert_{2}\leq\frac{\beta(T_{i,t},\frac{\delta}{u})+\epsilon_{*}\sqrt{T_{i,t}}}{\sqrt{\lambda+\lambda_{\text{min}}(\bm{M}_{i,t})}},\forall{i\in\mathcal{U}}\,,

(A.153)

and the estimated error of the current cluster
\left\lVert\tilde{\bm{\theta}}^{j(i)}-\bm{\theta}^{j(i)}\right\rVert
also satisfies this inequality. For set-based clustering structure, to ensure for each user there is only one
\zeta
-close cluster, we let:

\frac{\beta(T_{i,t},\frac{\delta}{u})+\epsilon_{*}\sqrt{T_{i,t}}}{\sqrt{\lambda+\lambda_{\text{min}}(\bm{M}_{i,t})}}\leq\frac{\gamma_{1}}{6}

(A.154)

By assuming
\lambda<2\log(\frac{u}{\delta})+d\log(1+\frac{T_{i,t}}{\lambda d})
, we can simplify it to

\frac{2\log(\frac{u}{\delta})+d\log(1+\frac{T_{i,t}}{\lambda d})}{2\tilde{\lambda}_{x}T_{i,t}}<\frac{1}{4}(\frac{\gamma_{1}}{6}-\epsilon_{*}\sqrt{\frac{1}{2\tilde{\lambda}_{x}}})^{2}

(A.155)

which can be proved by
\frac{2\log(\frac{u}{\delta)}}{2\tilde{\lambda}_{x}T_{i,t}}\leq\frac{1}{8}(\frac{\gamma_{1}}{6}-\epsilon_{*}\sqrt{\frac{1}{2\tilde{\lambda}_{x}}})^{2}
and
\frac{d\log(1+\frac{T_{i,t}}{\lambda d})}{2\tilde{\lambda}_{x}T_{i,t}}\leq\frac{1}{8}(\frac{\gamma_{1}}{6}-\epsilon_{*}\sqrt{\frac{1}{2\tilde{\lambda}_{x}}})^{2}
. It’s obvious that the former one can be satisfied by
T_{i,t}\geq\frac{8\log(u/\delta)}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}(\frac{\gamma_{1}}{6}-\epsilon_{*}\sqrt{1/2\tilde{\lambda}_{x}})^{2}}
. As for the latter one, by
[
139
]
Lemma 9, we can get
T_{i,t}\geq\frac{8d\log(\frac{16}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}\lambda(\frac{\gamma_{1}}{6}-\epsilon_{*}\sqrt{1/2\tilde{\lambda}_{x}})^{2}}}{4\tilde{\lambda}_{x}(\frac{\gamma_{1}}{6}-\epsilon_{*}\sqrt{1/2\tilde{\lambda}_{x}})^{2}}
. By assuming
\frac{u}{\delta}\geq\frac{16}{4\tilde{\lambda}_{x}\lambda(\frac{\gamma_{1}}{6}-\epsilon_{*}\sqrt{2/4\tilde{\lambda}_{x}})^{2}}
, the lemma is proved.

A.3.15
Proof of Theorem
A.3.5

After

rounds,in each phase, at most

times split operations will happen, we use

to bound the regret generated in these rounds. Then in the remained rounds the cluster num will be no more than

For the instantaneous regret

\displaystyle R_{t}

\displaystyle=(\bm{x}^{\mathrm{T}}_{a^{*}_{t}}\bm{\theta}_{i_{t}}+\bm{\epsilon}_{a^{*}_{t}}^{i_{t},t})-(\bm{x}^{\mathrm{T}}_{a_{t}}\bm{\theta}_{i_{t}}+\bm{\epsilon}_{a_{t}}^{i_{t},t})

(A.156)

\displaystyle=\bm{x}_{a_{t}^{*}}^{\top}(\bm{\theta}_{i_{t}}-\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{\overline{V}_{t},t-1})+(\bm{x}_{a_{t}^{*}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{\overline{V}_{t},t-1}+C_{a_{t}^{*},t})-(\bm{x}_{a_{t}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{\overline{V}_{t},t-1}+C_{a_{t},t})

\displaystyle+\bm{x}_{a_{t}}^{\top}(\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{\overline{V}_{t},t-1}-\bm{\theta}_{i_{t}})+C_{a_{t},t}-C_{a_{t}^{*},t}+(\bm{\epsilon}^{i_{t},t}_{a_{t}^{*}}-\bm{\epsilon}^{i_{t},t}_{a_{t}})

\displaystyle\leq 2C_{a_{t}}+2\epsilon_{*}+(12\epsilon_{*}\sqrt{\frac{1}{2\tilde{\lambda}_{x}}}+\frac{3\epsilon_{*}\sqrt{2d}}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}^{\frac{3}{2}}})\mathbb{I}(\overline{V}_{t}\notin V)

where the last inequality holds due to the UCB arm selection strategy, the concentration bound given in Lemma

and the fact that

.

Define such events. Let:

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{2}

\displaystyle=\{\text{All clusters $\overline{V}_{t}$ only contain users who satisfy}

\displaystyle\left\lVert\tilde{\bm{\theta}}_{i}-\tilde{\bm{\theta}}_{\overline{V}_{t}}\right\rVert\leq\alpha_{1}(\sqrt{\frac{1+\log(1+T_{i,t})}{1+T_{i,t}}}+\sqrt{\frac{1+\log(1+T_{\overline{V}_{t},t})}{1+T_{\overline{V}_{t},t}}})+\alpha_{2}\epsilon_{*}\}

(A.157)

\mathcal{E}_{3}=\{r_{t}\leq 2C_{a_{t}}+2\epsilon_{*}+12\epsilon_{*}\sqrt{\frac{1}{2\tilde{\lambda}_{x}}}+\frac{3\epsilon_{*}\sqrt{2d}}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\}

\mathcal{E}^{{}^{\prime}}=\mathcal{E}_{2}\cap\mathcal{E}_{3}

From previous analysis, we can know that
\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}_{2})\geq 1-3\delta
and
\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}_{3})\geq 1-2\delta
, thus
\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}^{{}^{\prime}}\geq 1-5\delta)
. By taking
\delta=\frac{1}{T}
, we can get:

\displaystyle E(R_{t})

\displaystyle=P(\mathcal{E})\mathbb{I}\{\mathcal{E}\}R_{t}+P(\bar{\mathcal{E}})\mathbb{I}\{\bar{\mathcal{E}}\}R_{t}

(A.158)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{I}\{\mathcal{E}\}R_{t}+5

\displaystyle\leq 2T_{1}+2\epsilon_{*}T+(12\epsilon_{*}\sqrt{\frac{1}{2\tilde{\lambda}_{x}}}+\frac{3\epsilon_{*}\sqrt{2d}}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}^{\frac{3}{2}}})T+2\sum_{2T_{1}}^{T}C_{a_{t}}+5

Now we need to bound
2\sum_{2T_{1}}^{T}C_{a_{t}}
.
We already know that after
2T_{1}
rounds, in each phase
k
after the first
u
rounds,there will be at most
m
clusters

Consider phase
k
, for simplicity, ignore the fist
u
rounds. For the first term in

:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=T_{k-1}}^{T_{k}}\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a_{t}}\right\rVert_{\overline{\bm{M}}_{\overline{V}_{t},t-1}}^{-1}

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=T_{k-1}}^{T_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{m_{t}}\mathbb{I}\{i\in\overline{V}_{t,j}\}\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a_{t}}\right\rVert_{\bm{\overline{M}}_{\overline{V}_{t,j}}^{-1}}

(A.159)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{m_{t}}\sqrt{\sum_{t=T_{k-1}}^{T_{k}}\mathbb{I}\{i\in V_{t,j}\}\sum_{t=T_{k-1}}^{T_{k}}\mathbb{I}\{i\in V_{t,j}\}\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a_{t}}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{\overline{V}_{t,j}}^{-1}}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{m_{t}}\sqrt{2T_{k,j}d\log(1+\frac{T}{\lambda d})}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2m(T_{k}-T_{k-1})d\log(1+\frac{T}{\lambda d})}

For all phases:

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{s}\sqrt{2m(T_{k+1}-T_{k})d\log(1+\frac{T}{\lambda d})}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2\sum_{k=1}^{s}1\sum_{k=1}^{s}(T_{k+1}-T_{k})md\log(1+\frac{T}{\lambda d})}

(A.160)

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2mdT\log(T)\log(1+\frac{T}{\lambda d})}

Similarly, for the second term in
C_{a_{t}}
:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=T_{k-1}}^{T_{k}}\sum_{s\in[t-1]\atop i_{s}\in\overline{V}_{t}}\epsilon_{*}\lvert\bm{x}_{a_{t}}^{\mathrm{T}}\bm{\overline{M}}_{\overline{V}_{t,t-1}}^{-1}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\rvert

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=T_{k-1}}^{T_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{m_{t}}\mathbb{I}\{i\in\overline{V}_{t,j}\}\sum_{s\in[t-1]\atop i_{s}\in\overline{V}_{t,j}}\epsilon_{*}\lvert\bm{x}_{a_{t}}^{\mathrm{T}}\bm{\overline{M}}_{\overline{V}_{t,j}^{-1}}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon_{*}\sum_{t=T_{k-1}}^{T_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{m_{t}}\mathbb{I}\{i\in\overline{V}_{t,j}\}\sqrt{\sum_{s\in[t-1]\atop i_{s}\in\overline{V}_{t,j}}1\sum_{s\in[t-1]\atop i_{s}\in\overline{V}_{t,j}}\lvert\bm{x}_{a_{t}}^{T}\bm{\overline{M}}_{\overline{V}_{t,j}^{-1}}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\rvert^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon_{*}\sum_{t=T_{k-1}}^{T_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{m_{t}}\mathbb{I}\{i\in\overline{V}_{t,j}\}\sqrt{T_{k,j}\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a_{t}}\right\rVert_{\bm{\overline{M}}_{\overline{V}_{t,j}}^{-1}}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon_{*}\sum_{t=T_{k-1}}^{T_{k}}\sqrt{\sum_{j=1}^{m_{t}}\mathbb{I}\{i\in\overline{V}_{t,j}\}\sum_{j=1}^{m_{t}}\mathbb{I}\{i\in\overline{V}_{t,j}\}T_{k,j}\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a_{t}}\right\rVert_{\bm{\overline{M}}_{\overline{V}_{t,j}}^{-1}}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon_{*}\sqrt{(T_{k}-T_{k-1})}\sum_{t=T_{k-1}}^{T_{k}}\sqrt{\sum_{j=1}^{m_{t}}\mathbb{I}\{i\in\overline{V}_{t,j}\}\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a_{t}}\right\rVert_{\bm{\overline{M}}_{\overline{V}_{t,j}}^{-1}}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon_{*}(T_{k}-T_{k-1})\sqrt{2md\log(1+\frac{T}{\lambda d})}

Then for all phases this term can be bounded by
\epsilon_{*}T\sqrt{2md\log(1+\frac{T}{\lambda d})}
.

Thus the total regret can be bounded by:

\displaystyle R_{t}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sqrt{2mTd\log(T)\log(1+\frac{T}{\lambda d})}(\sqrt{2\log(T)+d\log(1+\frac{T}{\lambda d})}+2\sqrt{\lambda})

\displaystyle+2\epsilon_{*}T\sqrt{2md\log(1+\frac{T}{\lambda d})}+2\epsilon_{*}T+12\epsilon_{*}\sqrt{\frac{1}{2\tilde{\lambda}_{x}}}T+\frac{3\epsilon_{*}\sqrt{2d}}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}^{\frac{3}{2}}}T+2T_{1}+u\log(T)+5

where
T_{1}=16u\log(\frac{u}{\delta})+4u\max\{\frac{16}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}^{2}}\log(\frac{8d}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}^{2}\delta}),\frac{8d}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}(\frac{\gamma_{1}}{6}-\epsilon_{*}\sqrt{\frac{1}{2\tilde{{\lambda}}_{x}}})^{2}}\log(\frac{u}{\delta})\}

A.3.16
More Experiments

(a)

Known Misspecification Level

(b)

Unknown Misspecification Level

Figure A.1
:
The cumulative regret of the algorithms under different scales of misspecification level.

For ablation study, we test our algorithms’ performance under different scales of deviation. We test RCLUMB and RSCLUMB when
\epsilon^{*}=0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3\text{ and }0.4
in both misspecification level known and unknown cases. In the known case, we set
\epsilon^{*}
according to the real misspecification level, and we compare our algorithms’ performance to the baselines except LinUCB and CW-OFUL which perform worst; in the unknown case, we keep
\epsilon^{*}=0.2
, and we compare our algorithms to RLinUCB-Ind as only it has the pre-spicified parameter
\epsilon^{*}
among the baselines. The results are shown in Fig.
A.1
. We plot each algorithm’s final cumulative regret under different misspecification levels. All the algorithms’ performances get worse when the deviation gets larger, and our two algorithms always perform better than the baselines. Besides, the regrets in the unknown case are only slightly larger than the known case. These results can match our theoretical results and again show our algorithms’ effectiveness, as well as verify that our algorithm can handle the unknown misspecification level.

A.4
Appendix for chapter
6

A.4.1
Proof of Lemma
A.3.2

We first prove the following result:

With probability at least
1-\delta
for some
\delta\in(0,1)
, at any
t\in[T]
:

\left\lVert\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t}-\bm{\theta}^{j(i)}\right\rVert_{2}\leq\frac{\beta(T_{i,t},\frac{\delta}{u})}{\sqrt{\lambda+\lambda_{\text{min}}(\bm{M}_{i,t})}},\forall{i\in\mathcal{U}}\,,

(A.161)

where
\beta(T_{i,t},\frac{\delta}{u})\triangleq\sqrt{2\log(\frac{u}{\delta})+d\log(1+\frac{T_{i,t}}{\lambda d})}+\sqrt{\lambda}+\alpha C
.

\displaystyle\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t}-\bm{\theta}^{j(i)}

\displaystyle=(\lambda\bm{I}+\bm{M}_{i,t})^{-1}\bm{b}_{i,t}-\bm{\theta}^{j(i)}

\displaystyle=(\lambda\bm{I}+\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}=i}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}^{\top})^{-1}\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}=i}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}r_{s}-\bm{\theta}^{j(i)}

\displaystyle=(\lambda\bm{I}+\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}=i}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}^{\top})^{-1}\bigg(\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}=i}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}(\bm{x}_{a_{s}}^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{i_{s}}+\eta_{s}+c_{s})\bigg)-\bm{\theta}^{j(i)}

\displaystyle=(\lambda\bm{I}+\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}=i}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}^{\top})^{-1}\bigg[(\lambda\bm{I}+\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}=i}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}^{\top})\bm{\theta}^{j(i)}-\lambda\bm{\theta}^{j(i)}

\displaystyle\hskip 11.74988pt\hskip 11.74988pt+\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}=i}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\eta_{s}+\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}=i}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}c_{s}\bigg]-\bm{\theta}^{j(i)}

\displaystyle=-\lambda\bm{M}_{i,t}^{\prime-1}\bm{\theta}^{j(i)}+{\bm{M}_{i,t}^{\prime-1}}\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}=i}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\eta_{s}+\bm{M}_{i,t}^{\prime-1}\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}=i}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}c_{s}\,,

where we denote
{\bm{M}_{i,t}^{\prime}}=\bm{M}_{i,t}+\lambda\bm{I}
, and the above equations hold by definition.

Therefore, we have

\left\lVert\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t}-\bm{\theta}^{j(i)}\right\rVert_{2}\leq\lambda\left\lVert\bm{M}_{i,t}^{\prime-1}\bm{\theta}^{j(i)}\right\rVert_{2}+\left\lVert\bm{M}_{i,t}^{\prime-1}\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}=i}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\eta_{s}\right\rVert_{2}+\left\lVert\bm{M}_{i,t}^{\prime-1}\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}=i}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}c_{s}\right\rVert_{2}\,.

(A.162)

We then bound the three terms in Eq.(
A.162
) one by one. For the first term:

\lambda\left\lVert\bm{M}_{i,t}^{\prime-1}\bm{\theta}^{j(i)}\right\rVert_{2}\leq\lambda\left\lVert{\bm{M}_{i,t}^{\prime-\frac{1}{2}}}\right\rVert_{2}^{2}\left\lVert\bm{\theta}^{j(i)}\right\rVert_{2}\leq\frac{\sqrt{\lambda}}{\sqrt{\lambda_{\text{min}}({\bm{M}_{i,t}^{\prime}})}}\,,

(A.163)

where we use the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality, the inequality for the operator norm of matrices, and the fact that
\lambda_{\text{min}}({\bm{M}_{i,t}^{\prime}})\geq\lambda
.

For the second term in Eq.(
A.162
), we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert\bm{M}_{i,t}^{\prime-1}\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}=i}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\eta_{s}\right\rVert_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\bm{M}_{i,t}^{\prime-\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}=i}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\eta_{s}\right\rVert_{2}\left\lVert{\bm{M}_{i,t}^{\prime-\frac{1}{2}}}\right\rVert_{2}

(A.164)

\displaystyle=\frac{\left\lVert\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}=i}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\eta_{s}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{i,t}^{\prime-1}}}{\sqrt{\lambda_{\text{min}}({\bm{M}_{i,t}^{\prime}})}}\,,

(A.165)

where Eq.(
A.164
) follows by the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality and the inequality for the operator norm of matrices, and Eq.(
A.165
) follows by the Courant-Fischer theorem.

Let
\tilde{\bm{x}}_{s}\triangleq\sqrt{w_{i_{s},s}}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}
,
\tilde{\eta}_{s}\triangleq\sqrt{w_{i_{s},s}}\eta_{s}
, then we have:
\left\lVert\tilde{\bm{x}}_{s}\right\rVert_{2}\leq\left\lVert\sqrt{w_{i_{s},s}}\right\rVert_{2}\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\right\rVert_{2}\leq 1
,
\tilde{\eta}_{s}
is still 1-sub-gaussian (since
\eta_{s}
is 1-sub-gaussian and
\sqrt{w_{i_{s},s}}\leq 1
),
{\bm{M}_{i,t}^{\prime}}=\lambda\bm{I}+\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}=i}\tilde{\bm{x}}_{s}\tilde{\bm{x}}_{s}^{\top}
, and the nominator in Eq.(
A.165
) becomes
\left\lVert\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}=i}\tilde{\bm{x}}_{s}\tilde{\eta}_{s}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{i,t}^{\prime-1}}
. Then, following Theorem 1 in
[
1
]
and by union bound, with probability at least
1-\delta
for some
\delta\in(0,1)
, for any
i\in\mathcal{U}
, we have:

\displaystyle\left\lVert\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}=i}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\eta_{s}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{i,t}^{\prime-1}}

\displaystyle=\left\lVert\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}=i}\tilde{\bm{x}}_{s}\tilde{\eta}_{s}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{i,t}^{\prime-1}}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2\log(\frac{u}{\delta})+\log(\frac{\text{det}({\bm{M}_{i,t}^{\prime}})}{\text{det}(\lambda\bm{I})})}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2\log(\frac{u}{\delta})+d\log(1+\frac{T_{i,t}}{\lambda d})}\,,

(A.166)

where
\text{det}(\cdot)
denotes the determinant of matrix arguement, Eq.(
A.166
) is because
\text{det}({\bm{M}_{i,t}^{\prime}})\leq\Bigg(\frac{\text{trace}(\lambda\bm{I}+\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}=i}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}^{\top})}{d}\Bigg)^{d}\leq\big(\frac{\lambda d+T_{i,t}}{d}\big)^{d}
, and
\text{det}(\lambda\bm{I})=\lambda^{d}
.

For the third term in Eq.(
A.162
), we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert\bm{M}_{i,t}^{\prime-1}\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}=i}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}c_{s}\right\rVert_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\bm{M}_{i,t}^{\prime-\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}=i}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}c_{s}\right\rVert_{2}\left\lVert{\bm{M}_{i,t}^{\prime-\frac{1}{2}}}\right\rVert_{2}

(A.167)

\displaystyle=\frac{\left\lVert\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}=i}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}c_{s}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{i,t}^{\prime-1}}}{\sqrt{\lambda_{\text{min}}({\bm{M}_{i,t}^{\prime}})}}

(A.168)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}=i}\left|c_{s}\right|w_{i,s}\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{i,t}^{\prime-1}}}{\sqrt{\lambda_{\text{min}}({\bm{M}_{i,t}^{\prime}})}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\alpha C}{\sqrt{\lambda_{\text{min}}({\bm{M}_{i,t}^{\prime}})}}

(A.169)

where Eq.(
A.167
) follows by the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality and the inequality for the operator norm of matrices, Eq.(
A.168
) follows by the Courant-Fischer theorem, and Eq.(
A.169
) is because by definition
w_{i,s}\leq\frac{\alpha}{\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{i,s}^{\prime-1}}}\leq\frac{\alpha}{\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{i,t}^{\prime-1}}}
(since
\bm{M}_{i,t}^{\prime}\succeq\bm{M}_{i,s}^{\prime}
,
\bm{M}_{i,s}^{\prime-1}\succeq\bm{M}_{i,t}^{\prime-1}
,
\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{i,s}^{\prime-1}}\geq\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{i,t}^{\prime-1}}
),
\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left|c_{t}\right|\leq C
.

Combining the above bounds of these three terms, we can get that Eq.(
A.161
) holds.

We then prove the following technical lemma.

Lemma A.4.1
.

Under Assumption
9.4
, at any time
t
, for any fixed unit vector
\bm{\theta}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}

\mathbb{E}_{t}[(\bm{\theta}^{\top}\bm{x}_{a_{t}})^{2}|\left|\mathcal{A}_{t}\right|]\geq\tilde{\lambda}_{x}\triangleq\int_{0}^{\lambda_{x}}(1-e^{-\frac{(\lambda_{x}-x)^{2}}{2\sigma^{2}}})^{K}dx\,,

(A.170)

where
K
is the upper bound of
\left|\mathcal{A}_{t}\right|
for any
t
.

Proof.

The proof of this lemma mainly follows the proof of Claim 1 in
[
81
]
, but with more careful analysis, since their assumption on the arm generation distribution is more stringent than our Assumption
9.4
by putting more restrictions on the variance upper bound
\sigma^{2}
(specifically, they require
\sigma^{2}\leq\frac{\lambda^{2}}{8\log(4K)}
).

Denote the feasible arms at round
t
by
{\mathcal{A}_{t}=\{\bm{x}_{t,1},\bm{x}_{t,2},\ldots,\bm{x}_{t,\left|\mathcal{A}_{t}\right|}\}}
.
Consider the corresponding i.i.d. random variables
\theta_{i}=(\bm{\theta}^{\top}\bm{x}_{t,i})^{2}-\mathbb{E}_{t}[(\bm{\theta}^{\top}\bm{x}_{t,i})^{2}|\left|\mathcal{A}_{t}\right|],i=1,2,\ldots,\left|\mathcal{A}_{t}\right|
. By Assumption
9.4
,
\theta_{i}
s are sub-Gaussian random variables with variance bounded by
\sigma^{2}
. Therefore, for any
\alpha>0
and any
i\in[\left|\mathcal{A}_{t}\right|]
, we have:

\mathbb{P}_{t}(\theta_{i}<-\alpha|\left|\mathcal{A}_{t}\right|)\leq e^{-\frac{\alpha^{2}}{2\sigma^{2}}}\,,

where we use
\mathbb{P}_{t}(\cdot)
to be the shorthand for the conditional probability
\mathbb{P}(\cdot|(i_{1},\mathcal{A}_{1},r_{1}),\ldots,(i_{t-1},\mathcal{A}_{t-1},r_{t-1}),i_{t})
.

By Assumption
9.4
, we can also get that

\mathbb{E}_{t}[(\bm{\theta}^{\top}\bm{x}_{t,i})^{2}|\left|\mathcal{A}_{t}\right|=\mathbb{E}_{t}[\bm{\theta}^{\top}\bm{x}_{t,i}\bm{x}_{t,i}^{\top}\bm{\theta}|\left|\mathcal{A}_{t}\right|]\geq\lambda_{\text{min}}(\mathbb{E}_{\bm{x}\sim\rho}[\bm{x}\bm{x}^{\top}])\geq\lambda_{x}
.
With these inequalities above, we can get

\mathbb{P}_{t}(\min_{i=1,\ldots,\left|\mathcal{A}_{t}\right|}(\bm{\theta}^{\top}\bm{x}_{t,i})^{2}\geq\lambda_{x}-\alpha|\left|\mathcal{A}_{t}\right|)\geq(1-e^{-\frac{\alpha^{2}}{2\sigma^{2}}})^{K}\,.

Therefore, we can get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{t}[(\bm{\theta}^{\top}\bm{x}_{a_{t}})^{2}|\left|\mathcal{A}_{t}\right|]

\displaystyle\geq\mathbb{E}_{t}[\min_{i=1,\ldots,\left|\mathcal{A}_{t}\right|}(\bm{\theta}^{\top}\bm{x}_{t,i})^{2}|\left|\mathcal{A}_{t}\right|]

\displaystyle\geq\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathbb{P}_{t}(\min_{i=1,\ldots,\left|\mathcal{A}_{t}\right|}(\bm{\theta}^{\top}\bm{x}_{t,i})^{2}\geq x|\left|\mathcal{A}_{t}\right|)dx

\displaystyle\geq\int_{0}^{\lambda_{x}}(1-e^{-\frac{(\lambda_{x}-x)^{2}}{2\sigma^{2}}})^{K}dx\triangleq\tilde{\lambda}_{x}

∎

Note that
w_{i,s}=\min\{1,\frac{\alpha}{\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{i,t}^{\prime-1}}}\}
, and we have

\frac{\alpha}{\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{i,t}^{\prime-1}}}=\frac{\alpha}{\sqrt{\bm{x}_{a_{s}}^{\top}\bm{M}_{i,t}^{\prime-1}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}}}\geq\frac{\alpha}{\sqrt{\lambda_{\text{min}}(\bm{M}_{i,t}^{\prime-1})}}=\alpha\sqrt{\lambda_{\text{min}}(\bm{M}_{i,t}^{\prime})}\geq\alpha\sqrt{\lambda}.

Since
\alpha\sqrt{\lambda}<1
typically holds, we have
w_{i,s}\geq\alpha\sqrt{\lambda}
.

Then, with the item regularity assumption stated in Assumption
9.4
, the technical Lemma
A.4.1
, together with Lemma 7 in
[
139
]
, with probability at least
1-\delta
, for a particular user
i
, at any
t
such that
T_{i,t}\geq\frac{16}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}^{2}}\log(\frac{8d}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}^{2}\delta})
, we have:

\lambda_{\text{min}}(\bm{M}_{i,t}^{\prime})\geq 2\alpha\sqrt{\lambda}\tilde{\lambda}_{x}T_{i,t}+\lambda\,.

(A.171)

With this result, together with Eq.(
A.161
), we can get that for any
t
such that
T_{i,t}\geq\frac{16}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}^{2}}\log(\frac{8d}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}^{2}\delta})
, with probability at least
1-\delta
for some
\delta\in(0,1)
,
\forall{i\in\mathcal{U}}
, we have:

\displaystyle\left\lVert\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t}-\bm{\theta}^{j(i)}\right\rVert_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\beta(T_{i,t},\frac{\delta}{u})}{\sqrt{\lambda_{\text{min}}(\bm{M}_{i,t}^{\prime})}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\beta(T_{i,t},\frac{\delta}{u})}{\sqrt{2\alpha\sqrt{\lambda}\tilde{\lambda}_{x}T_{i,t}+\lambda}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\beta(T_{i,t},\frac{\delta}{u})}{\sqrt{2\alpha\sqrt{\lambda}\tilde{\lambda}_{x}T_{i,t}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{2\log(\frac{u}{\delta})+d\log(1+\frac{T_{i,t}}{\lambda d})}+\sqrt{\lambda}+\alpha C}{\sqrt{2\alpha\sqrt{\lambda}\tilde{\lambda}_{x}T_{i,t}}}\,.

(A.172)

Then, we want to find a sufficient time
T_{i,t}
for a fixed user
i
such that

\left\lVert\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t}-\bm{\theta}^{j(i)}\right\rVert_{2}<\frac{\gamma}{4}\,.

(A.173)

To do this, with Eq.(
A.172
), we can get it by letting

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{\lambda}}{\sqrt{2\alpha\sqrt{\lambda}\tilde{\lambda}_{x}T_{i,t}}}

\displaystyle<\frac{\gamma}{12}\,,

(A.174)

\displaystyle\frac{\alpha C}{\sqrt{2\alpha\sqrt{\lambda}\tilde{\lambda}_{x}T_{i,t}}}

\displaystyle<\frac{\gamma}{12}\,,

(A.175)

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{2\log(\frac{u}{\delta})+d\log(1+\frac{T_{i,t}}{\lambda d})}}{\sqrt{2\alpha\sqrt{\lambda}\tilde{\lambda}_{x}T_{i,t}}}

\displaystyle<\frac{\gamma}{12}\,.

(A.176)

For Eq.(
A.174
), we can get

T_{i,t}>\frac{72\sqrt{\lambda}}{\alpha\gamma^{2}\tilde{\lambda}_{x}}\,.

(A.177)

For Eq.(
A.175
), we can get

T_{i,t}>\frac{72\alpha C^{2}}{\gamma^{2}\sqrt{\lambda}\tilde{\lambda}_{x}}\,.

(A.178)

For Eq.(
A.176
), we have

\frac{2\log(\frac{u}{\delta})+d\log(1+\frac{T_{i,t}}{\lambda d})}{2\alpha\sqrt{\lambda}\tilde{\lambda}_{x}T_{i,t}}<\frac{\gamma^{2}}{144}\,.

(A.179)

Then it is sufficient to get Eq.(
A.179
) if the following holds

\displaystyle\frac{2\log(\frac{u}{\delta})}{2\alpha\sqrt{\lambda}\tilde{\lambda}_{x}T_{i,t}}

\displaystyle<\frac{\gamma^{2}}{288}\,,

(A.180)

\displaystyle\frac{d\log(1+\frac{T_{i,t}}{\lambda d})}{2\alpha\sqrt{\lambda}\tilde{\lambda}_{x}T_{i,t}}

\displaystyle<\frac{\gamma^{2}}{288}\,.

(A.181)

For Eq.(
A.180
), we can get

T_{i,t}>\frac{288\log(\frac{u}{\delta})}{\gamma^{2}\alpha\sqrt{\lambda}\tilde{\lambda}_{x}}

(A.182)

For Eq.(
A.181
), we can get

T_{i,t}>\frac{144d}{\gamma^{2}\alpha\sqrt{\lambda}\tilde{\lambda}_{x}}\log(1+\frac{T_{i,t}}{\lambda d})\,.

(A.183)

Following Lemma 9 in
[
139
]
, we can get the following sufficient condition for Eq.(
A.183
):

T_{i,t}>\frac{288d}{\gamma^{2}\alpha\sqrt{\lambda}\tilde{\lambda}_{x}}\log(\frac{288}{\gamma^{2}\alpha\sqrt{\lambda}\tilde{\lambda}_{x}})\,.

(A.184)

Then, since typically
\frac{u}{\delta}>\frac{288}{\gamma^{2}\alpha\sqrt{\lambda}\tilde{\lambda}_{x}}
, we can get the following sufficient condition for Eq.(
A.182
) and Eq.(
A.184
)

T_{i,t}>\frac{288d}{\gamma^{2}\alpha\sqrt{\lambda}\tilde{\lambda}_{x}}\log(\frac{u}{\delta})\,.

(A.185)

Together with Eq.(
A.177
), Eq.(
A.178
), and the condition for Eq.(
A.348
) we can get the following sufficient condition for Eq.(
A.173
) to hold

T_{i,t}>\max\{\frac{288d}{\gamma^{2}\alpha\sqrt{\lambda}\tilde{\lambda}_{x}}\log(\frac{u}{\delta}),\frac{16}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}^{2}}\log(\frac{8d}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}^{2}\delta}),\frac{72\sqrt{\lambda}}{\alpha\gamma^{2}\tilde{\lambda}_{x}},\frac{72\alpha C^{2}}{\gamma^{2}\sqrt{\lambda}\tilde{\lambda}_{x}}\}\,.

(A.186)

Then, with Assumption
9.3
on the uniform arrival of users, following Lemma 8 in
[
139
]
, and by union bound, we can get that with probability at least
1-\delta
, for all

t\geq T_{0}\triangleq 16u\log(\frac{u}{\delta})+4u\max\{\frac{288d}{\gamma^{2}\alpha\sqrt{\lambda}\tilde{\lambda}_{x}}\log(\frac{u}{\delta}),\frac{16}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}^{2}}\log(\frac{8d}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}^{2}\delta}),\frac{72\sqrt{\lambda}}{\alpha\gamma^{2}\tilde{\lambda}_{x}},\frac{72\alpha C^{2}}{\gamma^{2}\sqrt{\lambda}\tilde{\lambda}_{x}}\}\,,

(A.187)

Eq.(
A.185
) holds for all
i\in\mathcal{U}
, and therefore Eq.(
A.173
) holds for all
i\in\mathcal{U}
. With this, we can show that RCLUB-WCU will cluster all the users correctly after
T_{0}
. First, if RCLUB-WCU deletes the edge
(i,l)
, then user
i
and user
j
belong to different ground-truth clusters, i.e.,
\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{i}-\bm{\theta}_{l}\right\rVert_{2}>0
. This is because by the deletion rule of the algorithm, the concentration bound, and triangle inequality,

\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{i}-\bm{\theta}_{l}\right\rVert_{2}=\left\lVert\bm{\theta}^{j(i)}-\bm{\theta}^{j(l)}\right\rVert_{2}\geq\left\lVert\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t}-\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{l,t}\right\rVert_{2}-\left\lVert\bm{\theta}^{j(l)}-\bm{\theta}_{l,t}\right\rVert_{2}-\left\lVert\bm{\theta}^{j(i)}-\bm{\theta}_{i,t}\right\rVert_{2}>0
. Second, we show that if
\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{i}-\bm{\theta}_{l}\right\rVert\geq\gamma
, RCLUB-WCU will delete the edge
(i,l)
. This is because if
\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{i}-\bm{\theta}_{l}\right\rVert\geq\gamma
, then by the triangle inequality, and
\left\lVert\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t}-\bm{\theta}^{j(i)}\right\rVert_{2}<\frac{\gamma}{4}
,
\left\lVert\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{l,t}-\bm{\theta}^{j(l)}\right\rVert_{2}<\frac{\gamma}{4}
,
\bm{\theta}_{i}=\bm{\theta}^{j(i)}
,
\bm{\theta}_{l}=\bm{\theta}^{j(l)}
, we have
\left\lVert\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t}-\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{l,t}\right\rVert_{2}\geq\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{i}-\bm{\theta}_{l}\right\rVert-\left\lVert\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t}-\bm{\theta}^{j(i)}\right\rVert_{2}-\left\lVert\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{l,t}-\bm{\theta}^{j(l)}\right\rVert_{2}>\gamma-\frac{\gamma}{4}-\frac{\gamma}{4}=\frac{\gamma}{2}>\frac{\sqrt{\lambda}+\sqrt{2\log(\frac{u}{\delta})+d\log(1+\frac{T_{i,t}}{\lambda d})}}{\sqrt{\lambda+2\tilde{\lambda}_{x}T_{i,t}}}+\frac{\sqrt{\lambda}+\sqrt{2\log(\frac{u}{\delta})+d\log(1+\frac{T_{l,t}}{\lambda d})}}{\sqrt{\lambda+2\tilde{\lambda}_{x}T_{l,t}}}
, which will trigger the deletion condition Line
10
in Algo.
8
.

A.4.2
Proof of Lemma
6.4.2

After
T_{0}
, if the clustering structure is correct,
i.e.
,
V_{t}=V_{j(i_{t})}
, then we have

\displaystyle\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{V_{t},t-1}-\bm{\theta}_{i_{t}}

\displaystyle=\bm{M}_{{V}_{t},t-1}^{-1}\bm{b}_{{V}_{t},t-1}-\bm{\theta}_{i_{t}}

\displaystyle=(\lambda\bm{I}+\sum_{s\in[t-1]\atop i_{s}\in V_{t}}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}^{\top})^{-1}(\sum_{s\in[t-1]\atop i_{s}\in V_{t}}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}r_{s})-\bm{\theta}_{i_{t}}

\displaystyle=(\lambda\bm{I}+\sum_{s\in[t-1]\atop i_{s}\in V_{t}}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}^{\top})^{-1}\big(\sum_{s\in[t-1]\atop i_{s}\in V_{t}}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}(\bm{x}_{a_{s}}^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{i_{t}}+\eta_{s}+c_{s})\big)-\bm{\theta}_{i_{t}}

(A.188)

\displaystyle=(\lambda\bm{I}+\sum_{s\in[t-1]\atop i_{s}\in V_{t}}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}^{\top})^{-1}\bigg(\sum_{s\in[t-1]\atop i_{s}\in V_{t}}(w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}^{\top}+\lambda\bm{I})\bm{\theta}_{i_{t}}-\lambda\bm{\theta}_{i_{t}}

\displaystyle\hskip 11.74988pt\hskip 11.74988pt+\sum_{s\in[t-1]\atop i_{s}\in V_{t}}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\eta_{s}+\sum_{s\in[t-1]\atop i_{s}\in V_{t}}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}c_{s})\bigg)-\bm{\theta}_{i_{t}}

\displaystyle=-\lambda\bm{M}_{{V}_{t},t-1}^{\prime-1}\bm{\theta}_{i_{t}}-\bm{M}_{{V}_{t},t-1}^{\prime-1}\sum_{s\in[t-1]\atop i_{s}\in V_{t}}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\eta_{s}+\bm{M}_{{V}_{t},t-1}^{\prime-1}\sum_{s\in[t-1]\atop i_{s}\in V_{t}}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}c_{s}\,,

where we denote
\bm{M}_{{V}_{t},t-1}^{\prime}=\bm{M}_{{V}_{t},t-1}+\lambda\bm{I}
, and Eq.(
A.188
) is because
V_{t}=V_{j(i_{t})}
thus
\bm{\theta}_{i_{s}}=\bm{\theta}_{i_{t}},\forall i_{s}\in V_{t}
.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\left|\bm{x}_{a}^{\top}(\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{V_{t},t-1}-\bm{\theta}_{i_{t}})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\lambda\left|\bm{x}_{a}^{\top}\bm{M}_{{V}_{t},t-1}^{\prime-1}\bm{\theta}_{i_{t}}\right|+\left|\bm{x}_{a}^{\top}\bm{M}_{{V}_{t},t-1}^{\prime-1}\sum_{s\in[t-1]\atop i_{s}\in V_{t}}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\eta_{s}\right|+\left|\bm{x}_{a}^{\top}\bm{M}_{{V}_{t},t-1}^{\prime-1}\sum_{s\in[t-1]\atop i_{s}\in V_{t}}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}c_{s}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{{V}_{t},t-1}^{\prime-1}}\bigg(\sqrt{\lambda}+\left\lVert\sum_{s\in[t-1]\atop i_{s}\in V_{t}}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\eta_{s}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{{V}_{t},t-1}^{\prime-1}}+\left\lVert\sum_{s\in[t-1]\atop i_{s}\in V_{t}}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}c_{s}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{{V}_{t},t-1}^{\prime-1}}\bigg)\,,

(A.189)

where Eq.(
A.189
) is by Cauchy–Schwarz inequality, matrix operator inequality, and
\left|\bm{x}_{a}^{\top}\bm{M}_{{V}_{t},t-1}^{\prime-1}\bm{\theta}_{i_{t}}\right|\leq\lambda\left\lVert\bm{M}_{{V}_{t},t-1}^{\prime-\frac{1}{2}}\right\rVert_{2}\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{i_{t}}\right\rVert_{2}=\lambda\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda_{\text{min}}(\bm{M}_{{V}_{t},t-1})}}\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{i_{t}}\right\rVert_{2}\leq\sqrt{\lambda}
since
\lambda_{\text{min}}(\bm{M}_{{V}_{t},t-1})\geq\lambda
and
\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{i_{t}}\right\rVert_{2}\leq 1
.

Let
\tilde{\bm{x}}_{s}\triangleq\sqrt{w_{i_{s},s}}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}
,
\tilde{\eta}_{s}\triangleq\sqrt{w_{i_{s},s}}\eta_{s}
, then we have:
\left\lVert\tilde{\bm{x}}_{s}\right\rVert_{2}\leq\left\lVert\sqrt{w_{i_{s},s}}\right\rVert_{2}\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\right\rVert_{2}\leq 1
,
\tilde{\eta}_{s}
is still 1-sub-gaussian (since
\eta_{s}
is 1-sub-gaussian and
\sqrt{w_{i_{s},s}}\leq 1
),
{\bm{M}_{i,t}^{\prime}}=\lambda\bm{I}+\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}=i}\tilde{\bm{x}}_{s}\tilde{\bm{x}}_{s}^{\top}
, and
\left\lVert\sum_{s\in[t-1]\atop i_{s}\in V_{t}}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\eta_{s}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{{V}_{t},t-1}^{\prime-1}}
becomes
\left\lVert\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}=i}\tilde{\bm{x}}_{s}\tilde{\eta}_{s}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{{V}_{t},t-1}^{\prime-1}}
. Then, following Theorem 1 in
[
1
]
, with probability at least
1-\delta
for some
\delta\in(0,1)
, we have:

\displaystyle\left\lVert\sum_{s\in[t-1]\atop i_{s}\in V_{t}}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\eta_{s}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{{V}_{t},t-1}^{\prime-1}}

\displaystyle=\left\lVert\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}=i}\tilde{\bm{x}}_{s}\tilde{\eta}_{s}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{{V}_{t},t-1}^{\prime-1}}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2\log(\frac{u}{\delta})+\log(\frac{\text{det}(\bm{M}_{{V}_{t},t-1}^{\prime})}{\text{det}(\lambda\bm{I})})}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2\log(\frac{u}{\delta})+d\log(1+\frac{T}{\lambda d})}\,,

(A.190)

And for
\left\lVert\sum_{s\in[t-1]\atop i_{s}\in V_{t}}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}c_{s}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{{V}_{t},t-1}^{\prime-1}}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert\sum_{s\in[t-1]\atop i_{s}\in V_{t}}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}c_{s}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{{V}_{t},t-1}^{\prime-1}}\leq\sum_{s\in[t-1]\atop i_{s}\in V_{t}}w_{i_{s},s}\left|c_{s}\right|\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{{V}_{t},t-1}^{\prime-1}}\leq\alpha C\,,

(A.191)

where we use
\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left|c_{t}\right|\leq C
,
w_{i_{s},s}\leq\frac{\alpha}{\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{i_{s},t-1}^{\prime-1}}}\leq\frac{\alpha}{\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{{V}_{t},t-1}^{\prime-1}}}
.

Plugging Eq.(
A.191
) and Eq.(
A.190
) into Eq.(
A.189
), together with Lemma
A.3.2
, we can complete the proof of Lemma
6.4.2
.

A.4.3
Proof of Theorem
6.4.3

After
T_{0}
, we define event

\mathcal{E}=\{\text{the algorithm clusters all the users correctly for all }t\geq T_{0}\}\,.

(A.192)

Then, with Lemma
A.3.2
and picking
\delta=\frac{1}{T}
, we have

\displaystyle R(T)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E})\mathbb{I}\{\mathcal{E}\}R(T)+\mathbb{P}(\overline{\mathcal{E}})\mathbb{I}\{\overline{\mathcal{E}}\}R(T)

(A.193)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{I}\{\mathcal{E}\}R(T)+4\times\frac{1}{T}\times T

\displaystyle=\mathbb{I}\{\mathcal{E}\}R(T)+4\,.

Then it remains to bound
\mathbb{I}\{\mathcal{E}\}R(T)
. For the first
T_{0}
rounds, we can upper bound the regret in the first
T_{0}
rounds by
T_{0}
. After
T_{0}
, under event
\mathcal{E}
and by Lemma
6.4.2
, we have that with probability at least
1-\delta
, for any
\bm{x}_{a}
:

\left|\bm{x}_{a}^{\mathrm{T}}(\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{V_{t},t-1}-\bm{\theta}_{i_{t}})\right|\leq\beta\left\lVert\bm{x_{a}}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}^{-1}_{V_{t},t-1}}\triangleq C_{a,t}\,.

(A.194)

Therefore, for the instantaneous regret
R_{t}
at round
t
, with
\mathcal{E}
, with probability at least
1-\delta
, at
\forall{t\geq T_{0}}
:

\displaystyle R_{t}

\displaystyle=\bm{x}_{a_{t}^{*}}^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{i_{t}}-\bm{x}_{a_{t}}^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{i_{t}}

(A.195)

\displaystyle=\bm{x}_{a_{t}^{*}}^{\top}(\bm{\theta}_{i_{t}}-\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{V_{t},t-1})+(\bm{x}_{a_{t}^{*}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{V_{t},t-1}+C_{a_{t}^{*},t})-(\bm{x}_{a_{t}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{V_{t},t-1}+C_{a_{t},t})

\displaystyle+\bm{x}_{a_{t}}^{\top}(\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{\overline{V}_{t},t-1}-\bm{\theta}_{i_{t}})+C_{a_{t},t}-C_{a_{t}^{*},t}

\displaystyle\leq 2C_{a_{t},t}\,,

where the last inequality holds by the UCB arm selection strategy in Eq.(
6.3
) and Eq.(
A.194
).

Therefore, for
\mathbb{I}\{\mathcal{E}\}R(T)
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{I}\{\mathcal{E}\}R(T)

\displaystyle\leq R(T_{0})+\mathbb{E}[\mathbb{I}\{\mathcal{E}\}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}R_{t}]

\displaystyle\leq T_{0}+2\mathbb{E}[\mathbb{I}\{\mathcal{E}\}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}C_{a_{t},t}]\,.

(A.196)

Then it remains to bound
\mathbb{E}[\mathbb{I}\{\mathcal{E}\}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}C_{a_{t},t}]
.
For
\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}C_{a_{t},t}
, we can distinguish it into two cases:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}C_{a_{t},t}

\displaystyle\leq\beta\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\lVert\bm{x_{a_{t}}}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}^{-1}_{V_{t},t-1}}

\displaystyle=\beta\sum_{t\in[T]:w_{i_{t},t}=1}\left\lVert\bm{x_{a_{t}}}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}^{-1}_{V_{t},t-1}}+\beta\sum_{t\in[T]:w_{i_{t},t}<1}\left\lVert\bm{x_{a_{t}}}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}^{-1}_{V_{t},t-1}}\,.

(A.197)

Then, we prove the following technical lemma.

Lemma A.4.2
.

\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}\min\{\mathbb{I}\{i_{t}\in V_{j}\}\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a_{t}}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{V_{j},t-1}^{-1}}^{2},1\}\leq 2d\log(1+\frac{T}{\lambda d}),\forall{j\in[m]}\,.

(A.198)

Proof.

\displaystyle det(\bm{M}_{V_{j},T})

\displaystyle=det\bigg(\bm{M}_{V_{j},T-1}+\mathbb{I}\{i_{T}\in V_{j}\}\bm{x}_{a_{T}}\bm{x}_{a_{T}}^{\top}\bigg)

\displaystyle=det(\bm{M}_{V_{j},T-1})det\bigg(\bm{I}+\mathbb{I}\{i_{T}\in V_{j}\}\bm{M}_{V_{j},T-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\bm{x}_{a_{T}}\bm{x}_{a_{T}}^{\top}\bm{M}_{V_{j},T-1}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\bigg)

\displaystyle=det(\bm{M}_{V_{j},T-1})\bigg(1+\mathbb{I}\{i_{T}\in V_{j}\}\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a_{T}}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{V_{j},T-1}^{-1}}^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle=det(\bm{M}_{V_{j},T_{0}})\prod_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}\bigg(1+\mathbb{I}\{i_{t}\in V_{j}\}\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a_{t}}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{V_{j},t-1}^{-1}}^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle\geq det(\lambda\bm{I})\prod_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}\bigg(1+\mathbb{I}\{i_{t}\in V_{j}\}\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a_{t}}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{V_{j},t-1}^{-1}}^{2}\bigg)\,.

(A.199)

\forall{x\in[0,1]}
, we have
x\leq 2\log(1+x)
. Therefore

\displaystyle\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}\min\{\mathbb{I}\{i_{t}\in V_{j}\}\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a_{t}}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{V_{j},t-1}^{-1}}^{2},1\}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}\log\bigg(1+\mathbb{I}\{i_{t}\in V_{j}\}\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a_{t}}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{V_{j},t-1}^{-1}}^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle=2\log\bigg(\prod_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T}\big(1+\mathbb{I}\{i_{t}\in V_{j}\}\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a_{t}}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{V_{j},t-1}^{-1}}^{2}\big)\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq 2[\log(det(\bm{M}_{V_{j},T}))-\log(det(\lambda\bm{I}))]

\displaystyle\leq 2\log\bigg(\frac{trace(\lambda\bm{I}+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}\{i_{t}\in V_{j}\}\bm{x}_{a_{t}}\bm{x}_{a_{t}}^{\top})}{\lambda d}\bigg)^{d}

\displaystyle\leq 2d\log(1+\frac{T}{\lambda d})\,.

(A.200)

∎

Denote the rounds with
w_{i_{t},t}=1
as
\{\tilde{t}_{1},\ldots,\tilde{t}_{l_{1}}\}
, and gram matrix
\tilde{\bm{G}}_{V_{\tilde{t}_{\tau}},\tilde{t}_{\tau}-1}\triangleq\lambda\bm{I}+\sum_{s\in[\tau]\atop i_{s}\in V_{\tilde{t}_{\tau}}}\bm{x}_{a_{\tilde{t}_{s}}}\bm{x}_{a_{\tilde{t}_{s}}}^{\top}
; denote the rounds with
w_{i_{t},t}<1
as
\{{t}^{\prime}_{1},\ldots,{t}^{\prime}_{l_{2}}\}
, gram matrix
{\bm{G}}^{\prime}_{V_{t^{\prime}_{\tau}},t^{\prime}_{\tau}-1}\triangleq\lambda\bm{I}+\sum_{s\in[\tau]\atop i_{s}\in V_{{t}^{\prime}_{\tau}}}w_{i_{{t}^{\prime}_{s}},{t}^{\prime}_{s}}\bm{x}_{a_{{t}^{\prime}_{s}}}\bm{x}_{a_{{t}^{\prime}_{s}}}^{\top}
.

Then we have

\displaystyle\sum_{t\in[T]:w_{i_{t},t}=1}\left\lVert\bm{x_{a_{t}}}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}^{-1}_{V_{t},t-1}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{m}\sum_{\tau=1}^{l_{1}}\mathbb{I}\{i_{\tilde{t}_{\tau}}\in V_{j}\}\left\lVert\bm{x_{a_{\tilde{t}_{\tau}}}}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}^{-1}_{V_{\tilde{t}_{\tau},\tilde{t}_{\tau}-1}}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{m}\sum_{\tau=1}^{l_{1}}\mathbb{I}\{i_{\tilde{t}_{\tau}}\in V_{j}\}\left\lVert\bm{x_{a_{\tilde{t}_{\tau}}}}\right\rVert_{\tilde{\bm{G}}^{-1}_{V_{\tilde{t}_{\tau}},\tilde{t}_{\tau}-1}}

(A.201)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{m}\sqrt{\sum_{\tau=1}^{l_{1}}\mathbb{I}\{i_{\tilde{t}_{\tau}}\in V_{j}\}\sum_{\tau=1}^{l_{1}}\min\{1,\mathbb{I}\{i_{\tilde{t}_{\tau}}\in V_{j}\}\left\lVert\bm{x_{a_{\tilde{t}_{\tau}}}}\right\rVert^{2}_{\tilde{\bm{G}}^{-1}_{V_{\tilde{t}_{\tau}},\tilde{t}_{\tau}-1}}\}}

(A.202)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{m}\sqrt{T_{V_{j},T}\times 2d\log(1+\frac{T}{\lambda d})}

(A.203)

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2m\sum_{j=1}^{m}T_{V_{j},T}d\log(1+\frac{T}{\lambda d})}=\sqrt{2mdT\log(1+\frac{T}{\lambda d})}\,,

(A.204)

where Eq.(
A.201
) is because
\tilde{\bm{G}}^{-1}_{V_{\tilde{t}_{\tau}},\tilde{t}_{\tau}-1}\succeq\bm{M}^{-1}_{V_{\tilde{t}_{\tau}},\tilde{t}_{\tau}-1}
in Eq.(
A.202
) we use Cauchy–Schwarz inequality, in Eq.(
A.203
) we use Lemma
A.4.2
and
\sum_{\tau=1}^{l_{1}}\mathbb{I}\{i_{\tilde{t}_{\tau}}\in V_{j}\}\leq T_{V_{j},T}
, in Eq.(
A.204
) we use Cauchy–Schwarz inequality and
\sum_{j=1}^{m}T_{V_{j},T}=T
.

For the second part in Eq.(
A.197
), Let
{\bm{x}}_{a_{{t}^{\prime}_{\tau}}}^{\prime}\triangleq\sqrt{w_{i_{{t}^{\prime}_{\tau}},{t}^{\prime}_{\tau}}}\bm{x}_{a_{{t}^{\prime}_{\tau}}}
, then

\displaystyle\sum_{t:w_{i_{t},t}<1}\left\lVert\bm{x_{a_{t}}}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}^{-1}_{V_{t},t-1}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{t:w_{i_{t},t}<1}\frac{\left\lVert\bm{x_{a_{t}}}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}^{-1}_{V_{t},t-1}}^{2}}{\left\lVert\bm{x_{a_{t}}}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}^{-1}_{V_{t},t-1}}}=\sum_{t:w_{i_{t},t}<1}\frac{w_{i_{t},t}\left\lVert\bm{x_{a_{t}}}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}^{-1}_{V_{t},t-1}}^{2}}{\alpha}

(A.205)

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{m}\sum_{\tau=1}^{l_{2}}\mathbb{I}\{i_{{t}^{\prime}_{\tau}}\in V_{j}\}\frac{w_{i_{{t}^{\prime}_{\tau}},{t}^{\prime}_{\tau}}}{\alpha}\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a_{{t}^{\prime}_{\tau}}}\right\rVert_{{\bm{M}}^{-1}_{V_{t^{\prime}_{\tau}},t^{\prime}_{\tau}-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{m}\frac{\sum_{\tau=1}^{l_{2}}\min\{1,\mathbb{I}\{i_{{t}^{\prime}_{\tau}}\in V_{j}\}\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a_{{t}^{\prime}_{\tau}}}^{\prime}\right\rVert_{{\bm{G}}^{\prime-1}_{V_{t^{\prime}_{\tau}},t^{\prime}_{\tau}-1}}^{2}\}}{\alpha}

(A.206)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{m}\frac{2d\log(1+\frac{T}{\lambda d})}{\alpha}=\frac{2md\log(1+\frac{T}{\lambda d})}{\alpha}

(A.207)

where in Eq.(
A.205
) we use the definition of the weights, in Eq.(
A.206
) we use
{\bm{G}}^{\prime-1}_{V_{t^{\prime}_{\tau}},t^{\prime}_{\tau}-1}\succeq{\bm{M}}^{-1}_{V_{t^{\prime}_{\tau}},t^{\prime}_{\tau}-1}
, and Eq.(
A.207
) uses Lemma
A.4.2
.

Then, with Eq.(
A.207
), Eq.(
A.204
), Eq.(
A.197
), Eq.(
A.193
), Eq.(
A.196
),
\delta=\frac{1}{T}
, and
\beta=\sqrt{\lambda}+\sqrt{2\log(T)+d\log(1+\frac{T}{\lambda d})}+\alpha C
, we can get

\displaystyle R(T)

\displaystyle\leq 4+T_{0}+\big(2\sqrt{\lambda}+\sqrt{2\log(T)+d\log(1+\frac{T}{\lambda d})}+\alpha C\big)\times\bigg(\sqrt{2mdT\log(1+\frac{T}{\lambda d})}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\hskip 9.24994pt+\frac{2md\log(1+\frac{T}{\lambda d})}{\alpha}\bigg)

\displaystyle=4+16u\log(uT)+4u\max\{\frac{288d}{\gamma^{2}\alpha\sqrt{\lambda}\tilde{\lambda}_{x}}\log(uT),\frac{16}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}^{2}}\log(\frac{8dT}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}^{2}}),\frac{72\sqrt{\lambda}}{\alpha\gamma^{2}\tilde{\lambda}_{x}},\frac{72\alpha C^{2}}{\gamma^{2}\sqrt{\lambda}\tilde{\lambda}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\hskip 9.24994pt+\big(2\sqrt{\lambda}+\sqrt{2\log(T)+d\log(1+\frac{T}{\lambda d})}+\alpha C\big)\times\bigg(\sqrt{2mdT\log(1+\frac{T}{\lambda d})}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\hskip 9.24994pt+\frac{2md\log(1+\frac{T}{\lambda d})}{\alpha}\bigg)\,.

Picking
\alpha=\frac{\sqrt{\lambda}+\sqrt{d}}{C}
, we can get

R(T)\leq O\big((\frac{C\sqrt{d}}{\gamma^{2}\tilde{\lambda}_{x}}+\frac{1}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}^{2}})u\log(T)\big)+O\big(d\sqrt{mT}\log(T)\big)+O\big(mCd\log^{1.5}(T)\big)\,.

(A.208)

Thus we complete the proof of Theorem
6.4.3
.

A.4.4
Proof and Discussions of Theorem
6.4.4

Table 1 of the work
[
93
]
gives a lower bound for linear bandits with adversarial corruption for a single user. The lower bound of
R(T)
is given by:

R(T)\geq\Omega(d\sqrt{T}+dC)
. Therefore, suppose our problem with multiple users and
m
underlying clusters where the arrival times are
T_{i}
for each cluster, then for any algorithms, even if they know the underlying clustering structure and keep
m
independent linear bandit algorithms to leverage the common information of clusters, the best they can get is
R(T)\geq dC+\sum_{i\in[m]}d\sqrt{T_{i}}
. For a special case where
T_{i}=\frac{T}{m},\forall i\in[m]
, we can get
R(T)\geq dC+\sum_{i\in[m]}d\sqrt{\frac{T}{m}}=d\sqrt{mT}+dC
, which gives a lower bound of
\Omega(d\sqrt{mT}+dC)
for the LOCUD problem.

Recall that the regret upper bound of RCLUB-WCU shown in Theorem
6.4.3
is of
O\bigg((\frac{C\sqrt{d}}{\gamma^{2}\tilde{\lambda}_{x}}+\frac{1}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}^{2}})u\log(T)\bigg)+O\big(d\sqrt{mT}\log(T)\big)+O\big(mCd\log^{1.5}(T)\big)
, asymptotically matching this lower bound with respect to
T
up to logarithmic factors and with respect to
C
up to
O(\sqrt{m})
factors, showing the tightness of our theoretical results (where

m
are typically very small for real applications).

We conjecture that the gap for the
m
factor in the
mC
term of the lower bound is due to the strong assumption that cluster structures are known to prove our lower bound, and whether there exists a tighter lower bound will be left for future work.

A.4.5
Proof of Theorem
6.4.5

We prove the theorem using the proof by contrapositive. Specifically, in Theorem
6.4.5
, we need to prove that for any
t\geq T_{0}
, if the detection condition in Line
7
of Algo.
9
for user
i
, then with probability at least
1-5\delta
, user
i
is indeed a corrupted user. By the proof by contrapositive, we can prove Theorem
6.4.5
by showing that: for any
t\geq T_{0}
, if user
i
is a normal user, then with probability at least
1-5\delta
, the detection condition in Line
7
of Algo.
9
will not be satisfied for user
i
.

If the clustering structure is correct at
t
, then for any normal user
i

\displaystyle\tilde{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t}-\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{V_{i,t},t}

\displaystyle=\tilde{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t}-\bm{\theta}_{i}+\bm{\theta}_{i}-\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{V_{i,t},t}\,,

(A.209)

where
\tilde{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t}
is the non-robust estimation of the ground-truth
\theta_{i}
, and
\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{V_{i,t},t-1}
is the robust estimation of the inferred cluster
V_{i,t}
for user
i
at round
t
. Since the clustering structure is correct at
t
,
\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{V_{i,t},t-1}
is the robust estimation of user
i
’s ground-truth cluster’s preference vector
\bm{\theta}^{j(i)}=\bm{\theta}_{i}
at round
t
.

We have

\displaystyle\tilde{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t}-\bm{\theta}_{i}

\displaystyle=(\lambda\bm{I}+\tilde{\bm{M}}_{i,t})^{-1}\tilde{\bm{b}}_{i,t}-\bm{\theta}_{i}

\displaystyle=(\lambda\bm{I}+\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}=i}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}^{\top})^{-1}(\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}=i}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}r_{s})-\bm{\theta}_{i}

\displaystyle=(\lambda\bm{I}+\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}=i}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}^{\top})^{-1}\big(\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}=i}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}(\bm{x}_{a_{s}}^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{i}+\eta_{s})\big)-\bm{\theta}_{i}

(A.210)

\displaystyle=(\lambda\bm{I}+\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}=i}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}^{\top})^{-1}\big((\lambda\bm{I}+\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}=i}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}^{\top})\bm{\theta}_{i}-\lambda\bm{\theta}_{i}+\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}=i}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\eta_{s})\big)-\bm{\theta}_{i}

\displaystyle=-\lambda\tilde{\bm{M}}_{i,t}^{\prime-1}\bm{\theta}_{i}+\tilde{\bm{M}}_{i,t}^{\prime-1}\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}=i}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\eta_{s}\,,

where we denote
\tilde{\bm{M}}_{i,t}^{\prime}\triangleq\lambda\bm{I}+\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}=i}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}^{\top}
, and Eq.(
A.210
) is because since user
i
is normal, we have
c_{s}=0,\forall s:i_{s}=i
.

Then, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert\tilde{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t}-\bm{\theta}_{i}\right\rVert_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\lambda\tilde{\bm{M}}_{i,t}^{\prime-1}\bm{\theta}_{i}\right\rVert_{2}+\left\lVert\tilde{\bm{M}}_{i,t}^{\prime-1}\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}=i}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\eta_{s}\right\rVert_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\lambda\left\lVert{\tilde{\bm{M}}_{i,t}^{\prime-\frac{1}{2}}}\right\rVert_{2}^{2}\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{i}\right\rVert_{2}+\left\lVert{\tilde{\bm{M}}_{i,t}^{\prime-\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}=i}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\eta_{s}\right\rVert_{2}\left\lVert{\tilde{\bm{M}}_{i,t}^{\prime-\frac{1}{2}}}\right\rVert_{2}

(A.211)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sqrt{\lambda}+\left\lVert\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}=i}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\eta_{s}\right\rVert_{\tilde{\bm{M}}_{i,t}^{\prime-1}}}{\sqrt{\lambda_{\text{min}}({\tilde{\bm{M}}_{i,t}^{\prime}})}}\,,\,,

(A.212)

where Eq.(
A.211
) follows by the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality and the inequality for the operator norm of matrices, and Eq.(
A.212
) follows by the Courant-Fischer theorem and the fact that
\lambda_{\text{min}}(\tilde{\bm{M}}_{i,t}^{\prime})\geq\lambda
.

Following Theorem 1 in
[
1
]
, for a fixed normal user
i
, with probability at least
1-\delta
for some
\delta\in(0,1)
we have:

\displaystyle\left\lVert\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}=i}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\eta_{s}\right\rVert_{\tilde{\bm{M}}_{i,t}^{\prime-1}}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2\log(\frac{1}{\delta})+\log(\frac{\text{det}(\tilde{\bm{M}}_{i,t}^{\prime})}{\text{det}(\lambda\bm{I})})}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2\log(\frac{1}{\delta})+d\log(1+\frac{T_{i,t}}{\lambda d})}\,,

(A.213)

where Eq.(
A.213
) is because
\text{det}(\tilde{\bm{M}}_{i,t}^{\prime})\leq\Bigg(\frac{\text{trace}(\lambda\bm{I}+\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}=i}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}^{\top})}{d}\Bigg)^{d}\leq\big(\frac{\lambda d+T_{i,t}}{d}\big)^{d}
, and
\text{det}(\lambda\bm{I})=\lambda^{d}
.

Plugging this into Eq.(
A.212
), we can get

\left\lVert\tilde{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t}-\bm{\theta}_{i}\right\rVert_{2}\leq\frac{\sqrt{\lambda}+\sqrt{2\log(\frac{1}{\delta})+d\log(1+\frac{T_{i,t}}{\lambda d})}}{\sqrt{\lambda_{\text{min}}({\tilde{\bm{M}}_{i,t}^{\prime}})}}\,.

(A.214)

Then we need to bound
\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{i}-\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{V_{i,t},t}\right\rVert_{2}
. With the correct clustering,
V_{i,t}=V_{j(i)}
, we have

\displaystyle\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{V_{i,t},t}-\bm{\theta}_{i}

\displaystyle=\bm{M}_{{V}_{i,t},t}^{-1}\bm{b}_{{V}_{j,t},t}

\displaystyle=(\lambda\bm{I}+\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}\in V_{j(i)}}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}^{\top})^{-1}(\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}\in V_{j(i)}}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}r_{s})-\bm{\theta}_{i}

\displaystyle=(\lambda\bm{I}+\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}\in V_{j(i)}}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}^{\top})^{-1}(\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}\in V_{j(i)}}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}(\bm{x}_{a_{s}}^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{i}+\eta_{s}+c_{s})))-\theta_{i}

(A.215)

\displaystyle=(\lambda\bm{I}+\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}\in V_{j(i)}}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}^{\top})^{-1}\big((\lambda\bm{I}+\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}\in V_{j(i)}}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}^{\top})\bm{\theta}_{i}-\lambda\bm{\theta}_{i}

\displaystyle\hskip 11.74988pt+\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}\in V_{j(i)}}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\eta_{s}+\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}\in V_{j(i)}}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}c_{s}))\big)-\theta_{i}

\displaystyle=-\lambda\bm{M}_{{V}_{i,t},t}^{-1}\bm{\theta}_{i}+\bm{M}_{{V}_{i,t},t}^{-1}\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}\in V_{j(i)}}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\eta_{s}+\bm{M}_{{V}_{i,t},t}^{-1}\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}\in V_{j(i)}}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}c_{s}\,.

(A.216)

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{i}-\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{V_{i,t},t}\right\rVert_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\lambda\left\lVert\bm{M}_{{V}_{i,t},t}^{-1}\bm{\theta}_{i}\right\rVert_{2}+\left\lVert\bm{M}_{{V}_{i,t},t}^{-1}\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}\in V_{j(i)}}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\eta_{s}\right\rVert_{2}+\left\lVert\bm{M}_{{V}_{i,t},t}^{-1}\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}\in V_{j(i)}}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}c_{s}\right\rVert_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\lambda\left\lVert{\bm{M}_{{V}_{i,t},t}^{-\frac{1}{2}}}\right\rVert_{2}^{2}\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{i}\right\rVert_{2}+\left\lVert{\bm{M}_{{V}_{i,t},t}^{-\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}\in V_{j(i)}}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\eta_{s}\right\rVert_{2}\left\lVert{\bm{M}_{{V}_{i,t},t}^{-\frac{1}{2}}}\right\rVert_{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 11.74988pt\hskip 11.74988pt+\left\lVert{\bm{M}_{{V}_{i,t},t}^{-\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}\in V_{j(i)}}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\eta_{s}\right\rVert_{2}\left\lVert{\bm{M}_{{V}_{i,t},t}^{-\frac{1}{2}}}\right\rVert_{2}

(A.217)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sqrt{\lambda}+\left\lVert\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}\in V_{j(i)}}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\eta_{s}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{{V}_{i,t},t}^{-1}}+\left\lVert\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}\in V_{j(i)}}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}c_{s}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{{V}_{i,t},t}^{-1}}}{\sqrt{\lambda_{\text{min}}(\bm{M}_{{V}_{i,t},t})}}

(A.218)

Let
\tilde{\bm{x}}_{s}\triangleq\sqrt{w_{i_{s},s}}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}
,
\tilde{\eta}_{s}\triangleq\sqrt{w_{i_{s},s}}\eta_{s}
, then we have:
\left\lVert\tilde{\bm{x}}_{s}\right\rVert_{2}\leq\left\lVert\sqrt{w_{i_{s},s}}\right\rVert_{2}\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\right\rVert_{2}\leq 1
,
\tilde{\eta}_{s}
is still 1-sub-gaussian (since
\eta_{s}
is 1-sub-gaussian and
\sqrt{w_{i_{s},s}}\leq 1
),
\bm{M}_{{V}_{i,t},t}=\lambda\bm{I}+\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}\in V_{j(i)}}\tilde{\bm{x}}_{s}\tilde{\bm{x}}_{s}^{\top}
, and
\left\lVert\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}\in V_{j(i)}}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\eta_{s}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{{V}_{i,t},t}^{-1}}
becomes
\left\lVert\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}\in V_{j(i)}}\tilde{\bm{x}}_{s}\tilde{\eta}_{s}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{{V}_{i,t},t}^{-1}}
. Then, following Theorem 1 in
[
1
]
, with probability at least
1-\delta
for some
\delta\in(0,1)
, for a fixed normal user
i
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}\in V_{j(i)}}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\eta_{s}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{{V}_{i,t},t}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2\log(\frac{1}{\delta})+\log(\frac{\text{det}(\bm{M}_{{V}_{i,t},t})}{\text{det}(\lambda\bm{I})})}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2\log(\frac{1}{\delta})+d\log(1+\frac{T_{V_{i,t},t}}{\lambda d})}\,,

(A.219)

where Eq.(
A.213
) is because
\text{det}(\bm{M}_{{V}_{i,t},t})\leq\Bigg(\frac{\text{trace}(\lambda\bm{I}+\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}\in V_{j(i)}}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}^{\top})}{d}\Bigg)^{d}\leq\big(\frac{\lambda d+T_{V_{i,t},t}}{d}\big)^{d}
, and
\text{det}(\lambda\bm{I})=\lambda^{d}
.

For
\left\lVert\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}\in V_{j(i)}}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}c_{s}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{{V}_{i,t},t}^{-1}}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}\in V_{j(i)}}w_{i_{s},s}\bm{x}_{a_{s}}c_{s}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{{V}_{i,t},t}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{s\in[t]\atop i_{s}\in V_{j(i)}}\left|c_{s}\right|w_{i_{s},s}\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{{V}_{i,t},t}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\leq\alpha C\,,

(A.220)

where Eq.(
A.220
) is because
w_{i_{s},s}\leq\frac{\alpha}{\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{i_{s},s}^{\prime-1}}}\leq\frac{\alpha}{\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{i_{s},t}^{\prime-1}}}\leq\frac{\alpha}{\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{{V}_{i,t},t}^{-1}}}
(since
\bm{M}_{{V}_{i,t},t}\succeq\bm{M}_{i_{s},t}^{\prime}\succeq\bm{M}_{i_{s},s}^{\prime}
,
\bm{M}_{i_{s},s}^{\prime-1}\succeq\bm{M}_{i_{s},t}^{\prime-1}\succeq\bm{M}_{{V}_{i,t},t}^{-1}
,
\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{i_{s},s}^{\prime-1}}\geq\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{i_{s},t}^{\prime-1}}\geq\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a_{s}}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{{V}_{i,t},t}^{-1}}
), and
\sum_{s\in[t]}\left|c_{s}\right|\leq C
.

Therefore, we have

\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{i}-\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{V_{i,t},t}\right\rVert_{2}\leq\frac{\sqrt{\lambda}+\sqrt{2\log(\frac{1}{\delta})+d\log(1+\frac{T_{V_{i,t},t}}{\lambda d})}+\alpha C}{\sqrt{\lambda_{\text{min}}(\bm{M}_{{V}_{i,t},t})}}\,.

(A.221)

With Eq.(
A.221
), Eq.(
A.214
) and Eq.(
A.209
), together with Lemma
A.3.2
, we have that for a normal user
i
, for any
t\geq T_{0}
, with probability at least
1-5\delta
for some
\delta\in(0,\frac{1}{5})

\displaystyle\left\lVert\tilde{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t}-\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{V_{i,t},t}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\tilde{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t}-\bm{\theta}_{i}\right\rVert_{2}+\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{i}-\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{V_{i,t},t}\right\rVert_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sqrt{\lambda}+\sqrt{2\log(\frac{1}{\delta})+d\log(1+\frac{T_{i,t}}{\lambda d})}}{\sqrt{\lambda_{\text{min}}({\tilde{\bm{M}}_{i,t}^{\prime}})}}+\frac{\sqrt{\lambda}+\sqrt{2\log(\frac{1}{\delta})+d\log(1+\frac{T_{V_{i,t},t}}{\lambda d})}+\alpha C}{\sqrt{\lambda_{\text{min}}(\bm{M}_{{V}_{i,t},t})}}\,,

(A.222)

which is exactly the detection condition in Line
7
of Algo.
9
.

Therefore, by the proof by contrapositive, we complete the proof of Theorem
6.4.5
.

A.4.6
Description of Baselines

We compare RCLUB-WCU to the following five baselines for recommendations.

•

LinUCB
[
135
]
: A state-of-the-art bandit approach for a single user without corruption.

•

LinUCB-Ind: Use a separate LinUCB for each user.

•

CW-OFUL
[
93
]
: A state-of-the-art bandit approach for single user with corruption.

•

CW-OFUL-Ind: Use a separate CW-OFUL for each user.

•

CLUB
[
81
]
: A graph-based clustering of bandits approach for multiple users without corruption.

•

SCLUB
[
141
]
: A set-based clustering of bandits approach for multiple users without corruption.

A.4.7
More Experiments

A.4.7.1
Different Corruption Levels

To see our algorithm’s performance under different corruption levels, we conduct the experiments under different corruption levels for RCLUB-WCU, CLUB, and SCLUB on Amazon and Yelp datasets. Recall the corruption mechanism in Section
6.5.1
, we set
k
as 1,000; 10,000; 100,000. The results are shown in Fig.
A.2
. All the algorithms’ performance becomes worse when the corruption level increases. But RCLUB-WCU is much robust than the baselines.

(a)

Amazon Corruption Level

(b)

Yelp Corruption Level

Figure A.2
:
Cumulative regret in different corruption levels

A.4.7.2
Different Cluster numbers

Following
[
139
]
, we test the performances of the cluster-based algorithms (RCLUB-WCU, CLUB, SCLUB) when the underlying cluster number changes. We set
m
as 5, 10, 20, and 50. The results are shown in Fig.
A.3
. All these algorithms’ performances decrease when the cluster numbers increase, matching our theoretical results. The performances of CLUB and SCLUB decrease much faster than RCLUB-WCU, indicating that RCLUB-WCU is more robust when the underlying user cluster number changes.

(a)

Amazon Cluster Number

(b)

Yelp Cluster Number

:
Cumulative regret with different cluster numbers

A.5
Appendix of Chapter
7

A.5.1
Proof of Lemma
7.3.1

Proof.

According to the closed-form solution of
\bm{\theta}_{t}
in Eq. (
7.5
) (
7.6
), we can calculate the estimation error as follows

\displaystyle\bm{\theta}_{t}-\bm{\theta}_{*}

\displaystyle=\bm{M}_{t}^{-1}\bm{b}_{t}-\bm{\theta}_{*}

\displaystyle=\Bigg(\sum_{\tau=1}^{t-1}\bm{x}_{a_{\tau}}\bm{x}^{\top}_{a_{\tau}}+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{K}_{\tau}}\bm{\tilde{x}}_{k}\bm{\tilde{x}}^{\top}_{k}+\beta\bm{I}\Bigg)^{-1}\Bigg(\sum_{\tau=1}^{t-1}\bm{x}_{a_{\tau}}r_{a_{\tau},\tau}+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{K}_{\tau}}\bm{\tilde{x}}_{k}\tilde{r}_{k,\tau}\Bigg)-\bm{\theta}_{*}

\displaystyle=\Bigg(\sum_{\tau=1}^{t-1}\bm{x}_{a_{\tau}}\bm{x}^{\top}_{a_{\tau}}+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{K}_{\tau}}\bm{\tilde{x}}_{k}\bm{\tilde{x}}^{\top}_{k}+\beta\bm{I}\Bigg)^{-1}\Bigg(\sum_{\tau=1}^{t-1}\bm{x}_{a_{\tau}}\bigg(\bm{x}^{\top}_{a_{\tau}}\bm{\theta}_{*}+\epsilon_{\tau}\bigg)+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{K}_{\tau}}\bm{\tilde{x}}_{k}\bigg(\bm{\tilde{x}}_{k}^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{*}+\tilde{\epsilon}_{\tau}\bigg)\Bigg)

\displaystyle-\bm{\theta}_{*}

\displaystyle=\Bigg(\sum_{\tau=1}^{t-1}\bm{x}_{a_{\tau}}\bm{x}^{\top}_{a_{\tau}}+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{K}_{\tau}}\bm{\tilde{x}}_{k}\bm{\tilde{x}}^{\top}_{k}+\beta\bm{I}\Bigg)^{-1}\Bigg(\sum_{\tau=1}^{t-1}\bm{x}_{a_{\tau}}\bm{x}^{\top}_{a_{\tau}}+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{K}_{\tau}}\bm{\tilde{x}}_{k}\bm{\tilde{x}}^{\top}_{k}+\beta\bm{I}-\beta\bm{I}\Bigg)\bm{\theta}_{*}-\bm{\theta}_{*}

\displaystyle+\bm{M}_{t}^{-1}(\sum_{\tau=1}^{t-1}\bm{x}_{a_{\tau}}\epsilon_{\tau}+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{K}_{\tau}}\bm{\tilde{x}}_{k}\tilde{\epsilon}_{\tau})

\displaystyle=-\beta\bm{M}_{t}^{-1}\bm{\theta}_{*}+\bm{M}_{t}^{-1}(\sum_{\tau=1}^{t-1}\bm{x}_{a_{\tau}}\epsilon_{\tau}+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{K}_{\tau}}\bm{\tilde{x}}_{k}\tilde{\epsilon}_{\tau})\,.

We can then bound the projection of the estimation error onto the direction of the action vector
\bm{x}_{a}
:

\displaystyle\left|\bm{x}_{a}^{\top}(\bm{\theta}_{t}-\bm{\theta}_{*})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\beta\left|\bm{x}_{a}^{\top}\bm{M}_{t}^{-1}\bm{\theta}_{*}\right|+\left|\bm{x}_{a}^{\top}\bm{M}_{t}^{-1}(\sum_{\tau=1}^{t-1}\bm{x}_{a_{\tau}}\epsilon_{\tau}+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{K}_{\tau}}\bm{\tilde{x}}_{k}\tilde{\epsilon}_{\tau})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\beta\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a}^{\top}\bm{M}_{t}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\rVert_{2}\left\lVert\bm{M}_{t}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\bm{\theta}_{*}\right\rVert_{2}+\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a}^{\top}\bm{M}_{t}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\rVert_{2}\times\left\lVert\bm{M}_{t}^{-\frac{1}{2}}(\sum_{\tau=1}^{t-1}\bm{x}_{a_{\tau}}\epsilon_{\tau}+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{K}_{\tau}}\bm{\tilde{x}}_{k}\tilde{\epsilon}_{\tau})\right\rVert_{2}

(A.223)

\displaystyle\leq\beta\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{t}^{-1}}\left\lVert\bm{M}_{t}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\rVert_{2}\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{*}\right\rVert_{2}+\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{t}^{-1}}\left\lVert\sum_{\tau=1}^{t-1}\bm{x}_{a_{\tau},\tau}\epsilon_{\tau}+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{K}_{\tau}}\bm{\tilde{x}}_{k}\tilde{\epsilon}_{\tau}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{t}^{-1}}

(A.224)

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{t}^{-1}}\Bigg(\sqrt{\beta}\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{*}\right\rVert_{2}+\left\lVert\sum_{\tau=1}^{t-1}\bm{x}_{a_{\tau}}\epsilon_{\tau}+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{K}_{\tau}}\bm{\tilde{x}}_{k}\tilde{\epsilon}_{\tau}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{t}^{-1}}\Bigg)\,,

(A.225)

where Eq. (
A.223
) is by the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality, Eq. (
A.224
) is by the inequality of the matrix operator norm, and Eq. (
A.225
) is because
\lambda_{min}(\bm{M}_{t})\geq\beta,\,\,\left\lVert\bm{M}_{t}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\rVert_{2}=\sqrt{\lambda_{max}(\bm{M}_{t}^{-1})}=\sqrt{\frac{1}{\lambda_{min}(\bm{M}_{t})}}\leq\sqrt{\frac{1}{\beta}}
.

Theorem 1 in
[
1
]
suggests that with probability at least
1-\delta

\left\lVert\sum_{\tau=1}^{t-1}\bm{x}_{a_{\tau}}\epsilon_{\tau}+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{K}_{\tau}}\bm{\tilde{x}}_{k}\tilde{\epsilon}_{k}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{t}^{-1}}\leq\sqrt{2\log\bigg(\frac{det(\bm{M}_{t})^{\frac{1}{2}}det(\beta\bm{I})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\delta}\bigg)}\,,

(A.226)

where
det(\cdot)
denotes the determinate of the argument.

We have

\displaystyle det(\bm{M}_{t})=\prod_{i=1}^{d}\lambda_{i}

\displaystyle\leq\big(\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{d}\lambda_{i}}{d}\big)^{d}

(A.227)

\displaystyle=\big(\frac{trace(\bm{M}_{t})}{d}\big)^{d}

(A.228)

\displaystyle=\Bigg(\frac{trace(\sum_{\tau=1}^{t-1}\bm{x}_{a_{\tau}}\bm{x}^{\top}_{a_{\tau}}+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{K}_{\tau}}\bm{\tilde{x}}_{k}\bm{\tilde{x}}^{\top}_{k}+\beta\bm{I})}{d}\Bigg)^{d}

\displaystyle\leq\big(\frac{t+b(t)+\beta d}{d}\big)^{d}\,,

where
\lambda_{i},i=1,2,\ldots,d
denotes the eigenvalues of the matrix
\bm{M}_{t}
,
trace(\bm{M}_{t})
denotes the trace of
\bm{M}_{t}
, Eq. (
A.227
) follows by the inequality of arithmetic and geometric means, Eq. (
A.228
) follows since the trace of a matrix is equal to the sum of its eigenvalues.

Plugging the above inequality and
det(\beta\bm{I})=\beta^{d}
into Eq. (
A.226
), we can get

\left\lVert\sum_{\tau=1}^{t-1}\bm{x}_{a_{\tau}}\epsilon_{\tau}+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{K}_{\tau}}\bm{\tilde{x}}_{k}\tilde{\epsilon}_{k}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{t}^{-1}}\leq\sqrt{2\log(\frac{1}{\delta})+d\log(1+\frac{b(t)+t}{\beta d})}\,.

(A.229)

The result then follows by plugging Eq. (
A.229
) into Eq. (
A.225
), and the fact that
\left\lVert\bm{\theta}^{*}\right\rVert_{2}\leq 1
.
∎

A.5.2
Proof of Lemma
7.3.2

Proof.

Recall that in ConLinUCB-BS, the key-terms are uniformly sampled from the pre-computed barycentric spanner
\mathcal{B}
, i.e.,
{k}\sim\text{unif}(\mathcal{B})
. Therefore we have

\lambda_{\mathcal{B}}\coloneqq\lambda_{\min}(\bm{E}_{k\sim\text{unif}(\mathcal{B})}[\tilde{\bm{x}}_{k}\tilde{\bm{x}}_{k}^{\top}])>0\,.

(A.230)

Using Eq. (
7.11
) in the Lemma 7 in
[
139
]
, and the fact that
b_{t}=b\cdot t
, then with probability at least
1-\delta
for
\delta\in(0,\frac{1}{8}]
, we have

\lambda_{\min}(\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{K}_{\tau}}\bm{\tilde{x}}_{k}\bm{\tilde{x}}_{k}^{\top})\geq\frac{\lambda_{\mathcal{B}}bt}{2}\,,

(A.231)

for all
t\geq t_{0}=\frac{256}{b\lambda_{\mathcal{B}}^{2}}\log(\frac{128d}{\lambda_{\mathcal{B}}^{2}\delta})
.

Then, by Courant–Fischer theorem
[
102
]
, the fact that
\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a}\right\rVert_{2}=1
, together with Eq. (
A.231
), we have that for any
t\geq t_{0}
,with probability at least
1-\delta
for
\delta\in(0,\frac{1}{8}]
,

\displaystyle\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{t}^{-1}}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\bm{x}_{a}^{\top}\bm{M}_{t}^{-1}\bm{x}_{a}}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{\bm{x}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}\atop\left\lVert\bm{x}\right\rVert_{2}=1}\sqrt{\bm{x}^{\top}\bm{M}_{t}^{-1}\bm{x}}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\lambda_{\max}(\bm{M}_{t}^{-1})}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\lambda_{\max}\bigg((\sum_{\tau=1}^{t-1}\bm{x}_{a_{\tau}}\bm{x}^{\top}_{a_{\tau}}+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{K}_{\tau}}\bm{\tilde{x}}_{k}\bm{\tilde{x}}^{\top}_{k}+\beta\bm{I})^{-1}\bigg)}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{1}{\lambda_{\min}\bigg(\sum_{\tau=1}^{t-1}\bm{x}_{a_{\tau}}\bm{x}^{\top}_{a_{\tau}}+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{K}_{\tau}}\bm{\tilde{x}}_{k}\bm{\tilde{x}}^{\top}_{k}+\beta\bm{I}\bigg)}}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{1}{\lambda_{\min}(\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{K}_{\tau}}\bm{\tilde{x}}_{k}\bm{\tilde{x}}_{k}^{\top})}}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{2}{\lambda_{\mathcal{B}}bt}}\,.

∎

A.5.3
Proof of Theorem
7.3.3

Proof.

We denote the instantaneous regret at round
t
as
R_{t}
. With the definition of the cumulative regret given in Eq. (
7.2
), the arm selection strategy shown in Eq. (
7.7
) and Lemma
7.3.1
, we can bound the regret
R_{t}
at each round
t=1,2,3,4,...,T
as follows

\displaystyle R_{t}

\displaystyle=\bm{x}_{a_{t}^{*}}^{\top}\bm{\theta}^{*}-\bm{x}_{a_{t}}^{\top}\bm{\theta}^{*}

(A.232)

\displaystyle=\bm{x}_{a_{t}^{*}}^{\top}(\bm{\theta}^{*}-\bm{\theta}_{t})+(\bm{\theta}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}_{a_{t}^{*}}+C_{a_{t}^{*},t})-(\bm{\theta}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}_{a_{t}}+C_{a_{t},t})

\displaystyle+\bm{x}_{a_{t}}^{\top}(\bm{\theta}_{t}-\bm{\theta}^{*})+C_{a_{t},t}-C_{a_{t}^{*},t}

\displaystyle\leq 2C_{a_{t},t}\,.

With Lemma
7.3.2
, together with the assumption that
r_{t}\leq 1
for any
t
, with probability at least
1-\delta
for some
\delta\in(0,\frac{1}{4}]
, we can get

\displaystyle R(T)

\displaystyle=R(\lceil t_{0}\rceil)+\sum_{t=\lceil t_{0}\rceil+1}^{T}R_{t}

\displaystyle\leq t_{0}+1+2\sum_{t=\lceil t_{0}\rceil}^{T}C_{a_{t},t}

\displaystyle\leq t_{0}+1+2\alpha_{t}\sum_{t=\lceil t_{0}\rceil}^{T}\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a_{t}}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{t}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\leq t_{0}+1+2\alpha_{T}\sum_{t=\lceil t_{0}\rceil}^{T}\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a_{t}}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{t}^{-1}}

(A.233)

\displaystyle\leq t_{0}+1+2\alpha_{T}\sum_{t=\lceil t_{0}\rceil}^{T}\sqrt{\frac{2}{\lambda_{\mathcal{B}}bt}}

\displaystyle\leq t_{0}+1+2\alpha_{T}\sqrt{\frac{2}{\lambda_{\mathcal{B}}b}}\int_{t_{0}}^{T}\sqrt{\frac{1}{t}}dt

\displaystyle=\leq t_{0}+1+4\alpha_{T}\sqrt{\frac{2}{\lambda_{\mathcal{B}}b}}(\sqrt{T}-\sqrt{t_{0}})

\displaystyle\leq t_{0}+1+4\alpha_{T}\sqrt{\frac{2}{\lambda_{\mathcal{B}}b}}\sqrt{T}\,,

where Eq. (
A.233
) follows since
\alpha_{t}
is non-decreasing in
t
.

The result follows by plugging in the definition of
t_{0}
and
\alpha_{T}
.
∎

A.5.4
Proof of Theorem
7.3.4

Proof.

We first prove the following result:

For any two positive definite matrices
\bm{A},\bm{B}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
, and any vector
\bm{x}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
, we have:

\left\lVert\bm{x}\right\rVert_{(\bm{A}+\bm{B})^{-1}}^{2}\leq\left\lVert\bm{x}\right\rVert_{\bm{A}^{-1}}^{2}\,.

(A.234)

This result can be proved by the following arguments:

\displaystyle\left\lVert\bm{x}\right\rVert_{(\bm{A}+\bm{B})^{-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\bm{x}^{\top}(\bm{A}+\bm{B})^{-1}\bm{x}

\displaystyle=\bm{x}^{\top}\big(\bm{A}^{-1}-\bm{A}^{-1}(\bm{B}^{-1}+\bm{A}^{-1})^{-1}\bm{A}^{-1}\big)\bm{x}

(A.235)

\displaystyle=\bm{x}^{\top}\bm{A}^{-1}\bm{x}-(\bm{A}^{-1}\bm{x})^{\top}(\bm{B}^{-1}+\bm{A}^{-1})^{-1}(\bm{A}^{-1}\bm{x})

\displaystyle\leq\bm{x}^{\top}\bm{A}^{-1}\bm{x}=\left\lVert\bm{x}\right\rVert_{\bm{A}^{-1}}^{2}\,,

(A.236)

where Eq. (
A.235
) follows from the Woodbury matrix identity
[
244
]
, and Eq. (
A.236
) is because
(\bm{B}^{-1}+\bm{A}^{-1})^{-1}
is a positive definite matrix.

With the above result, then following Eq. (
A.232
) and the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality, we can get

\displaystyle R(T)

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{t=1}^{T}C_{a_{t},t}

(A.237)

\displaystyle=2\alpha_{t}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a_{t}}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{t}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\leq 2\alpha_{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a_{t}}\right\rVert_{\bm{M}_{t}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\leq 2\alpha_{T}\sqrt{T\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a_{t}}\right\rVert^{2}_{\bm{M}^{-1}_{t}}}

\displaystyle\leq 2\alpha_{T}\sqrt{T\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a_{t}}\right\rVert^{2}_{\bm{V}^{-1}_{t}}}\,.

Using Lemma 11 in
[
1
]
, with probability at least
1-\delta
, we can get

\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a,t}\right\rVert_{\bm{V}^{-1}_{t}}^{2}\leq 2\log\bigg(\frac{det(\bm{V}_{T})}{det(\beta\bm{I})}\bigg)\,.

(A.238)

Following similar steps as in Eq. (
A.228
), we can get that

\frac{det(\bm{V}_{T})}{det(\beta\bm{I})}\leq\big(\frac{T+\beta d}{\beta d}\big)^{d}\,.

(A.239)

Therefore we have

\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\lVert\bm{x}_{a,t}\right\rVert_{\bm{V}^{-1}_{t}}^{2}\leq 2d\log(1+\frac{T+1}{\beta d})\,.

(A.240)

The result then follows by plugging in the definition of
\alpha_{T}
and Eq. (
A.240
) into Eq. (
A.237
).
∎

A.6
Appendix for Chapter
8

A.6.1
\text{Restarted SAVE}^{+}
-BOB

In this section, we provide the details of our proposed
\text{Restarted SAVE}^{+}
-BOB algorithm. The
\text{Restarted SAVE}^{+}
-BOB algorithm is summarized in Algo.
18
.
We divide the
K
rounds into
\lceil\frac{K}{H}\rceil
blocks, with each block having
H
rounds (except the last one may have less than
H
).
Within each block
i
, we use a fixed
(\alpha_{i},w_{i})
pair to run the Restarted
\text{SAVE}^{+}
algorithm. To adaptively learn the optimal
(\alpha,w)
pair without the knowledge of
V_{K}
and
B_{K}
, we employ an
adversarial bandit algorithm (Exp3 in
[
12
]
) as the meta-learner to select
\alpha_{i},w_{i}
over time for
i\in\lceil\frac{K}{H}\rceil
blocks. Specifically, in each block, the meta learner selects a
(\alpha,w)
pair from the candidate pool to feed to Restarted
\text{SAVE}^{+}
, and the cumulative reward received by Restarted
\text{SAVE}^{+}
within the block is fed to the meta-learner as the reward feedback to select a better pair for the next block.

We set
H
to be
\lceil d^{\frac{2}{5}}K^{\frac{2}{5}}\rceil
, and set the candidate pool of
(\alpha,w)
pairs for the Exp3 algorithm as:

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}=\{(w,\alpha):w\in\mathcal{W},\alpha\in\mathcal{J}\}\,,

(A.241)

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{W}

\displaystyle=\{w_{i}=d^{\frac{1}{3}}2^{i-1}|i\in\lceil\frac{1}{3}\log_{2}K\rceil+1\}\cup\{w_{i}=d^{\frac{2}{5}}2^{i-1}|i\in\lceil\frac{2}{5}\log_{2}K\rceil+1\}\,,

(A.242)

and

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}

\displaystyle=\{\alpha_{i}=d^{\frac{1}{3}}2^{-i+1}|i\in\lceil\frac{1}{3}\log_{2}K\rceil+1\}\cup\{\alpha_{i}=d^{\frac{11}{30}}2^{-i+1}|i\in\lceil\frac{11}{30}\log_{2}K\rceil+1\}\,.

(A.243)

The algorithm also labels all the
\left|\mathcal{P}\right|=\big(\lceil\frac{1}{3}\log_{2}K\rceil+\lceil\frac{2}{5}\log_{2}K\rceil+2\big)\cdot\big(\lceil\frac{1}{3}\log_{2}K\rceil+\lceil\frac{11}{30}\log_{2}K\rceil+2\big)
candidate pairs of parameters in
\mathcal{P},
i.e.
,
\mathcal{P}=\{(w_{i},\alpha_{i})\}_{i=1}^{\left|\mathcal{P}\right|}
. The algorithm initializes
\{s_{j,1}\}^{\left|\mathcal{P}\right|}_{j=1}
to be
s_{j,1}=1,\hskip 11.74988pt\forall j=0,1,\ldots,\left|\mathcal{P}\right|
, which means that at the beginning, the algorithm selects a pair from
\mathcal{P}
uniformly at random. At the beginning of each block
i\in[\lceil K/H\rceil]
, the meta-learner (Exp3) calculates the distribution
(p_{j,i})^{\left|\mathcal{P}\right|}_{j=1}
over the candidate set
\mathcal{P}
by

\displaystyle p_{j,i}=(1-\gamma)\frac{s_{j,i}}{\sum_{u=1}^{\left|\mathcal{P}\right|}s_{u,i}}+\frac{\gamma}{\left|\mathcal{P}\right|+1},\hskip 11.74988pt\forall j=1,\ldots,\left|\mathcal{P}\right|\,,

(A.244)

where
\gamma
is defined as

\displaystyle\gamma=\min\left\{1,\sqrt{\frac{(\left|\mathcal{P}\right|+1)\ln(\left|\mathcal{P}\right|+1)}{(e-1)\lceil K/H\rceil}}\right\}\,.

(A.245)

Then, the meta-learner draws a
j_{i}
from the distribution
(p_{j,i})^{\left|\mathcal{P}\right|}_{j=1}
, and sets the pair of parameters in block
i
to be
(w_{j_{i}},\alpha_{j_{i}})
, and runs the base algorithm Algo.
12
from scratch in this block with
(w_{j_{i}},\alpha_{j_{i}})
, then feeds the cumulative reward in the block
\sum_{k=(i-1)H+1}^{\min\{i\cdot H,K\}}r_{k}
to the meta-learner. The meta-learner rescales
\sum_{k=(i-1)H+1}^{\min\{i\cdot H,K\}}r_{k}
to
\frac{\sum_{k=(i-1)H+1}^{\min\{i\cdot H,K\}}r_{k}}{H+R\sqrt{\frac{H}{2}\log\big(K(\frac{K}{H}+1)\big)}+\frac{2}{3}\cdot R\log\big(K(\frac{K}{H}+1)\big)}
to make it in the range
[0,1]
with high probability (supported by Lemma
A.6.9
). The meta-learner updates the parameter
s_{j_{i},i+1}
to be
