\displaystyle s_{j_{i},i+1}=s_{j_{i},i}\cdot\exp\left(\frac{\gamma}{(\left|\mathcal{P}\right|+1)p_{j_{i},i}}\left(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\sum_{k=(i-1)H+1}^{\min\{i\cdot H,K\}}r_{k}}{H+R\sqrt{\frac{H}{2}\log\big(K(\frac{K}{H}+1)\big)}+\frac{2}{3}\cdot R\log\big(K(\frac{K}{H}+1)\big)}\right)\right)\,,

(A.246)

and keep others unchanged,
i.e.
,
s_{u,i+1}=s_{u,i},~\forall u\neq j_{i}
. After that, the algorithm will go to the next block, and repeat the same process in block
i+1
.

Algorithm 18

\text{Restarted SAVE}^{+}
-BOB

0:

total time rounds
K
; problem dimension
d
; noise upper bound
R
;
\alpha>0
; the upper bound on the
\ell_{2}
-norm of
\mathbf{a}
in
\mathcal{D}_{k}(k\geq 1)
, i.e.,
A
; the upper bound on the
\ell_{2}
-norm of
\bm{\theta}_{k}

(k\geq 1)
, i.e.,
B
.

1:

Initialize
H=\lceil d^{\frac{2}{5}}K^{\frac{2}{5}}\rceil
;
\mathcal{P}
as defined in Eq.(
A.241
), and index the
\left|\mathcal{P}\right|=\big(\lceil\frac{1}{3}\log_{2}K\rceil+\lceil\frac{2}{5}\log_{2}K\rceil+2\big)\cdot\big(\lceil\frac{1}{3}\log_{2}K\rceil+\lceil\frac{11}{30}\log_{2}K\rceil+2\big)
items in
\mathcal{P}
,
i.e.
,
\mathcal{P}=\{(w_{i},\alpha_{i})\}_{i=1}^{\left|\mathcal{P}\right|}
;
\gamma=\min\left\{1,\sqrt{\frac{(\left|\mathcal{P}\right|+1)\ln(\left|\mathcal{P}\right|+1)}{(e-1)\lceil K/H\rceil}}\right\}
;
\{s_{j,1}\}^{\left|\mathcal{P}\right|}_{j=1}
is set to
s_{j,1}=1,\hskip 11.74988pt\forall j=0,1,\ldots,\left|\mathcal{P}\right|
.

2:

for

i=1,2,\ldots,\lceil K/H\rceil

do

3:

Calculate the distribution
(p_{j,i})^{\left|\mathcal{P}\right|}_{j=1}
by
p_{j,i}=(1-\gamma)\frac{s_{j,i}}{\sum_{u=1}^{\left|\mathcal{P}\right|}s_{u,i}}+\frac{\gamma}{\left|\mathcal{P}\right|+1},\hskip 11.74988pt\forall j=1,\ldots,\left|\mathcal{P}\right|
.

4:

Set
j_{i}\leftarrow j
with probability
p_{j,i}
, and
(w_{i},\alpha_{i})\leftarrow(w_{i_{i}},\alpha_{j_{i}})
.

5:

Run Algo.
12
from scratch in block
i
(
i.e.
, in rounds
k=(i-1)H+1,\ldots,\min\{i\cdot H,K\}
) with
(w,\alpha)=(w_{i},\alpha_{i})
.

6:

Update
s_{j_{i},i+1}=s_{j_{i},i}\cdot\exp\left(\frac{\gamma}{(\left|\mathcal{P}\right|+1)p_{j_{i},i}}\left(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\sum_{k=(i-1)H+1}^{\min\{i\cdot H,K\}}r_{k}}{H+R\sqrt{\frac{H}{2}\log\big(K(\frac{K}{H}+1)\big)}+\frac{2}{3}\cdot R\log\big(K(\frac{K}{H}+1)\big)}\right)\right)
, and keep all the others unchanged,
i.e.
,
s_{u,i+1}=s_{u,i},~\forall u\neq j_{i}
.

7:

end

for

We have the following theorem to bound the regret of Restarted
\text{SAVE}^{+}
-BOB.

Theorem A.6.1
.

By using the BOB framework with Exp3 as the meta-algorithm and Restarted
\text{SAVE}^{+}
as the base algorithm, with the candidate pool
\mathcal{P}
for Exp3 specified as in Eq.(
A.241
), Eq.(
A.242
), Eq.(
A.243
), and
H=\lceil d^{\frac{2}{5}}K^{\frac{2}{5}}\rceil
, then the regret of Restarted
\text{SAVE}^{+}
-BOB (Algo.
18
) satisfies

\displaystyle\text{Regret}(K)

\displaystyle=\tilde{O}(d^{4/5}V_{K}^{2/5}B_{K}^{1/5}K^{2/5}+d^{2/3}B_{K}^{1/3}K^{2/3}+d^{2/5}K^{7/10}).

(A.247)

Proof.

See Appendix
A.6.7
for the full proof.
∎

Remark 21
.

We discuss the regret of Algo.
18
in Corollary
8.2
in the following special cases. In the case where the
total variance
is small,
i.e.
,
V_{K}=\tilde{O}(1)
, assuming
K^{2}>d
, our result becomes
\tilde{O}(d^{2/3}B_{K}^{1/3}K^{2/3}+d^{1/5}K^{7/10})
, when
d^{14}B_{K}^{10}>K
, it becomes
\tilde{O}(d^{2/3}B_{K}^{1/3}K^{2/3})
, better than all the previous results
[
39
,
288
,
226
,
241
]
.
In the worst case where
V_{K}=O(K)
, our result becomes
\tilde{O}(d^{4/5}B_{K}^{1/5}K^{4/5})
.

A.6.2
Additional Experiment Setup

For Restarted-
\text{WeightedOFUL}^{+}
, we set
\lambda=1
,
\hat{\beta}_{k}=10
,
w=1000
, and we grid search the variance parameters
\alpha
and
\gamma
, both among values [1, 1.5, 2, 2.5, 3]. Finally we set
\alpha=1
, and
\gamma=2
. For
\text{Restarted SAVE}^{+}
we set
w=1000
,
\hat{\beta}_{k,\ell}=2^{-\ell+1}
, and grid search
L
from 1 to 10 with stepsize of 1 and finally choose
L=6
. For SW-UCB, we set
\lambda=1
,
w=1000
,
\beta_{k}=10
. The Modified EXP3.S requires two parameters
\bar{\alpha}
and
\bar{\gamma}
, and we set
\bar{\gamma}=0.01
and
\bar{\alpha}=\frac{1}{K}
.

To test the algorithms’ performance under different total time horizons, we let
K
vary from
3\times 10^{4}
to
2.4\times 10^{5}
, with a stepsize of
3\times 10^{4}
, and plot the cumulative regret
\text{Regret}(K)
for these different total time step
K
. We set
B_{K}=1,10,20,\text{and }K^{1/3}
to observe their performance in different levels of
B_{K}
.

A.6.3
Proof of Theorem
8.3.1

We prove the lower bound in Theorem
8.3.1
here. We need the following lemma from
[
292
]
.

Lemma A.6.2

(Modification from Lemma 25,
[
292
]
)
.

Fix a positive real
0<\delta\leq 1/3
, and positive integers
T,d
and assume that
T\geq d^{2}/(2\delta)
. Let
\Delta=\sqrt{d\delta/T}/(4\sqrt{2})
and consider the linear bandit problems
\mathcal{L}_{\bm{\mu}}
parameterized with a parameter vector
\bm{\mu}\in\{-\Delta,\Delta\}^{d}
and action set
\mathcal{A}=\{-1/\sqrt{d},1/\sqrt{d}\}^{d}
so that the reward distribution for taking action
\mathbf{a}\in\mathcal{A}
is a Bernoulli distribution
B(\delta+\langle\bm{\mu}^{*},\mathbf{a}\rangle)
. Then for any bandit algorithm
\mathcal{B}
such that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\mu}\sim\text{Unif}\{-\Delta,\Delta\}^{d}}[\text{Regret}(T,\mathcal{L}_{\bm{\mu}})]\geq\frac{d\sqrt{T\delta}}{8\sqrt{2}}.

(A.248)

Here
\text{Regret}(T,\mathcal{L}_{\bm{\mu}})
represents the regret under algorithm
\mathcal{B}
on the instance
\mathcal{L}_{\bm{\mu}}
.

Next we prove Theorem
8.3.1
.

Proof of Theorem
8.3.1
.

Let
T<K
be some constant to be defined. Let
\delta
be a constant satisfying
2\delta\leq d^{2}/T
. We create
w=K/T
number of linear bandit instances with the linear parameter
\bm{\mu}_{1},\dots,\bm{\mu}_{w}
, where
\bm{\mu}_{i}\sim\{-\Delta,\Delta\}^{d},\Delta=\sqrt{d\delta/T}/4\sqrt{2}
. Our nonstationary instance
\mathcal{L}_{\bm{\mu}_{1},\dots,\bm{\mu}_{w}}
consists of
\mathcal{L}_{\bm{\mu}_{1}},\dots,\mathcal{L}_{\bm{\mu}_{w}}
, where at the step
i\cdot T+1,\dots,i\cdot T+T
,
\mathcal{L}_{\bm{\mu}_{1},\dots,\bm{\mu}_{w}}
follows
\mathcal{L}_{\bm{\mu}_{i}}
. Then by the independence of
\mu_{i}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\mu}_{1},\dots,\bm{\mu}_{w}\sim\text{Unif}\{-\Delta,\Delta\}^{d}}\text{Regret}(T,\mathcal{L}_{\bm{\mu}_{1},\dots,\bm{\mu}_{w}})=\sum_{i=1}^{w}\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\mu}_{i}\sim\text{Unif}\{-\Delta,\Delta\}^{d}}[\text{Regret}(T,\mathcal{L}_{\bm{\mu}_{i}})]

\displaystyle\geq\frac{d\sqrt{T\delta}}{8\sqrt{2}}\cdot\frac{K}{T}.

(A.249)

Next we calculate the total variation and total variance for instance
\mathcal{L}_{\bm{\mu}_{1},\dots,\bm{\mu}_{w}}
. For each step, the reward distribution is a Bernoulli distribution
B(\delta+\langle\bm{\mu}_{i},\mathbf{a}\rangle)
, whose variance is

\displaystyle(\delta+\langle\bm{\mu}_{i},\mathbf{a}\rangle)(1-\delta-\langle\bm{\mu}_{i},\mathbf{a}\rangle)\leq(\delta+\langle\bm{\mu}_{i},\mathbf{a}\rangle)\leq 2\delta,

(A.250)

where we use the fact
\sqrt{d}\Delta\leq\delta
. Therefore, the total variance over
K
steps is bounded by

\displaystyle V\leq 2K\delta.

(A.251)

Next, for the total variation, we have for any
k,k+1
belong to the same
\bm{\mu}_{i}
, the variation of
\bm{\mu}
is 0. Note that for any two different
\bm{\mu}_{i},\bm{\mu}_{j}
, their difference is at most
\|\bm{\mu}_{i}-\bm{\mu}_{j}\|\leq 2\sqrt{d\cdot\Delta^{2}}
, then the total variation is bounded by

\displaystyle B\leq\frac{K}{T}\cdot 2\Delta\sqrt{d}=\sqrt{d\delta/T}/(4\sqrt{2})\frac{K}{T}\cdot 2\sqrt{d}=\frac{dK\sqrt{\delta}}{2\sqrt{2T^{3}}}.

(A.252)

Then we select
\delta
and
T
as

\displaystyle\delta=\frac{V_{K}}{2K},\ T=\max\{\bigg(\frac{KV_{K}d^{2}}{16B_{K}^{2}}\bigg)^{1/3},d^{2}K/V_{K}\},\

\displaystyle\text{satisfying }2K\delta\leq V_{K},\frac{dK\sqrt{\delta}}{2\sqrt{2T^{3}}}\leq B_{K},\ T\geq\frac{d^{2}}{2\delta}.

(A.253)

We have the lower bound as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\mu}_{1},\dots,\bm{\mu}_{w}\sim\text{Unif}\{-\Delta,\Delta\}^{d}}\text{Regret}(T,\mathcal{L}_{\bm{\mu}_{1},\dots,\bm{\mu}_{w}})\geq\Omega(d^{2/3}B_{K}^{1/3}V_{K}^{1/3}K^{1/3}\land V_{K}).

(A.254)

Therefore, there must exists
\bm{\mu}_{1}^{*},\dots,\bm{\mu}_{w}^{*}
, satisfying

\displaystyle\text{Regret}(T,\mathcal{L}_{\bm{\mu}_{1}^{*},\dots,\bm{\mu}_{w}^{*}})\geq\Omega(d^{2/3}B_{K}^{1/3}V_{K}^{1/3}K^{1/3}\land V_{K}).

(A.255)

Finally, combining (
A.255
) with the lower bound result in
[
242
]
concludes our proof.
∎

A.6.4
Proof of Lemma
8.4.1

For simplicity, we denote

\displaystyle\hat{\beta}

\displaystyle:=12\sqrt{d\log(1+\frac{wA^{2}}{\alpha^{2}d\lambda})\log(32(\log(\frac{\gamma^{2}}{\alpha}+1)\frac{w^{2}}{\delta})}

\displaystyle+30\log(32(\log(\frac{\gamma^{2}}{\alpha})+1)\frac{w^{2}}{\delta})\frac{R}{\gamma^{2}}+\sqrt{\lambda}B.

(A.256)

It is obvious that
\hat{\beta}\geq\hat{\beta}_{k}
for all
k\in[K]
. We call the restart time rounds
grids
and denote them by
g_{1},g_{2},\ldots g_{\lceil\frac{K}{w}\rceil-1}
, where
g_{i}\%w=0
for all
i\in[\lceil\frac{K}{w}\rceil-1]
. Let
i_{k}
be the grid index of time round
k
,
i.e.
,
g_{i_{k}}\leq k<g_{i_{k}+1}
.

For ease of exposition and without loss of generality, we prove the lemma for
k\in[1,w]
. We calculate the estimation difference
|\bm{a}^{\top}(\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{k}-\bm{\theta}_{k})|
for any
\mathbf{a}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
,
\|\mathbf{a}\|_{2}\leq A
,
k\in[1,w]
.
By definition:

\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{k}=\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}\bm{b}_{k}=\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}(\sum_{t=1}^{k-1}\frac{r_{t}\mathbf{a}_{t}}{\bar{\sigma}_{t}^{2}})=\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}(\sum_{t=1}^{k-1}\frac{\mathbf{a}_{t}\mathbf{a}_{t}^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{t}}{\bar{\sigma}_{t}^{2}}+\sum_{t=1}^{k-1}\frac{\mathbf{a}_{t}\epsilon_{t}}{\bar{\sigma}_{t}^{2}})\,,

(A.257)

where
\hat{\Sigma}_{k}=\lambda\bm{I}+\sum_{t=g_{i_{k}}}^{k-1}\frac{\mathbf{a}_{t}\mathbf{a}_{t}^{\top}}{\bar{\sigma}_{t}^{2}}
.

Then we have

\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{k}-\bm{\theta}_{k}=\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}(\sum_{t=1}^{k-1}\frac{\mathbf{a}_{t}\mathbf{a}_{t}^{\top}}{\bar{\sigma}_{t}^{2}}(\bm{\theta}_{t}-\bm{\theta}_{k})+\sum_{t=1}^{k-1}\frac{\mathbf{a}_{t}\epsilon_{t}}{\bar{\sigma}_{t}^{2}})-\lambda\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}\bm{\theta}_{k}\,.

(A.258)

Therefore

\displaystyle|\mathbf{a}^{\top}(\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{k}-\bm{\theta}_{k})|\leq\left|\mathbf{a}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{k-1}\frac{\mathbf{a}_{t}\mathbf{a}_{t}^{\top}}{\bar{\sigma}_{t}^{2}}(\bm{\theta}_{t}-\bm{\theta}_{k})\right|

\displaystyle+\|\mathbf{a}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}}\|\sum_{t=1}^{k-1}\frac{\mathbf{a}_{t}\epsilon_{t}}{\bar{\sigma}_{t}^{2}}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}}+\lambda\|\mathbf{a}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}}\|\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\bm{\theta}_{k}\|_{2}\,,

(A.259)

where we use the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality.

For the first term, we have that for any
k\in[1,w]

\displaystyle\left|\mathbf{a}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{k}\frac{\mathbf{a}_{t}\mathbf{a}_{t}^{\top}}{\bar{\sigma}_{t}^{2}}(\bm{\theta}_{t}-\bm{\theta}_{k})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=1}^{k-1}|\mathbf{a}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}\frac{\mathbf{a}_{t}}{\bar{\sigma}_{t}}|\cdot|\frac{\mathbf{a}_{t}}{\bar{\sigma}_{t}}^{\top}(\sum_{s=t}^{k-1}(\bm{\theta}_{s}-\bm{\theta}_{s+1}))|

(triangle inequality )

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=1}^{k-1}|\mathbf{a}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}\frac{\mathbf{a}_{t}}{\bar{\sigma}_{t}}|\cdot\|\frac{\mathbf{a}_{t}}{\bar{\sigma}_{t}}\|_{2}\cdot\|\sum_{s=t}^{k-1}(\bm{\theta}_{s}-\bm{\theta}_{s+1})\|_{2}

(Cauchy-Schwarz)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{A}{\alpha}\sum_{t=1}^{k-1}|\mathbf{a}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}\frac{\mathbf{a}_{t}}{\bar{\sigma}_{t}}|\cdot\|\sum_{s=t}^{k-1}(\bm{\theta}_{s}-\bm{\theta}_{s+1})\|_{2}

(
\|\mathbf{a}_{t}\|\leq A
,
\bar{\sigma}_{t}\geq\alpha
)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{A}{\alpha}\sum_{s=1}^{k-1}\sum_{t=1}^{s}|\mathbf{a}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}\frac{\mathbf{a}_{t}}{\bar{\sigma}_{t}}|\cdot\|\bm{\theta}_{s}-\bm{\theta}_{s+1}\|_{2}

(
\sum_{t=1}^{k-1}\sum_{s=t}^{k-1}=\sum_{s=1}^{k-1}\sum_{t=1}^{s}
)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{A}{\alpha}\sum_{s=1}^{k-1}\sqrt{\bigg[\sum_{t=1}^{s}\mathbf{a}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{a}\bigg]\cdot\biggl[\sum_{t=1}^{s}\frac{\mathbf{a}_{t}}{\bar{\sigma}_{t}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}\frac{\mathbf{a}_{t}}{\bar{\sigma}_{t}}\bigg]}\cdot\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{s}-\bm{\theta}_{s+1}\right\rVert_{2}

(Cauchy-Schwarz)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{A}{\alpha}\sum_{s=1}^{k-1}\sqrt{\bigg[\sum_{t=1}^{s}\mathbf{a}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{a}\bigg]\cdot d}\cdot\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{s}-\bm{\theta}_{s+1}\right\rVert_{2}

(
(\star)
)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{A\|\mathbf{a}\|_{2}}{\alpha}\sqrt{d}\sum_{s=1}^{k-1}\sqrt{\frac{\sum_{t=1}^{k-1}1}{\lambda}}\cdot\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{s}-\bm{\theta}_{s+1}\right\rVert_{2}

(
\lambda_{\max}(\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1})\leq\frac{1}{\lambda}
)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{A^{2}}{\alpha}\sqrt{\frac{dw}{\lambda}}\sum_{s=1}^{k-1}\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{s}-\bm{\theta}_{s+1}\right\rVert_{2}\,,

(A.260)

where the inequality
(\star)
follows from the fact that
\sum_{t=1}^{s}\frac{\mathbf{a}_{t}}{\bar{\sigma}_{t}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}\frac{\mathbf{a}_{t}}{\bar{\sigma}_{t}}\leq d
that can be proved as follows. We have
\sum_{t=1}^{k-1}\frac{\mathbf{a}_{t}}{\bar{\sigma}_{t}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}\frac{\mathbf{a}_{t}}{\bar{\sigma}_{t}}=\sum_{t=1}^{k-1}\text{tr}\left(\frac{\mathbf{a}_{t}}{\bar{\sigma}_{t}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}\frac{\mathbf{a}_{t}}{\bar{\sigma}_{t}}\right)=\text{tr}\left(\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{k-1}\frac{\mathbf{a}_{t}}{\bar{\sigma}_{t}}\frac{\mathbf{a}_{t}}{\bar{\sigma}_{t}}^{\top}\right)
. Given the eigenvalue decomposition
\sum_{t=1}^{k-1}\frac{\mathbf{a}_{t}}{\bar{\sigma}_{t}}\frac{\mathbf{a}_{t}}{\bar{\sigma}_{t}}^{\top}=\text{diag}(\lambda_{1},\ldots,\lambda_{d})^{\top}
, we have
\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}=\text{diag}(\lambda_{1}+\lambda,\ldots,\lambda_{d}+\lambda)^{\top}
, and
\text{tr}\left(\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{k-1}\frac{\mathbf{a}_{t}}{\bar{\sigma}_{t}}\frac{\mathbf{a}_{t}}{\bar{\sigma}_{t}}^{\top}\right)=\sum_{i=1}^{d}\frac{\lambda_{j}}{\lambda_{j}+\lambda}\leq d
.

For the second term, by the assumption on
\epsilon_{k}
, we know that

\displaystyle|\epsilon_{k}/\bar{\sigma}_{k}|\leq R/\alpha,

\displaystyle|\epsilon_{k}/\bar{\sigma}_{k}|\cdot\min\{1,\|\mathbf{a}_{k}/\bar{\sigma}_{k}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}}\}\leq R\|\mathbf{a}_{k}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}}/\bar{\sigma}_{k}^{2}\leq R/\gamma^{2},

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\epsilon_{k}|\mathbf{a}_{1:k},\epsilon_{1:k-1}]=0,\ \mathbb{E}[(\epsilon_{k}/\bar{\sigma}_{k})^{2}|\mathbf{a}_{1:k},\epsilon_{1:k-1}]\leq 1,\ \|\mathbf{a}_{k}/\bar{\sigma}_{k}\|_{2}\leq A/\alpha,

Therefore, setting
\mathcal{G}_{k}=\sigma(\mathbf{a}_{1:k},\epsilon_{1:k-1})
,
and using that
\sigma_{k}
is
\mathcal{G}_{k}
-measurable, applying
Theorem
A.6.3
to
(\bm{x}_{k},\eta_{k})=(\bm{a}_{k}/\bar{\sigma}_{k},\epsilon_{k}/\bar{\sigma}_{k})
with
\epsilon=R/\gamma^{2}
, we get that
with probability at least
1-\delta
, for all
k\in[1,w]
,

\displaystyle\|\sum_{t=1}^{k-1}\frac{\mathbf{a}_{t}\epsilon_{t}}{\bar{\sigma}_{t}^{2}}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}}\leq 12\sqrt{d\log(1+\frac{(k\%w)A^{2}}{\alpha^{2}d\lambda})\log(32(\log(\frac{\gamma^{2}}{\alpha}+1)\frac{(k\%w)^{2}}{\delta})}

\displaystyle+30\log(32(\log(\frac{\gamma^{2}}{\alpha})+1)\frac{(k\%w)^{2}}{\delta})\frac{R}{\gamma^{2}}.

(A.261)

For the last term

\displaystyle\lambda\|\mathbf{a}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}}\|\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\bm{\theta}_{k}\|_{2}\leq\lambda\|\mathbf{a}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}}\|\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|_{2}\|\bm{\theta}_{k}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\lambda\|\mathbf{a}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda_{\text{min}}(\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k})}}\|\bm{\theta}_{k}\|_{2}\leq\sqrt{\lambda}B\|\mathbf{a}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}}\,,

(A.262)

where we use the fact that
\lambda_{\text{min}}(\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k})\geq\lambda
.

Therefore, with probabilty at least
1-\delta
, we have

\displaystyle|\mathbf{a}^{\top}(\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{k}-\bm{\theta}_{k})|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{A^{2}}{\alpha}\sqrt{\frac{dw}{\lambda}}\sum_{t=1}^{k-1}\|\bm{\theta}_{t}-\bm{\theta}_{t+1}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 11.74988pt+\|\mathbf{a}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}}\bigg(12\sqrt{d\log(1+\frac{(k\%w)A^{2}}{\alpha^{2}d\lambda})\log(32(\log(\frac{\gamma^{2}}{\alpha}+1)\frac{(k\%w)^{2}}{\delta})}

\displaystyle+30\log(32(\log(\frac{\gamma^{2}}{\alpha})+1)\frac{(k\%w)^{2}}{\delta})\frac{R}{\gamma^{2}}+\sqrt{\lambda}B\bigg)

\displaystyle=\frac{A^{2}}{\alpha}\sqrt{\frac{dw}{\lambda}}\sum_{t=1}^{k-1}\|\bm{\theta}_{t}-\bm{\theta}_{t+1}\|_{2}+\hat{\beta}_{k}\|\mathbf{a}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}}\,,

(A.263)

where
\hat{\beta}_{k}
is defined in Eq.(
8.5
).

A.6.5
Proof for Theorem
8.4.2

For simplicity of analysis, we only analyze the regret over the first grid,
i.e.
, we try to analyze
\text{Regret}(\tilde{K})
for
\tilde{K}\in[1,w]
. Denote
\mathcal{E}_{1}
as the event when Lemma
8.4.1
holds.
Therefore, under event
\mathcal{E}_{1}
, for any
\tilde{K}\in[1,w]
, the regret can be bounded by

\displaystyle\text{Regret}(\tilde{K})

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{\tilde{K}}\big[\langle\mathbf{a}_{k}^{*}-\mathbf{a}_{k},\bm{\theta}_{k}\rangle\big]

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{\tilde{K}}\big[\langle\mathbf{a}_{k}^{*},\bm{\theta}_{k}-\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{k}\rangle+(\langle\mathbf{a}_{k}^{*},\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{k}\rangle+\hat{\beta}_{k}\|\mathbf{a}_{k}^{*}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}})-(\langle\mathbf{a}_{k},\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{k}\rangle+\hat{\beta}_{k}\|\mathbf{a}_{k}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}})

\displaystyle\hskip 11.74988pt+\langle\mathbf{a}_{k},\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{k}-\bm{\theta}_{k}\rangle+\hat{\beta}_{k}\|\mathbf{a}_{k}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}}-\hat{\beta}_{k}\|\mathbf{a}_{k}^{*}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}}\big]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2A^{2}}{\alpha}\sqrt{\frac{dw}{\lambda}}\sum_{k=1}^{\tilde{K}}\sum_{t=1}^{k-1}\|\bm{\theta}_{t}-\bm{\theta}_{t+1}\|_{2}+2\sum_{k=1}^{\tilde{K}}\min\Big\{1,\hat{\beta}_{k}\|\mathbf{a}_{k}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}}\Big\}\,,

(A.264)

where in the last inequality we use the definition of event
\mathcal{E}_{1}
, the arm selection rule in Line 7 of Algo.
8
, and
0\leq\langle\mathbf{a}_{k}^{*},\bm{\theta}^{*}\rangle-\langle\mathbf{a}_{k},\bm{\theta}^{*}\rangle\leq 2
.

Then we will bound the two terms in Eq.(
A.264
).

For the first term, we have

\displaystyle\frac{2A^{2}}{\alpha}\sqrt{\frac{dw}{\lambda}}\sum_{k=1}^{\tilde{K}}\sum_{t=1}^{k-1}\|\bm{\theta}_{t}-\bm{\theta}_{t+1}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{2A^{2}}{\alpha}\sqrt{\frac{dw}{\lambda}}\sum_{t=1}^{\tilde{K}-1}\sum_{k=t}^{\tilde{K}}\|\bm{\theta}_{t}-\bm{\theta}_{t+1}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2A^{2}}{\alpha}\sqrt{\frac{dw}{\lambda}}w\sum_{t=1}^{\tilde{K}-1}\|\bm{\theta}_{t}-\bm{\theta}_{t+1}\|_{2}\,.

(A.265)

To bound the second term in Eq.(
A.264
), we decompose the set
[\tilde{K}]
into a union of two disjoint subsets
[K]=\mathcal{I}_{1}\cup\mathcal{I}_{2}
.

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{1}=\Big\{k\in[\tilde{K}]:\|\frac{\mathbf{a}_{k}}{\bar{\sigma}_{k}}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}}\geq 1\Big\},\ \mathcal{I}_{2}=\Big\{k\in[\tilde{K}]:\|\frac{\mathbf{a}_{k}}{\bar{\sigma}_{k}}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}}<1\Big\}.

(A.266)

Then the following upper bound of
|\mathcal{I}_{1}|
holds:

\displaystyle|\mathcal{I}_{1}|

\displaystyle=\sum_{k\in\mathcal{I}_{1}}\min\Big\{1,\|\frac{\mathbf{a}_{k}}{\bar{\sigma}_{k}}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}}^{2}\Big\}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=1}^{\tilde{K}}\min\Big\{1,\|\frac{\mathbf{a}_{k}}{\bar{\sigma}_{k}}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}}^{2}\Big\}

\displaystyle\leq 2d\iota,

(A.267)

where
\iota=\log(1+\frac{wA^{2}}{d\lambda\alpha^{2}})
, the first equality holds since
\|\frac{\mathbf{x}_{k}}{\bar{\sigma}_{k}}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}}\geq 1
for
k\in\mathcal{I}_{1}
, the last inequality holds due to Lemma
A.6.4
together with the fact
\|\frac{\mathbf{a}_{k}}{\bar{\sigma}_{k}}\|_{2}\leq\frac{A}{\alpha}
since
\bar{\sigma}_{k}\geq\alpha
and
\|\mathbf{a}_{k}\|_{2}\leq A
.

Then, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{\tilde{K}}\min\Big\{1,\hat{\beta}_{k}\|\mathbf{a}_{k}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}}\Big\}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k\in\mathcal{I}_{1}}\min\Big\{1,\bar{\sigma}_{k}\hat{\beta}_{k}\|\frac{\mathbf{a}_{k}}{\bar{\sigma}_{k}}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}}\Big\}+\sum_{k\in\mathcal{I}_{2}}\min\Big\{1,\bar{\sigma}_{k}\hat{\beta}_{k}\|\frac{\mathbf{a}_{k}}{\bar{\sigma}_{k}}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}}\Big\}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg[\sum_{k\in\mathcal{I}_{1}}1\bigg]+\sum_{k\in\mathcal{I}_{2}}\bar{\sigma}_{k}\hat{\beta}_{k}\|\frac{\mathbf{a}_{k}}{\bar{\sigma}_{k}}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\leq 2d\iota+\hat{\beta}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{I}_{2}}\bar{\sigma}_{k}\|\frac{\mathbf{a}_{k}}{\bar{\sigma}_{k}}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}},

(A.268)

where the first inequality holds since
\min\{1,x\}\leq 1
and also
\min\{1,x\}\leq x
,
the second inequality holds by Eq.(
A.267
), and the fact the
\hat{\beta}\geq\hat{\beta}_{k}
for all
k\in[K]
(
\hat{\beta}
is defined in Eq.(
A.256
)). Next we further bound the second summation term in (
A.268
). We decompose
\mathcal{I}_{2}=\mathcal{J}_{1}\cup\mathcal{J}_{2}
, where

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{1}=\bigg\{k\in\mathcal{I}_{2}:\bar{\sigma}_{k}=\sigma_{k}\cup\bar{\sigma}_{k}=\alpha\bigg\},\ \mathcal{J}_{2}=\bigg\{k\in\mathcal{I}_{2}:\bar{\sigma}_{k}=\gamma\sqrt{\|\mathbf{a}_{k}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}}}\bigg\}.

Then
\sum_{k\in\mathcal{I}_{2}}\bar{\sigma}_{k}\|\frac{\mathbf{a}_{k}}{\bar{\sigma}_{k}}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}}=\sum_{k\in\mathcal{J}_{1}}\bar{\sigma}_{k}\|\frac{\mathbf{a}_{k}}{\bar{\sigma}_{k}}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}}
+
\sum_{k\in\mathcal{J}_{2}}\bar{\sigma}_{k}\|\frac{\mathbf{a}_{k}}{\bar{\sigma}_{k}}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}}
. First, for
k\in\mathcal{J}_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k\in\mathcal{J}_{1}}\bar{\sigma}_{k}\|\frac{\mathbf{a}_{k}}{\bar{\sigma}_{k}}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k\in\mathcal{J}_{1}}(\sigma_{k}+\alpha)\min\bigg\{1,\|\frac{\mathbf{a}_{k}}{\bar{\sigma}_{k}}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\sum_{k=1}^{\tilde{K}}(\sigma_{k}+\alpha)^{2}}\sqrt{\sum_{k=1}^{\tilde{K}}\min\bigg\{1,\|\frac{\mathbf{a}_{k}}{\bar{\sigma}_{k}}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}}\bigg\}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2\sum_{k=1}^{\tilde{K}}(\sigma_{k}^{2}+\alpha^{2})}\sqrt{\sum_{k=1}^{\tilde{K}}\min\bigg\{1,\|\frac{\mathbf{a}_{k}}{\bar{\sigma}_{k}}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}}^{2}\bigg\}}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sqrt{\sum_{k=1}^{\tilde{K}}\sigma_{k}^{2}+{\tilde{K}}\alpha^{2}}\sqrt{d\iota}\,,

(A.269)

where the first inequality holds since
\bar{\sigma}_{k}\leq\sigma_{k}+\alpha
for
k\in\mathcal{J}_{1}
and
\|\frac{\mathbf{a}_{k}}{\bar{\sigma}_{k}}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}}\leq 1
since
k\in\mathcal{J}_{1}\subseteq\mathcal{I}_{2}
, the second inequality holds by Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, the third inequality holds due to
(a+b)^{2}\leq 2(a^{2}+b^{2})
, and the last inequality holds due to Lemma
A.6.4
.

Finally we bound the summation for
k\in\mathcal{J}_{2}
. When
k\in\mathcal{J}_{2}
, we have
\bar{\sigma}_{k}=\gamma^{2}\|\frac{\mathbf{a}_{k}}{\bar{\sigma}_{k}}\|_{\hat{\Sigma}_{k}^{-1}}
. Therefore we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k\in\mathcal{J}_{2}}\bar{\sigma}_{k}\|\frac{\mathbf{a}_{k}}{\bar{\sigma}_{k}}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k\in\mathcal{J}_{2}}\gamma^{2}\|\frac{\mathbf{a}_{k}}{\bar{\sigma}_{k}}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=1}^{\tilde{K}}\gamma^{2}\min\bigg\{1,\|\frac{\mathbf{a}_{k}}{\bar{\sigma}_{k}}\|^{2}_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq 2\gamma^{2}d\iota\,,

(A.270)

where in the first inequality we use the fact that
\|\frac{\mathbf{a}_{k}}{\bar{\sigma}_{k}}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}}\leq 1
since
k\in\mathcal{J}_{2}\subseteq\mathcal{I}_{2}
, and in the last inequality we use Lemma
A.6.4
.

Therefore, with Eq.(
A.264
), Eq.(
A.265
), Eq.(
A.268
), Eq.(
A.269
), Eq.(
A.270
), we can get the regret upper bound for
\tilde{K}\in[1,w]

\displaystyle\text{Regret}(\tilde{K})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2A^{2}w^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\alpha}\sqrt{\frac{d}{\lambda}}\sum_{k=1}^{\tilde{K}-1}\|\bm{\theta}_{k}-\bm{\theta}_{k+1}\|_{2}+4\hat{\beta}\sqrt{d\iota}\sqrt{\sum_{k\in[\tilde{K}]}\sigma_{k}^{2}+w\alpha^{2}}

\displaystyle+4d\iota\gamma^{2}\hat{\beta}+4d\iota\,.

(A.271)

Therefore, by the same deduction, we can get that

\displaystyle\text{Regret}([g_{i},g_{i+1}])

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2A^{2}w^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\alpha}\sqrt{\frac{d}{\lambda}}\sum_{k=g_{i}}^{g_{i+1}-1}\|\bm{\theta}_{k}-\bm{\theta}_{k+1}\|_{2}

\displaystyle+4\hat{\beta}\sqrt{d\iota}\sqrt{\sum_{k=g_{i}}^{g_{i+1}}\sigma_{k}^{2}+w\alpha^{2}}+4d\iota\gamma^{2}\hat{\beta}+4d\iota\,,

(A.272)

where we use
\text{Regret}([g_{i},g_{i+1}])
to denote the regret accumulated in the time period
[g_{i},g_{i+1}]
.

Finally, without loss of generality, we assume
K\%w=0
. Then we have

\displaystyle\text{Regret}(\tilde{K})

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=0}^{\frac{K}{w}-1}\text{Regret}([g_{i},g_{i+1}])

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2A^{2}w^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\alpha}\sqrt{\frac{d}{\lambda}}\sum_{i=0}^{\frac{K}{w}-1}\sum_{k=g_{i}}^{g_{i+1}-1}\|\bm{\theta}_{k}-\bm{\theta}_{k+1}\|_{2}+4\hat{\beta}\sqrt{d\iota}\sum_{i=0}^{\frac{K}{w}-1}\sqrt{\sum_{k=g_{i}}^{g_{i+1}}\sigma_{k}^{2}+w\alpha^{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{4d\iota\gamma^{2}\hat{\beta}K}{w}+\frac{4dK\iota}{w}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2A^{2}w^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\alpha}\sqrt{\frac{d}{\lambda}}\sum_{k=1}^{K-1}\|\bm{\theta}_{k}-\bm{\theta}_{k+1}\|_{2}

\displaystyle+4\hat{\beta}\sqrt{d\iota}\sqrt{\frac{K}{w}\sum_{i=0}^{\frac{K}{w}-1}(\sum_{k=g_{i}}^{g_{i+1}}\sigma_{k}^{2}+w\alpha^{2})}+\frac{4d\iota\gamma^{2}\hat{\beta}K}{w}+\frac{4dK\iota}{w}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2A^{2}w^{\frac{3}{2}}B_{K}}{\alpha}\sqrt{\frac{d}{\lambda}}+4\hat{\beta}\sqrt{\frac{Kd\iota}{w}}\sqrt{\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sigma_{k}^{2}+K\alpha^{2}}+\frac{4d\iota\gamma^{2}\hat{\beta}K}{w}+\frac{4dK\iota}{w}\,,

where in the second inequality we use Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, and the last inequality holds due to
\sum_{k\in[K-1]}\|\bm{\theta}_{k}-\bm{\theta}_{k+1}\|_{2}\leq B_{K}
.

A.6.6
Proof for Theorem
8.5.1

Recall that we call the restart time rounds
grids
and denote them by
g_{1},g_{2},\ldots g_{\lceil\frac{K}{w}\rceil-1}
, where
g_{i}\%w=0
for all
i\in[\lceil\frac{K}{w}\rceil-1]
. Let
i_{k}
be the grid index of time round
k
,
i.e.
,
g_{i_{k}}\leq k<g_{i_{k}+1}
. We denote
\hat{\Psi}_{k,\ell}:=\{t:t\in[g_{i_{k}},k-1],\ell_{t}=\ell\}
.

For simplicity of analysis, we first try to bound the regret over the first grid,
i.e.
, we try to analyze
\text{Regret}(\tilde{K})
for
\tilde{K}\in[1,w]
. Note that in this case, for any
k\in[\tilde{K}]
with
\tilde{K}\in[1,w]
, we have
g_{i_{k}}=1
, so
\hat{\Psi}_{k,\ell}:=\{t:t\in[1,k-1],\ell_{t}=\ell\}
.

First, we calculate the estimation difference
|\bm{a}^{\top}(\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{k,\ell}-\bm{\theta}_{k})|
for any
\mathbf{a}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
,
\|\mathbf{a}\|_{2}\leq A
. Recall that by definition,
\hat{\Sigma}_{k,\ell}=2^{-2\ell}\bm{I}+\sum_{t\in\hat{\Psi}_{k,\ell}}w_{t}^{2}\mathbf{a}_{t}\mathbf{a}_{t}^{\top}
,
\hat{\bm{b}}_{k,\ell}=\sum_{t\in\hat{\Psi}_{k,\ell}}w_{t}^{2}r_{t}\mathbf{a}_{t}
, and

\displaystyle\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{k,\ell}

\displaystyle=\hat{\Sigma}_{k,\ell}^{-1}\hat{\bm{b}}_{k,\ell}=\hat{\Sigma}_{k,\ell}^{-1}(\sum_{t\in\hat{\Psi}_{k,\ell}}w_{t}^{2}r_{t}\mathbf{a}_{t})=\hat{\Sigma}_{k,\ell}^{-1}(\sum_{t\in\hat{\Psi}_{k,\ell}}w_{t}^{2}\mathbf{a}_{t}\mathbf{a}_{t}^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{t}+\sum_{t\in\hat{\Psi}_{k,\ell}}w_{t}^{2}\mathbf{a}_{t}\epsilon_{t})\,.

Then we have

\displaystyle\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{k,\ell}-\bm{\theta}_{k}=\hat{\Sigma}_{k,\ell}^{-1}(\sum_{t\in\hat{\Psi}_{k,\ell}}w_{t}^{2}\mathbf{a}_{t}\mathbf{a}_{t}^{\top}(\bm{\theta}_{t}-\bm{\theta}_{k})+\sum_{t\in\hat{\Psi}_{k,\ell}}w_{t}^{2}\mathbf{a}_{t}\epsilon_{t})-2^{-2\ell}\hat{\Sigma}_{k,\ell}^{-1}\bm{\theta}_{k}\,.

(A.273)

Therefore, we can get

\displaystyle|\mathbf{a}^{\top}(\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{k,\ell}-\bm{\theta}_{k})|\leq\left|\mathbf{a}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k,\ell}^{-1}\sum_{t\in\hat{\Psi}_{k,\ell}}w_{t}^{2}{\mathbf{a}_{t}\mathbf{a}_{t}^{\top}}(\bm{\theta}_{t}-\bm{\theta}_{k})\right|+\|\mathbf{a}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k,\ell}^{-1}}\|\sum_{t\in\hat{\Psi}_{k,\ell}}w_{t}^{2}{\mathbf{a}_{t}\epsilon_{t}}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k,\ell}^{-1}}

\displaystyle+2^{-2\ell}\|\mathbf{a}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k,\ell}^{-1}}\|\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k,\ell}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\bm{\theta}_{k}\|_{2}\,,

(A.274)

where we use the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality.

For the first term, we have that for any
k\in[1,w]

\displaystyle\left|\mathbf{a}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k,\ell}^{-1}\sum_{t\in\hat{\Psi}_{k,\ell}}w_{t}^{2}{\mathbf{a}_{t}\mathbf{a}_{t}^{\top}}(\bm{\theta}_{t}-\bm{\theta}_{k})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t\in\hat{\Psi}_{k,\ell}}|\mathbf{a}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}_{k,\ell}^{-1}w_{t}\mathbf{a}_{t}|\cdot|w_{t}\mathbf{a}_{t}^{\top}(\sum_{s=t}^{k-1}(\bm{\theta}_{s}-\bm{\theta}_{s+1}))|

(triangle inequality )

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t\in\hat{\Psi}_{k,\ell}}|\mathbf{a}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}_{k,\ell}^{-1}w_{t}{\mathbf{a}_{t}}|\cdot\|w_{t}\mathbf{a}_{t}\|_{2}\cdot\|\sum_{s=t}^{k-1}(\bm{\theta}_{s}-\bm{\theta}_{s+1})\|_{2}

(Cauchy-Schwarz)

\displaystyle\leq{A}\sum_{t\in\hat{\Psi}_{k,\ell}}|\mathbf{a}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k,\ell}^{-1}w_{t}{\mathbf{a}_{t}}|\cdot\|\sum_{s=t}^{k-1}(\bm{\theta}_{s}-\bm{\theta}_{s+1})\|_{2}

(
\|\mathbf{a}_{t}\|\leq A
,
w_{t}=\frac{2^{-\ell_{t}}}{\|\mathbf{a}_{t}\|_{\hat{\Sigma}_{t,\ell_{t}}^{-1}}}\leq 1
)

\displaystyle\leq{A}\sum_{s=1}^{k-1}\sum_{t\in\hat{\Psi}_{k,\ell}}|\mathbf{a}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k,\ell}^{-1}w_{t}{\mathbf{a}_{t}}|\cdot\|\bm{\theta}_{s}-\bm{\theta}_{s+1}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq{A}\sum_{s=1}^{k-1}\sqrt{\bigg[\sum_{t\in\hat{\Psi}_{k,\ell}}\mathbf{a}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k,\ell}^{-1}\mathbf{a}\bigg]\cdot\biggl[\sum_{t\in\hat{\Psi}_{k,\ell}}w_{t}{\mathbf{a}_{t}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k,\ell}^{-1}w_{t}{\mathbf{a}_{t}}\bigg]}\cdot\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{s}-\bm{\theta}_{s+1}\right\rVert_{2}

(Cauchy-Schwarz)

\displaystyle\leq{A}\sum_{s=1}^{k-1}\sqrt{\bigg[\sum_{t\in\hat{\Psi}_{k,\ell}}\mathbf{a}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k,\ell}^{-1}\mathbf{a}\bigg]\cdot d}\cdot\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{s}-\bm{\theta}_{s+1}\right\rVert_{2}

(
(\star)
)

\displaystyle\leq{A}\|\mathbf{a}\|_{2}\sqrt{d}\sum_{s=1}^{k-1}\sqrt{2^{2\ell}{\sum_{t\in\hat{\Psi}_{k,\ell}}1}}\cdot\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{s}-\bm{\theta}_{s+1}\right\rVert_{2}

(
\lambda_{\max}(\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k,\ell}^{-1})\leq\frac{1}{2^{-2\ell}}=2^{2\ell}
)

\displaystyle\leq{A^{2}}2^{\ell}\sqrt{{dw}}\sum_{s=1}^{k-1}\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{s}-\bm{\theta}_{s+1}\right\rVert_{2}\,,

(A.275)

where the inequality
(\star)
follows from the fact that
\sum_{t\in\hat{\Psi}_{k,\ell}}w_{t}{\mathbf{a}_{t}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k,\ell}^{-1}w_{t}{\mathbf{a}_{t}}\leq d
that can be proved as follows. We have
\sum_{t\in\hat{\Psi}_{k,\ell}}w_{t}{\mathbf{a}_{t}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k,\ell}^{-1}w_{t}{\mathbf{a}_{t}}=\sum_{t\in\hat{\Psi}_{k,\ell}}\text{tr}\left(w_{t}{\mathbf{a}_{t}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k,\ell}^{-1}w_{t}{\mathbf{a}_{t}}\right)=\text{tr}\left(\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k,\ell}^{-1}\sum_{t\in\hat{\Psi}_{k,\ell}}w_{t}^{2}{\mathbf{a}_{t}}{\mathbf{a}_{t}}^{\top}\right)
. Given the eigenvalue decomposition
\sum_{t\in\hat{\Psi}_{k,\ell}}w_{t}^{2}{\mathbf{a}_{t}}{\mathbf{a}_{t}}^{\top}=\text{diag}(\lambda_{1},\ldots,\lambda_{d})^{\top}
, we have
\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k,\ell}=\text{diag}(\lambda_{1}+\lambda,\ldots,\lambda_{d}+\lambda)^{\top}
, and
\text{tr}\left(\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k,\ell}^{-1}\sum_{t\in\hat{\Psi}_{k,\ell}}w_{t}^{2}{\mathbf{a}_{t}}{\mathbf{a}_{t}}^{\top}\right)=\sum_{i=1}^{d}\frac{\lambda_{j}}{\lambda_{j}+\lambda}\leq d
.

For the second term in Eq.(
A.274
), we can apply Theorem
A.6.5
for the layer
\ell
. In detail, for any
k\in[K]
, for each
t\in\hat{\Psi}_{k,\ell}
, we have

\displaystyle\|w_{t}\mathbf{a}_{t}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{t,\ell}^{-1}}=2^{-\ell},\hskip 11.74988pt\mathbb{E}[w_{t}^{2}\epsilon_{t}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t}]\leq w_{t}^{2}\mathbb{E}[\epsilon_{t}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{t}]\leq w_{t}^{2}\sigma_{t}^{2},\hskip 11.74988pt|w_{t}\epsilon_{t}|\leq|\epsilon_{t}|\leq R,

where the last inequality holds due to the fact that

w_{t}=\frac{2^{-\ell_{t}}}{\|\mathbf{a}_{t}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{t,\ell_{t}}^{-1}}}\leq 1
. According to Theorem
A.6.5
, and taking a union bound, we can deduce that with probability at least
1-\delta
, for all
\ell\in[L]
, for all round
k\in\Psi_{K+1,\ell},\

\displaystyle\|\sum_{t\in\hat{\Psi}_{k,\ell}}w_{t}^{2}{\mathbf{a}_{t}\epsilon_{t}}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k,\ell}^{-1}}\leq 16\cdot 2^{-\ell}\sqrt{\sum_{t\in\hat{\Psi}_{k,\ell}}w_{t}^{2}\sigma_{t}^{2}\log(\frac{4w^{2}L}{\delta})}+6\cdot 2^{-\ell}R\log(\frac{4w^{2}L}{\delta})\,.

(A.276)

For simplicity, we denote
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{conf}}
as the event such that Eq.(
A.276
) holds.

For the third term in Eq.(
A.274
), we have

\displaystyle 2^{-2\ell}\|\mathbf{a}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k,\ell}^{-1}}\|\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k,\ell}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\bm{\theta}_{k}\|_{2}\leq 2^{-2\ell}\|\mathbf{a}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k,\ell}^{-1}}\|\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\|_{2}\|\bm{\theta}_{k}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2^{-2\ell}\|\mathbf{a}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k,\ell}^{-1}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda_{\text{min}}(\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k,\ell})}}\|\bm{\theta}_{k}\|_{2}\leq 2^{-\ell}B\|\mathbf{a}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k}^{-1}}\,,

(A.277)

where we use the fact that
\lambda_{\text{min}}(\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k,\ell})\geq 2^{-2\ell}
.

For simplicity, we denote
\ell^{*}=\lceil\frac{1}{2}\log_{2}\log\left(4(w+1)^{2}L/\delta\right)\rceil+8
.
Then, under
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{conf}}
, by the definition of
\hat{\beta}_{k,\ell}
in Eq.(
8.9
), Lemma
A.6.6
and Lemma
A.6.7
, with probability at least
1-\delta
, we have for all
\ell^{*}+1\leq\ell\leq L
,

\displaystyle\hat{\beta}_{k,\ell}\geq 16\cdot 2^{-\ell}\sqrt{\sum_{t\in\hat{\Psi}_{k,\ell}}w_{t}^{2}\sigma_{t}^{2}\log(\frac{4w^{2}L}{\delta})}+6\cdot 2^{-\ell}R\log(\frac{4w^{2}L}{\delta})+2^{-\ell}B.

(A.278)

Therefore, with Eq.(
A.274
), Eq.(
A.275
), Eq.(
A.276
), Eq.(
A.277
), Eq.(
A.278
), with probability at least
1-3\delta
, for all
\ell^{*}+1\leq\ell\leq L
we have

|\mathbf{a}^{\top}(\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{k,\ell}-\bm{\theta}_{k})|\leq{A^{2}}2^{\ell}\sqrt{{dw}}\sum_{s=1}^{k-1}\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{s}-\bm{\theta}_{s+1}\right\rVert_{2}+\hat{\beta}_{k,\ell}\|\mathbf{a}\|_{\hat{\Sigma}_{k,\ell}^{-1}}\,.

(A.279)

Then for all
k\in[K]
such that
\ell^{*}+1\leq\ell_{k}\leq L
, with probability at least
1-3\delta
we have

\displaystyle\langle\mathbf{a}_{k}^{*},\bm{\theta}_{k}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\min_{\ell\in[L]}\langle\mathbf{a}_{k}^{*},\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{k,\ell}\rangle+{A^{2}}2^{\ell}\sqrt{{dw}}\sum_{s=1}^{k-1}\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{s}-\bm{\theta}_{s+1}\right\rVert_{2}+\hat{\beta}_{k,\ell}\|\mathbf{a}_{k}^{*}\|_{\hat{\Sigma}_{k,\ell}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\leq{A^{2}}2^{L}\sqrt{{dw}}\sum_{s=1}^{k-1}\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{s}-\bm{\theta}_{s+1}\right\rVert_{2}+\min_{\ell\in[L]}\langle\mathbf{a}_{k}^{*},\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{k,\ell}\rangle+\hat{\beta}_{k,\ell}\|\mathbf{a}_{k}^{*}\|_{\hat{\Sigma}_{k,\ell}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\leq{A^{2}}2^{L}\sqrt{{dw}}\sum_{s=1}^{k-1}\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{s}-\bm{\theta}_{s+1}\right\rVert_{2}+\min_{\ell\in[L]}\langle\mathbf{a}_{k},\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{k,\ell}\rangle+\hat{\beta}_{k,\ell}\|\mathbf{a}_{k}\|_{\hat{\Sigma}_{k,\ell}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\leq{A^{2}}2^{L}\sqrt{{dw}}\sum_{s=1}^{k-1}\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{s}-\bm{\theta}_{s+1}\right\rVert_{2}+\langle\mathbf{a}_{k},\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{k,\ell_{k}-1}\rangle+\hat{\beta}_{k,\ell_{k}-1}\|\mathbf{a}_{k}\|_{\hat{\Sigma}_{k,\ell_{k}-1}^{-1}}\,,

(A.280)

where the first inequality holds because of Eq.(
A.279
), the third inequality holds because of the arm selection rule in Line 8 of Algo.
12
.

We decompose the regret for
\tilde{K}\in[1,w]
as follows

\displaystyle\text{Regret}(\tilde{K})

\displaystyle=\sum_{k\in[\tilde{K}]}(\langle\mathbf{a}_{k}^{*},\bm{\theta}_{k}\rangle-\langle\mathbf{a}_{k},\bm{\theta}_{k}\rangle)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\ell\in[\ell^{*}]}\sum_{k\in\hat{\Psi}_{\tilde{K}+1,\ell}}(\langle\mathbf{a}_{k}^{*},\bm{\theta}_{k}\rangle-\langle\mathbf{a}_{k},\bm{\theta}_{k}\rangle)+\sum_{\ell\in[L]\backslash[\ell^{*}]}\sum_{k\in\hat{\Psi}_{\tilde{K}+1,\ell}}(\langle\mathbf{a}_{k}^{*},\bm{\theta}_{k}\rangle-\langle\mathbf{a}_{k},\bm{\theta}_{k}\rangle)

\displaystyle\hskip 11.74988pt+\sum_{k\in\hat{\Psi}_{\tilde{K}+1,L+1}}(\langle\mathbf{a}_{k}^{*},\bm{\theta}_{k}\rangle-\langle\mathbf{a}_{k},\bm{\theta}_{k}\rangle)\,.

(A.281)

We will bound the three terms separately. For the first term, we have
for layer
\ell\in[\ell^{*}]
and round
k\in\hat{\Psi}_{\tilde{K}+1,\ell}
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k\in\hat{\Psi}_{\tilde{K}+1,\ell}}\big(\langle\mathbf{a}_{k}^{*},\bm{\theta}^{*}\rangle-\langle\mathbf{a}_{k},\bm{\theta}^{*}\rangle\big)

\displaystyle\leq 2\left|\Psi_{K+1,\ell}\right|

\displaystyle=2^{2\ell+1}\sum_{k\in\hat{\Psi}_{\tilde{K}+1,\ell}}\|w_{k}\mathbf{a}_{k}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k,\ell}^{-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\cdot 128^{2}\log(\frac{4(w+1)^{2}L}{\delta})\sum_{k\in\hat{\Psi}_{\tilde{K}+1,\ell}}\|w_{k}\mathbf{a}_{k}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k,\ell}^{-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\cdot 128^{2}\log(\frac{4(w+1)^{2}L}{\delta})\cdot 2d\log(1+\frac{2^{2\ell}wA^{2}}{d})

\displaystyle=\tilde{O}(d)\,,

(A.282)

where the first inequality holds because the reward is in
[-1,1]
, the equation follows from the fact that
\|w_{k}\mathbf{a}_{k}\|_{\hat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{k,\ell}^{-1}}=2^{-\ell}
holds for all
k\in\Psi_{K+1,\ell}
,
the second inequality holds due to the fact that
2^{\ell^{*}}\leq 128\sqrt{\log(4(w+1)^{2}L/\delta)}
, and the last inequality holds due to Lemma
A.6.4
.

Therefore

\sum_{\ell\in[\ell^{*}]}\sum_{k\in\hat{\Psi}_{\tilde{K}+1,\ell}}(\langle\mathbf{a}_{k}^{*},\bm{\theta}_{k}\rangle-\langle\mathbf{a}_{k},\bm{\theta}_{k}\rangle)=\tilde{O}(d)\,.

(A.283)

For the second part in Eq.(
A.281
), we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell\in[L]\backslash[\ell^{*}]}\sum_{k\in\hat{\Psi}_{\tilde{K}+1,\ell}}(\langle\mathbf{a}_{k}^{*},\bm{\theta}_{k}\rangle-\langle\mathbf{a}_{k},\bm{\theta}_{k}\rangle)

\displaystyle\hskip 11.74988pt\leq\sum_{\ell\in[L]\backslash[\ell^{*}]}\sum_{k\in\hat{\Psi}_{\tilde{K}+1,\ell}}\bigg(\langle\mathbf{a}_{k},\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{k,\ell-1}\rangle+\hat{\beta}_{k,\ell-1}\|\mathbf{a}_{k}\|_{\hat{\Sigma}_{k,\ell-1}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\hskip 11.74988pt+{A^{2}}2^{L}\sqrt{{dw}}\sum_{k\in\hat{\Psi}_{\tilde{K}+1,\ell}}\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{s}-\bm{\theta}_{s+1}\right\rVert_{2}-\langle\mathbf{a}_{k},\bm{\theta}_{k}\rangle\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{\ell\in[L]\backslash[\ell^{*}]}\sum_{k\in\hat{\Psi}_{\tilde{K}+1,\ell}}\hat{\beta}_{k,\ell-1}\|\mathbf{a}_{k}\|_{\hat{\Sigma}_{k,\ell-1}^{-1}}+{A^{2}}\sqrt{{dw}}\sum_{\ell\in[L]\backslash[\ell^{*}]}\sum_{k\in\hat{\Psi}_{\tilde{K}+1,\ell}}2^{L}\sum_{s=1}^{k-1}\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{s}-\bm{\theta}_{s+1}\right\rVert_{2}\,,

(A.284)

where the inequality holds due to Eq.(
A.280
), the second inequality holds due to Eq.(
A.279
). We then try to bound the two terms.

For the first term in Eq.(
A.284
), we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell\in[L]\backslash[\ell^{*}]}\sum_{k\in\hat{\Psi}_{\tilde{K}+1,\ell}}\hat{\beta}_{k,\ell-1}\|\mathbf{a}_{k}\|_{\hat{\Sigma}_{k,\ell-1}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\ell\in[L]\backslash[\ell^{*}]}\sum_{k\in\hat{\Psi}_{\tilde{K}+1,\ell}}\hat{\beta}_{k,\ell-1}\cdot 2^{-\ell}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\ell\in[L]\backslash[\ell^{*}]}\hat{\beta}_{\tilde{K},\ell-1}\cdot 2^{-\ell}\left|\hat{\Psi}_{\tilde{K}+1,\ell}\right|

\displaystyle=\sum_{\ell\in[L]\backslash[\ell^{*}]}\hat{\beta}_{\tilde{K},\ell-1}\cdot 2^{\ell}\sum_{k\in\hat{\Psi}_{\tilde{K}+1,\ell}}\|w_{k}\mathbf{a}_{k}\|_{\Sigma_{k,\ell}^{-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\ell\in[L]\backslash[\ell^{*}]}\hat{\beta}_{\tilde{K},\ell-1}\cdot 2^{\ell}\cdot 2d\log(1+\frac{2^{2\ell}\tilde{K}A^{2}}{d})

\displaystyle=\tilde{O}(d\cdot 2^{\ell}\cdot\hat{\beta}_{\tilde{K},\ell-1})

\displaystyle=\tilde{O}\bigg(d\big(\sqrt{\sum_{k=1}^{\tilde{K}}\sigma_{k}^{2}}+R+1\big)\bigg)\,,

(A.285)

where the first inequality holds because by the algorithm design, we have for all
k\in\hat{\Psi}_{\tilde{K}+1,\ell}
:
\|\mathbf{a}_{k}\|_{\hat{\Sigma}_{k,\ell-1}^{-1}}\leq 2^{-\ell}
; the second inequality holds because for all
k\in\hat{\Psi}_{\tilde{K}+1,\ell}
,
\hat{\beta}_{k,\ell-1}\leq\hat{\beta}_{\tilde{K},\ell-1}
; the first equality holds because for all
k\in\hat{\Psi}_{\tilde{K}+1,\ell}
,
\|w_{k}\mathbf{a}_{k}\|_{\Sigma_{k,\ell}^{-1}}^{2}=2^{-2\ell}
; the third inequality holds by Lemma
A.6.4
; the last two equalities hold because by Lemma
A.6.6
and Lemma
A.6.7
, we have
\hat{\beta}_{\tilde{K},\ell-1}=\tilde{O}\bigg(2^{-\ell}(\sqrt{\sum_{k=1}^{\tilde{K}}\sigma_{k}^{2}}+R+1)\bigg)
.

For the second term in Eq.(
A.284
), we have

\displaystyle{A^{2}}\sqrt{{dw}}\sum_{\ell\in[L]\backslash[\ell^{*}]}\sum_{k\in\hat{\Psi}_{\tilde{K}+1,\ell}}2^{L}\sum_{s=1}^{k-1}\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{s}-\bm{\theta}_{s+1}\right\rVert_{2}

\displaystyle\leq A^{2}2^{L}\sqrt{dw}\sum_{k\in[\tilde{K}-1]}\sum_{s=1}^{k-1}\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{s}-\bm{\theta}_{s+1}\right\rVert_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{A^{2}\sqrt{d}w^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\alpha}\sum_{k=1}^{\tilde{K}-1}\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{k}-\bm{\theta}_{k+1}\right\rVert_{2}

(A.286)

Therefore, with this, Eq.(
A.284
), and Eq.(
A.285
), we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell\in[L]\backslash[\ell^{*}]}\sum_{k\in\hat{\Psi}_{\tilde{K}+1,\ell}}(\langle\mathbf{a}_{k}^{*},\bm{\theta}_{k}\rangle-\langle\mathbf{a}_{k},\bm{\theta}_{k}\rangle)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{A^{2}\sqrt{d}w^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\alpha}\sum_{k=1}^{\tilde{K}-1}\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{k}-\bm{\theta}_{k+1}\right\rVert_{2}+\tilde{O}\bigg(d\big(\sqrt{\sum_{k=1}^{\tilde{K}}\sigma_{k}^{2}}+R+1\big)\bigg)\,.

(A.287)

Finally, for the last term in Eq.(
A.281
), we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k\in\hat{\Psi}_{\tilde{K}+1,L+1}}(\langle\mathbf{a}_{k}^{*},\bm{\theta}_{k}\rangle-\langle\mathbf{a}_{k},\bm{\theta}_{k}\rangle)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k\in\hat{\Psi}_{\tilde{K}+1,L+1}}\bigg(\langle\mathbf{a}_{k},\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{k,L}\rangle+\hat{\beta}_{k,L}\|\mathbf{a}_{k}\|_{\hat{\Sigma}_{k,L}^{-1}}+A^{2}2^{L}\sqrt{dw}\sum_{s=1}^{k-1}\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{s}-\bm{\theta}_{s+1}\right\rVert_{2}-\langle\mathbf{a}_{k},\bm{\theta}_{k}\rangle\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k\in\hat{\Psi}_{\tilde{K}+1,L+1}}\bigg(2\hat{\beta}_{k,L}\|\mathbf{a}_{k}\|_{\hat{\Sigma}_{k,L}^{-1}}+A^{2}2^{L+1}\sqrt{dw}\sum_{s=1}^{k-1}\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{s}-\bm{\theta}_{s+1}\right\rVert_{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k\in\hat{\Psi}_{\tilde{K}+1,L+1}}\bigg(2^{-L+1}\hat{\beta}_{k,L}+A^{2}2^{L+1}\sqrt{dw}\sum_{s=1}^{k-1}\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{s}-\bm{\theta}_{s+1}\right\rVert_{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2A^{2}\sqrt{d}w^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\alpha}\sum_{k=1}^{\tilde{K}-1}\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{k}-\bm{\theta}_{k+1}\right\rVert_{2}+\sum_{k\in\hat{\Psi}_{\tilde{K}+1,L+1}}2^{-L+1}\hat{\beta}_{\tilde{K},L}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2A^{2}\sqrt{d}w^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\alpha}\sum_{k=1}^{\tilde{K}-1}\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{k}-\bm{\theta}_{k+1}\right\rVert_{2}+w\cdot 2\alpha\cdot\hat{\beta}_{\tilde{K},L}

\displaystyle=\frac{2A^{2}\sqrt{d}w^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\alpha}\sum_{k=1}^{\tilde{K}-1}\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{k}-\bm{\theta}_{k+1}\right\rVert_{2}+\tilde{O}\bigg(w\alpha^{2}\cdot\big(\sqrt{\sum_{k=1}^{\tilde{K}}\sigma_{k}^{2}}+R+1\big)\bigg)\,,

(A.288)

where the first inequality holds due to Eq.(
A.280
), the second inequality holds due to Eq.(
A.279
), the third inequality holds because by the algorithm design, we have for all
k\in\hat{\Psi}_{\tilde{K}+1,L+1}
:
\|\mathbf{a}_{k}\|_{\hat{\Sigma}_{k,L}^{-1}}\leq 2^{-L}
, the fourth inequality holds due to the same reasons as before, and the fact that
\hat{\beta}_{\tilde{K},L}\geq\hat{\beta}_{k,L}
for all
k\in\hat{\beta}_{\tilde{K},L}
; the last inequality holds due to
\hat{\beta}_{\tilde{K},\ell-1}=\tilde{O}\bigg(\alpha(\sqrt{\sum_{k=1}^{\tilde{K}}\sigma_{k}^{2}}+R+1)\bigg)
.

Plugging Eq.(
A.287
), Eq.(
A.288
), and Eq.(
A.283
) into Eq.(
A.281
), we can get that for
\tilde{K}\in[1,w]

\displaystyle\text{Regret}(\tilde{K})

\displaystyle=\tilde{O}\bigg(\frac{A^{2}\sqrt{d}w^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\alpha}\sum_{k=1}^{\tilde{K}-1}\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{k}-\bm{\theta}_{k+1}\right\rVert_{2}+\big(w\alpha^{2}+d\big)\cdot\big(\sqrt{\sum_{k=1}^{\tilde{K}}\sigma_{k}^{2}}+R+1\big)\bigg)\,.

(A.289)

By the same deduction we can get

\displaystyle\text{Regret}([g_{i},g_{i+1}])

\displaystyle=\tilde{O}\bigg(\frac{A^{2}\sqrt{d}w^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\alpha}\sum_{k=g_{i}}^{g_{i+1}}\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{k}-\bm{\theta}_{k+1}\right\rVert_{2}+\big(w\alpha^{2}+d\big)\cdot\big(\sqrt{\sum_{k=g_{i}}^{g_{i+1}}\sigma_{k}^{2}}+R+1\big)\bigg)\,.

(A.290)

Finally, without loss of generality, we assume
K\%w=0
. Then we have

\displaystyle\text{Regret}(K)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=0}^{\frac{K}{w}-1}\text{Regret}([g_{i},g_{i+1}])

\displaystyle=\tilde{O}\bigg(\frac{A^{2}\sqrt{d}w^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\alpha}\sum_{i=0}^{\frac{K}{w}-1}\sum_{k=g_{i}}^{g_{i+1}}\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{k}-\bm{\theta}_{k+1}\right\rVert_{2}+\big(w\alpha^{2}+d\big)\cdot\sum_{i=0}^{\frac{K}{w}-1}\big(\sqrt{\sum_{k=g_{i}}^{g_{i+1}}\sigma_{k}^{2}}+R+1\big)\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq\tilde{O}\bigg(\frac{A^{2}\sqrt{d}w^{\frac{3}{2}}}{\alpha}\sum_{k=1}^{K-1}\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{k}-\bm{\theta}_{k+1}\right\rVert_{2}+\big(w\alpha^{2}+d\big)\cdot\big(\sqrt{\frac{K}{w}\sum_{i=0}^{\frac{K}{w}-1}\sum_{k=g_{i}}^{g_{i+1}}\sigma_{k}^{2}}+\frac{KR}{w}+\frac{K}{w}\big)\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq\tilde{O}\bigg(\frac{A^{2}\sqrt{d}w^{\frac{3}{2}}B_{K}}{\alpha}+\big(w\alpha^{2}+d\big)\cdot\sqrt{\frac{K}{w}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sigma_{k}^{2}}+\big(1+R\big)\cdot\big(K\alpha^{2}+\frac{Kd}{w}\big)\bigg)\,,

where the first inequality holds due to the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, the last inequality holds because
\sum_{k=1}^{K-1}\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{k}-\bm{\theta}_{k+1}\right\rVert_{2}\leq B_{K}
.

A.6.7
Proof of Theorem
A.6.1

With the candidate pool set
\mathcal{P}
designed as in Eq.(
A.241
), Eq.(
A.242
), Eq.(
A.243
), and
H=\lceil d^{\frac{2}{5}}K^{\frac{2}{5}}\rceil
, we have
\left|\mathcal{P}\right|=O(\log K)
, and for any
w\in\mathcal{W}
,
w\leq H
.

We denote the optimal
(w,\alpha)
with the knowledge of
V_{K}
and
B_{K}
in Corollary
8.2
as
(w^{*},\alpha^{*})
. We denote the best approximation of
(w^{*},\alpha^{*})
in the candidate set
\mathcal{P}
as
(w^{+},\alpha^{+})
. Then we can decompose the regret as follows

\displaystyle\text{Regret}(K)

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\langle\mathbf{a}_{t}^{*},\bm{\theta}_{k}\rangle-\langle\mathbf{a}_{t},\bm{\theta}_{k}\rangle

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\sum_{k=1}^{K}\langle\mathbf{a}_{t}^{*},\bm{\theta}_{k}\rangle-\sum_{i=1}^{\lceil\frac{K}{H}\rceil}\sum_{k=(i-1)H+1}^{iH}\langle\mathbf{a}_{t}(w^{+},\alpha^{+}),\bm{\theta}_{k}\rangle}_{(1)}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\sum_{i=1}^{\lceil\frac{K}{H}\rceil}\sum_{k=(i-1)H+1}^{iH}\langle\mathbf{a}_{t}(w^{+},\alpha^{+}),\bm{\theta}_{k}\rangle-\langle\mathbf{a}_{t}(w_{i},\alpha_{i}),\bm{\theta}_{k}\rangle}_{(2)}\,.

(A.291)

The first term (1) is the dynamic regret of
\text{Restarted SAVE}^{+}
with the best parameters in the candidate pool
\mathcal{P}
. The second term (2) is the regret overhead of meta-algorithm due to adaptive exploration of unknown optimal parameters.

By the design of the candidate pool set
\mathcal{P}
in Eq.(
A.241
), Eq.(
A.242
), Eq.(
A.243
), we have that there exists a pair
(w^{+},\alpha^{+})\in\mathcal{P}
such that
w^{+}<w^{*}<2w^{+}
, and
\alpha^{+}<\alpha^{*}<2\alpha^{+}
. Therefore, employing the regret bound in Theorem
8.5.1
, we can get

\displaystyle(1)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{\lceil\frac{K}{H}\rceil}\tilde{O}(\sqrt{d}w^{+1.5}B_{i}/\alpha^{+}+\alpha^{+2}(H+\sqrt{w^{+}HV_{i}})+d\sqrt{HV_{i}/w^{+}}+dH/w^{+})

\displaystyle\leq\tilde{O}(\sqrt{d}w^{+1.5}B_{K}/\alpha^{+}+\alpha^{+2}(K+\sqrt{w^{+}H\frac{K}{H}\sum_{i=1}^{\lceil\frac{K}{H}\rceil}V_{i}})+d\sqrt{H\frac{K}{H}\sum_{i=1}^{\lceil\frac{K}{H}\rceil}V_{i}/w^{+}}

\displaystyle\hskip 11.74988pt+dK/w^{+})

\displaystyle=\tilde{O}(\sqrt{d}w^{+1.5}B_{K}/\alpha^{+}+\alpha^{+2}(K+\sqrt{w^{+}KV_{K}})+d\sqrt{KV_{K}/w^{+}}+dK/w^{+})

\displaystyle=\tilde{O}(\sqrt{d}w^{*1.5}B_{K}/\alpha^{*}+\alpha^{*2}(K+\sqrt{w^{*}KV_{K}})+d\sqrt{KV_{K}/w^{*}}+dK/w^{*})

\displaystyle=\tilde{O}(d^{4/5}V_{K}^{2/5}B_{K}^{1/5}K^{2/5}+d^{2/3}B_{K}^{1/3}K^{2/3})\,,

(A.292)

where we denote
B_{i}
as the total variation budget in block
i
,
V_{i}
is the total variance in block
i
, the second inequality is by Cauchy–Schwarz inequality, the first equality holds due to
\sum_{i=1}^{\lceil\frac{K}{H}\rceil}B_{i}=B_{K}
,
\sum_{i=1}^{\lceil\frac{K}{H}\rceil}V_{i}=V_{K}
, the second equality holds due to
w^{+}<w^{*}<2w^{+}
and
\alpha^{+}<\alpha^{*}<2\alpha^{+}
, the last equality holds by Corollary
8.2
.

We then try to bound the second term (2). We denote by
\mathcal{E}
the event such that Lemma
A.6.9
holds, and denote by
R_{i}:=\sum_{k=(i-1)H+1}^{iH}\langle\mathbf{a}_{t}(w^{+},\alpha^{+}),\bm{\theta}_{k}\rangle-\langle\mathbf{a}_{t}(w_{i},\alpha_{i}),\bm{\theta}_{k}\rangle
the instantaneous regret of the meta learner in the block
i
. Then we have

\displaystyle(2)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bigg[\sum_{i=1}^{\lceil\frac{K}{H}\rceil}R_{i}\bigg]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bigg[\sum_{i=1}^{\lceil\frac{K}{H}\rceil}R_{i}|\mathcal{E}\bigg]P(\mathcal{E})+\mathbb{E}\bigg[\sum_{i=1}^{\lceil\frac{K}{H}\rceil}R_{i}|\overline{\mathcal{E}}\bigg]P(\mathcal{\overline{\mathcal{E}}})

\displaystyle\leq\tilde{O}\bigg(L_{\text{max}}\sqrt{\frac{K}{H}\left|\mathcal{P}\right|}\bigg)\cdot(1-\frac{2}{K})+\tilde{O}(K)\cdot\frac{2}{K}

\displaystyle=\tilde{O}(\sqrt{H\left|\mathcal{P}\right|K})

\displaystyle=\tilde{O}(d^{\frac{1}{5}}K^{\frac{7}{10}})\,,

(A.293)

where
L_{\text{max}}:=\max_{i\in[\lceil\frac{K}{H}\rceil]}L_{i}
, the first inequality holds due to the standard regret upper bound result for Exp3
[
12
]
, the third equality holds due to Lemma
A.6.9
, the last equality holds since
H=\lceil d^{\frac{2}{5}}K^{\frac{2}{5}}\rceil
, and
\left|\mathcal{P}\right|=O(\log K)
.

Finally, combining the above results for term (1) and term (2), we have

\displaystyle\text{Regret}(K)=\tilde{O}(d^{4/5}V_{K}^{2/5}B_{K}^{1/5}K^{2/5}+d^{2/3}B_{K}^{1/3}K^{2/3}+d^{\frac{1}{5}}K^{\frac{7}{10}}).

(A.294)

A.6.8
Technical Lemmas

Theorem A.6.3

(Theorem 4.3,
[
290
]
)
.

Let
\{\mathcal{G}_{k}\}_{k=1}^{\infty}
be a filtration, and
\{\mathbf{x}_{k},\eta_{k}\}_{k\geq 1}
be a stochastic process such that

\mathbf{x}_{k}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
is
\mathcal{G}_{k}
-measurable and
\eta_{k}\in\mathbb{R}
is
\mathcal{G}_{k+1}
-measurable.
Let
L,\sigma,\lambda,\epsilon>0
,
\bm{\mu}^{*}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
.
For
k\geq 1
,
let
y_{k}=\langle\bm{\mu}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{k}\rangle+\eta_{k}
and
suppose that
\eta_{k},\mathbf{x}_{k}
also satisfy

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\eta_{k}|\mathcal{G}_{k}]=0,\ \mathbb{E}[\eta_{k}^{2}|\mathcal{G}_{k}]\leq\sigma^{2},\ |\eta_{k}|\leq R,\,\|\mathbf{x}_{k}\|_{2}\leq L.

(A.295)

For
k\geq 1
, let
\mathbf{Z}_{k}=\lambda\mathbf{I}+\sum_{i=1}^{k}\mathbf{x}_{i}\mathbf{x}_{i}^{\top}
,
\mathbf{b}_{k}=\sum_{i=1}^{k}y_{i}\mathbf{x}_{i}
,
\bm{\mu}_{k}=\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{b}_{k}
, and

\displaystyle\beta_{k}

\displaystyle=12\sqrt{\sigma^{2}d\log(1+kL^{2}/(d\lambda))\log(32(\log(R/\epsilon)+1)k^{2}/\delta)}

\displaystyle\hskip 11.74988pt+24\log(32(\log(R/\epsilon)+1)k^{2}/\delta)\max_{1\leq i\leq k}\{|\eta_{i}|\min\{1,\|\mathbf{x}_{i}\|_{\mathbf{Z}_{i-1}^{-1}}\}\}

\displaystyle\hskip 11.74988pt+6\log(32(\log(R/\epsilon)+1)k^{2}/\delta)\epsilon.

Then, for any
0<\delta<1
, we have with probability at least
1-\delta
that,

\displaystyle\forall k\geq 1,\ \big\|\textstyle{\sum}_{i=1}^{k}\mathbf{x}_{i}\eta_{i}\big\|_{\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{-1}}\leq\beta_{k},\ \|\bm{\mu}_{k}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{\mathbf{Z}_{k}}\leq\beta_{k}+\sqrt{\lambda}\|\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}.

Lemma A.6.4

(Lemma 11,
[
1
]
)
.

For any
\lambda>0
and sequence
\{\mathbf{x}_{k}\}_{k=1}^{K}\subset\mathbb{R}^{d}

for
k\in[K]
, define
\mathbf{Z}_{k}=\lambda\mathbf{I}+\sum_{i=1}^{k-1}\mathbf{x}_{i}\mathbf{x}_{i}^{\top}
.
Then, provided that
\|\mathbf{x}_{k}\|_{2}\leq L
holds for all
k\in[K]
,
we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\min\big\{1,\|\mathbf{x}_{k}\|_{\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{-1}}^{2}\big\}\leq 2d\log\big(1+KL^{2}/(d\lambda)\big).

Theorem A.6.5

(Theorem 2.1,
[
286
]
)
.

Let
\{\mathcal{G}_{k}\}_{k=1}^{\infty}
be a filtration, and
\{\mathbf{x}_{k},\eta_{k}\}_{k\geq 1}
be a stochastic process such that

\mathbf{x}_{k}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
is
\mathcal{G}_{k}
-measurable and
\eta_{k}\in\mathbb{R}
is
\mathcal{G}_{k+1}
-measurable.
Let
L,\sigma,\lambda,\epsilon>0
,
\bm{\mu}^{*}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
.
For
k\geq 1
,
let
y_{k}=\langle\bm{\mu}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{k}\rangle+\eta_{k}
, where
\eta_{k},\mathbf{x}_{k}
satisfy

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\eta_{k}|\mathcal{G}_{k}]=0,\ |\eta_{k}|\leq R,\ \sum_{i=1}^{k}\mathbb{E}[\eta_{i}^{2}|\mathcal{G}_{i}]\leq v_{k},\ \ \text{for}\ \forall\ k\geq 1

For
k\geq 1
, let
\mathbf{Z}_{k}=\lambda\mathbf{I}+\sum_{i=1}^{k}\mathbf{x}_{i}\mathbf{x}_{i}^{\top}
,
\mathbf{b}_{k}=\sum_{i=1}^{k}y_{i}\mathbf{x}_{i}
,
\bm{\mu}_{k}=\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{b}_{k}
, and

\displaystyle\beta_{k}

\displaystyle=16\rho\sqrt{v_{k}\log(4w^{2}/\delta)}+6\rho R\log(4w^{2}/\delta),

where
\rho\geq\sup_{k\geq 1}\|\mathbf{x}_{k}\|_{\mathbf{Z}_{k-1}^{-1}}
.
Then, for any
0<\delta<1
, we have with probability at least
1-\delta
that,

\displaystyle\forall k\geq 1,\ \big\|\textstyle{\sum}_{i=1}^{k}\mathbf{x}_{i}\eta_{i}\big\|_{\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{-1}}\leq\beta_{k},\|\bm{\mu}_{k}-\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{\mathbf{Z}_{k}}\leq\beta_{k}+\sqrt{\lambda}\|\bm{\mu}^{*}\|_{2}.

Lemma A.6.6

(Adopted from Lemma B.4,
[
286
]
)
.

Let weight
w_{i}
be defined in Algorithm
12
.
With probability at least
1-2\delta
, for all
k\geq 1
,
\ell\in[L]
, the following two inequalities hold simultaneously:

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in\hat{\Psi}_{k+1,\ell}}w_{i}^{2}\sigma_{i}^{2}\leq 2\sum_{i\in\hat{\Psi}_{k+1,\ell}}w_{i}^{2}\epsilon_{i}^{2}+\frac{14}{3}R^{2}\log(4w^{2}L/\delta),

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in\hat{\Psi}_{k+1,\ell}}w_{i}^{2}\epsilon_{i}^{2}\leq\frac{3}{2}\sum_{i\in\hat{\Psi}_{k+1,\ell}}w_{i}^{2}\sigma_{i}^{2}+\frac{7}{3}R^{2}\log(4w^{2}L/\delta).

For simplicity, we denote
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{VaR}}
as the event such that the two inequalities in Lemma
A.6.6
holds.

Lemma A.6.7

(Adopted from Lemma B.5,
[
286
]
)
.

Suppose that
\|\bm{\theta}^{*}\|_{2}\leq B
. Let weight
w_{i}
be defined in Algorithm
12
.
On the event
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{conf}}
and
\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{VaR}}
(defined in Eq.(
A.276
), Lemma
A.6.6
), for all
k\geq 1
,
\ell\in[L]
such that
2^{\ell}\geq 64\sqrt{\log\left(4(w+1)^{2}L/\delta\right)}
, we have the following inequalities:

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in\Psi_{k+1,\ell}}w_{i}^{2}\sigma_{i}^{2}\leq 8\sum_{i\in\Psi_{k+1,\ell}}w_{i}^{2}\left(r_{i}-\langle\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{k+1,\ell},\mathbf{a}_{i}\rangle\right)^{2}+6R^{2}\log(4(w+1)^{2}L/\delta)+2^{-2\ell+2}B^{2},

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in\Psi_{k+1,\ell}}w_{i}^{2}\left(r_{i}-\langle\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{k+1,\ell},\mathbf{a}_{i}\rangle\right)^{2}\leq\frac{3}{2}\sum_{i\in\Psi_{k+1,\ell}}w_{i}^{2}\sigma_{i}^{2}+\frac{7}{3}R^{2}\log(4w^{2}L/\delta)+2^{-2\ell}B^{2}.

Lemma A.6.8

(
[
74
]
)
.

Let
M,v>0
be fixed constants. Let
\{x_{i}\}_{i=1}^{n}
be a stochastic process,
\{\mathcal{G}_{i}\}_{i}
be a filtration so that for all
i\in[n]
,
x_{i}
is
\mathcal{G}_{i}
-measurable, while almost surely

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[x_{i}|\mathcal{G}_{i-1}\right]=0,\hskip 11.74988pt|x_{i}|\leq M,\hskip 11.74988pt\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[x_{i}^{2}|\mathcal{G}_{i-1}]\leq v.

Then for any
\delta>0
, with probability at least
1-\delta
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}x_{i}\leq\sqrt{2v\log(1/\delta)}+2/3\cdot M\log(1/\delta).

Lemma A.6.9
.

Let
N=\lceil\frac{K}{H}\rceil
. Denote by
L_{i}
the absolute value of cumulative rewards for episode
i
, i.e.,
L_{i}=\sum_{k=(i-1)H+1}^{iH}r_{k}
, then

\mathbb{P}\left[\forall i\in[N],L_{i}\leq H+R\sqrt{\frac{H}{2}\log\big(K(\frac{K}{H}+1)\big)}+\frac{2}{3}\cdot R\log\big(K(\frac{K}{H}+1)\big)\right]\geq 1-\frac{1}{K}.

(A.296)

Proof.

By Lemma
A.6.8
, we have that with probability at least
1-1/K

\displaystyle\sum_{k=(i-1)\cdot H+1}^{i\cdot H}\epsilon_{i}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2\sum_{k=(i-1)\cdot H+1}^{i\cdot H}\sigma_{k}^{2}\log(NK)}+2/3\cdot R\log(NK)

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2H\frac{R^{2}}{4}\log(NK)}+2/3\cdot R\log(NK)

\displaystyle\leq R\sqrt{\frac{H}{2}\log\big(K\cdot(\frac{K}{H}+1)\big)}+\frac{2}{3}\cdot R\log\big(K\cdot(\frac{K}{H}+1)\big)\,,

(A.297)

where we use union bound, and in the second inequality we use the fact that since
\left|\epsilon_{k}\right|\leq R
, we have
\sigma_{k}^{2}\leq\frac{R^{2}}{4}
. Finally, together with the assumption that
r_{k}\leq 1
for all
k\in[K]
, we complete the proof.
∎

A.7
Clustering Of Neural Dueling Bandits (CONDB) Algorithm

Here we provide the complete statement of our CONDB algorithm.

Algorithm 19

Clustering Of Neural Dueling Bandits (CONDB)

1:

Input:

f(T_{i,t})\triangleq\frac{\beta_{T}+B\sqrt{\frac{\lambda}{\kappa_{\mu}}}+1}{\sqrt{2\tilde{\lambda}_{x}T_{i,t}}}
, regularization parameter
\lambda>0
, confidence parameter
\beta_{T}\triangleq\frac{1}{\kappa_{\mu}}\sqrt{\widetilde{d}+2\log(u/\delta)}
.

\phi(\bm{x})=\frac{1}{\sqrt{m_{\text{NN}}}}g(\bm{x};\bm{\theta}_{0})
where
\bm{\theta}_{0}
denotes the NN parameters at initialization.

2:

Initialization:

\bm{V}_{0}=\bm{V}_{i,0}=\frac{\lambda}{\kappa_{\mu}}\mathbf{I}
,
\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,0}=\bm{0}
,
\forall{i\in\mathcal{U}}
, a complete Graph
G_{0}=(\mathcal{U},E_{0})
over
\mathcal{U}
.

3:

for

t=1,\ldots,T

do

4:

Receive user
i_{t}\in\mathcal{U}
, and feasible arm set
\mathcal{X}_{t}
;

5:

Find the connected component
\overline{C}_{t}
for user
i_{t}
in the current graph
G_{t-1}
as the current cluster;

6:

Train the neural network using
\{(\bm{x}_{s,1},\bm{x}_{s,2},y_{s})\}_{s\in[t-1],i_{s}\in\overline{C}_{t}}
by minimizing the following loss function:

\displaystyle\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{t}

\displaystyle=\arg\min_{\bm{\theta}}-\frac{1}{m}\sum_{s\in[t-1]\atop i_{s}\in\overline{C}_{t}}\left(y_{s}\log\mu\left(h(\bm{x}_{s,1};\bm{\theta})-h(\bm{x}_{s,2};\bm{\theta})\right)+(1-y_{s})\log\mu\left(h(\bm{x}_{s,2};\bm{\theta})-h(\bm{x}_{s,1};\bm{\theta})\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\lambda}{2}\left\lVert\bm{\theta}-\bm{\theta}_{0}\right\rVert_{2}^{2};

(A.298)

7:

Calculate the aggregated information matrix for cluster
\overline{C}_{t}
:
\bm{V}_{t-1}=\bm{V}_{0}+\sum_{s\in[t-1]\atop i_{s}\in\overline{C}_{t}}(\phi(\bm{x}_{s,1})-\phi(\bm{x}_{s,2}))(\phi(\bm{x}_{s,1})-\phi(\bm{x}_{s,2}))^{\top}
.

8:

Choose the first arm
\bm{x}_{t,1}=\arg\max_{\bm{x}\in\mathcal{X}_{t}}h(\bm{x};\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{t})
;

9:

Choose the second arm
\bm{x}_{t,2}=\arg\max_{\bm{x}\in\mathcal{X}_{t}}h(\bm{x};\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{t})+\left(\beta_{T}+B\sqrt{\frac{\lambda}{\kappa_{\mu}}}+1\right)\left\lVert\left(\phi(\bm{x})-\phi(\bm{x}_{t,1})\right)\right\rVert_{\bm{V}_{t-1}^{-1}}
;

10:

Observe the preference feedback:
y_{t}=\mathbbm{1}(\bm{x}_{t,1}\succ\bm{x}_{t,2})
, and update history:
\mathcal{D}_{t}=\{i_{s},\bm{x}_{s,1},\bm{x}_{s,2},y_{s}\}_{s=1,\ldots,t}
;

11:

Train the neural network using all data for user
i_{t}
:
\{(\bm{x}_{s,1},\bm{x}_{s,2},y_{s})\}_{s\in[t],i_{s}=i_{t}}
by minimizing the following loss function:

\displaystyle\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i_{t},t}

\displaystyle=\text{argmin}_{\bm{\theta}}-\frac{1}{m_{\text{NN}}}\sum_{s\in[t-1]\atop i_{s}=i_{t}}\Big(y_{s}\log\mu\left(h(\bm{x}_{s,1};\bm{\theta})-h(\bm{x}_{s,2};\bm{\theta})\right)

\displaystyle+(1-y_{s})\log\mu\left(h(\bm{x}_{s,2};\bm{\theta})-h(\bm{x}_{s,1};\bm{\theta})\right)\Big)+\frac{\lambda}{2}\left\lVert\bm{\theta}-\bm{\theta}_{0}\right\rVert_{2}^{2};

(A.299)

keep the estimations of other users unchanged;

12:

Delete the edge
(i_{t},\ell)\in E_{t-1}
if

\sqrt{m_{\text{NN}}}\left\lVert\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i_{t},t}-\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{\ell,t}\right\rVert_{2}>f(T_{i_{t},t})+f(T_{\ell,t})

(A.300)

13:

end

for

A.8
Proof of Theorem
9.4.1

First, we prove the following lemma.

Lemma A.8.1
.

With probability at least
1-\delta
for some
\delta\in(0,1)
, at any
t\in[T]
:

\left\lVert\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t}-\bm{\theta}^{j(i)}\right\rVert_{2}\leq\frac{\sqrt{\lambda\kappa_{\mu}}+\sqrt{2\log(u/\delta)+d\log(1+T_{i,t}\kappa_{\mu}/d\lambda)}}{\kappa_{\mu}\sqrt{\lambda_{\text{min}}(\bm{V}_{i,t-1})}},\forall{i\in\mathcal{U}}\,,

(A.301)

where
\bm{V}_{i,t-1}=\frac{\lambda}{\kappa_{\mu}}\mathbf{I}+\sum_{s\in[t-1]\atop i_{s}=i}(\phi(\bm{x}_{s,1})-\phi(\bm{x}_{s,2}))(\phi(\bm{x}_{s,1})-\phi(\bm{x}_{s,2}))^{\top}
, and
T_{i,t}
denotes the number of rounds of seeing user
i
in the first
t
rounds.

Proof.

First, we prove the following result.

For a fixed user
i
, with probability at least
1-\delta
for some
\delta\in(0,1)
, at any
t\in[T]
:

\left\lVert\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t}-\bm{\theta}^{j(i)}\right\rVert_{\bm{V}_{i,t-1}}\leq\frac{\sqrt{\lambda\kappa_{\mu}}+\sqrt{2\log(1/\delta)+d\log(1+4T_{i,t}\kappa_{\mu}/d\lambda)}}{\kappa_{\mu}}\,,

(A.302)

Recall that
f_{i}(\bm{x})=\bm{\theta}_{i}^{\top}\phi(\bm{x})
. In iteration
s
, define
\widetilde{\phi}_{s}=\phi(\bm{x}_{s,1})-\phi(\bm{x}_{s,2})
.
And we define
\widetilde{f}_{i,s}=f_{i}(\bm{x}_{s,1})-f_{i}(\bm{x}_{s,2})=\bm{\theta}_{i}^{\top}\widetilde{\phi}_{s}
.

For any
\bm{\theta}_{f^{\prime}}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
, define

G_{i,t}(\bm{\theta}_{f^{\prime}})=\sum_{s\in[t-1]:\atop i_{s}=i}\left(\mu(\bm{\theta}_{f^{\prime}}^{\top}\widetilde{\phi}_{s})-\mu(\bm{\theta}_{i}^{\top}\widetilde{\phi}_{s})\right)\widetilde{\phi}_{s}+\lambda\bm{\theta}_{f^{\prime}}.

For
\lambda^{\prime}\in(0,1)
, setting
\bm{\theta}_{\bar{f}}=\lambda^{\prime}\bm{\theta}_{f^{\prime}_{1}}+(1-\lambda^{\prime})\bm{\theta}_{f^{\prime}_{2}}
.
and using the mean-value theorem, we get:

\displaystyle G_{i,t}(\bm{\theta}_{f^{\prime}_{1}})-G_{i,t}(\bm{\theta}_{f^{\prime}_{2}})

\displaystyle=\left[\sum_{s\in[t-1]:\atop i_{s}=i}\nabla\mu(\bm{\theta}_{\bar{f}}^{\top}\widetilde{\phi}_{s})\widetilde{\phi}_{s}\widetilde{\phi}_{s}^{\top}+\lambda\mathbf{I}\right](\bm{\theta}_{f^{\prime}_{1}}-\bm{\theta}_{f^{\prime}_{2}})

\displaystyle\left(\bm{\theta}_{i}\text{ is constant}\right)

Define
\bm{M}_{i,t-1}=\left[\sum_{s\in[t-1]:\atop i_{s}=i}\nabla\mu(\bm{\theta}_{\bar{f}}^{\top}\widetilde{\phi}_{s})\widetilde{\phi}_{s}\widetilde{\phi}_{s}^{\top}+\lambda\mathbf{I}\right]
, and recall that
\bm{V}_{i,t-1}=\sum_{s\in[t-1]:\atop i_{s}=i}\widetilde{\phi}_{s}\widetilde{\phi}_{s}^{\top}+\frac{\lambda}{\kappa_{\mu}}\mathbf{I}
.
Then we have that
\bm{M}_{i,t-1}\succeq\kappa_{\mu}\bm{V}_{i,t-1}
and that
\bm{V}^{-1}_{i,t-1}\succeq\kappa_{\mu}\bm{M}^{-1}_{i,t-1}
, where we use the notation
\bm{M}\succeq\bm{V}
to denote that
\bm{M}-\bm{V}
is a positive semi-definite matrix. Then we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert G_{i,t}(\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t})-\lambda\bm{\theta}_{i}\right\rVert_{\bm{V}_{i,t-1}^{-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\left\lVert G_{i,t}(\bm{\theta}_{i})-G_{t}(\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t})\right\rVert_{\bm{V}_{i,t-1}^{-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\left\lVert\bm{M}_{i,t-1}(\bm{\theta}_{i}-\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t})\right\rVert_{\bm{V}_{i,t-1}^{-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\left(G_{i,t}(\bm{\theta}_{i})=\lambda\bm{\theta}_{i}\text{ by definition}\right)

\displaystyle=(\bm{\theta}_{i}-\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t})^{\top}\bm{M}_{i,t-1}\bm{V}_{i,t-1}^{-1}\bm{M}_{i,t-1}(\bm{\theta}_{i}-\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t})

\displaystyle\geq(\bm{\theta}_{i}-\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t})^{\top}\bm{M}_{i,t-1}\kappa_{\mu}\bm{M}_{i,t-1}^{-1}\bm{M}_{i,t-1}(\bm{\theta}_{i}-\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t})

\displaystyle=\kappa_{\mu}(\bm{\theta}_{i}-\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t})^{\top}\bm{M}_{i,t-1}(\bm{\theta}_{i}-\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t})

\displaystyle\geq\kappa_{\mu}(\bm{\theta}_{i}-\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t})^{\top}\kappa_{\mu}\bm{V}_{i,t-1}(\bm{\theta}_{i}-\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t})

\displaystyle=\kappa_{\mu}^{2}(\bm{\theta}_{i}-\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t})^{\top}\bm{V}_{i,t-1}(\bm{\theta}_{i}-\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t})

\displaystyle=\kappa_{\mu}^{2}\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{i}-\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t}\right\rVert^{2}_{\bm{V}_{i,t-1}}

\displaystyle\left(\text{as }||\bm{x}||_{\bm{A}}^{2}=\bm{x}^{\top}\bm{A}\bm{x}\right)

The first inequality is because
\bm{V}^{-1}_{i,t-1}\succeq\kappa_{\mu}\bm{M}^{-1}_{i,t-1}
, and the second inequality follows from
\bm{M}_{i,t-1}\succeq\kappa_{\mu}\bm{V}_{i,t-1}
.

Note that
\frac{\kappa_{\mu}}{\lambda}\mathbf{I}\succeq\bm{V}_{i,t-1}
, which allows us to show that

\begin{split}\left\lVert\lambda\bm{\theta}_{i}\right\rVert_{\bm{V}_{i,t-1}^{-1}}=\lambda\sqrt{\bm{\theta}_{i}^{\top}\bm{V}_{i,t-1}^{-1}\bm{\theta}_{i}}\leq\lambda\sqrt{\bm{\theta}_{i}^{\top}\frac{\kappa_{\mu}}{\lambda}\bm{\theta}_{i}}\leq\sqrt{\lambda\kappa_{\mu}}\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{i}\right\rVert_{2}\leq\sqrt{\lambda\kappa_{\mu}}.\end{split}

(A.304)

Using the two equations above, we have that

\begin{split}\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{i}-\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t}\right\rVert_{\bm{V}_{i,t-1}}\leq\frac{1}{\kappa_{\mu}}\left\lVert G_{i,t}(\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t})-\lambda\bm{\theta}_{i}\right\rVert_{\bm{V}_{i,t-1}^{-1}}&\leq\frac{1}{\kappa_{\mu}}\left\lVert G_{i,t}(\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t})\right\rVert_{\bm{V}_{i,t-1}^{-1}}+\frac{1}{\kappa_{\mu}}\left\lVert\lambda\bm{\theta}_{i}\right\rVert_{\bm{V}_{i,t-1}^{-1}}\\
&\leq\frac{1}{\kappa_{\mu}}\left\lVert G_{i,t}(\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t})\right\rVert_{\bm{V}_{i,t-1}^{-1}}+\sqrt{\frac{\lambda}{\kappa_{\mu}}}\end{split}

(A.305)

Then, let
f^{i}_{t,s}=\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t}^{\top}\tilde{\phi}_{s}
, we have:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\kappa_{\mu}^{2}}\left\lVert G_{i,t}(\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t})\right\rVert_{\bm{V}_{i,t-1}^{-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\kappa_{\mu}^{2}}\left\lVert\sum_{s\in[t-1]:\atop i_{s}=i}(\mu(\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t}^{\top}\widetilde{\phi}_{s})-\mu(\bm{\theta}_{i}^{\top}\widetilde{\phi}_{s}))\widetilde{\phi}_{s}+\lambda\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t}\right\rVert_{\bm{V}_{i,t-1}^{-1}}^{2})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\kappa_{\mu}^{2}}\left\lVert\sum_{s\in[t-1]:\atop i_{s}=i}(\mu(f^{i}_{t,s})-\mu(\widetilde{f}_{i,s}))\widetilde{\phi}_{s}+\lambda\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t}\right\rVert_{\bm{V}_{i,t-1}^{-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\kappa_{\mu}^{2}}\left\lVert\sum_{s\in[t-1]:\atop i_{s}=i}(\mu(f^{i}_{t,s})-(y_{s}-\epsilon_{s}))\widetilde{\phi}_{s}+\lambda\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t}\right\rVert_{\bm{V}_{i,t-1}^{-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\kappa_{\mu}^{2}}\left\lVert\sum_{s\in[t-1]:\atop i_{s}=i}\left(\mu(f^{i}_{t,s})-y_{s}\right)\widetilde{\phi}_{s}+\sum_{s\in[t-1]:\atop i_{s}=i}\epsilon_{s}\widetilde{\phi}_{s}+\lambda\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t}\right\rVert_{\bm{V}_{i,t-1}^{-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\kappa_{\mu}^{2}}\left\lVert\sum_{s\in[t-1]:\atop i_{s}=i}\epsilon_{s}\widetilde{\phi}_{s}\right\rVert_{\bm{V}_{i,t-1}^{-1}}^{2}.

The last step holds due to the following reasoning. Recall that
\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t}
is computed using MLE by solving the following equation:

\displaystyle\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i_{t},t}=\arg\min_{\bm{\theta}}\Bigg[-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}s\in[t-1]\\
i_{s}=i_{t}\end{subarray}}\bigg(y_{s}\log\mu\big(\bm{\theta}^{\top}[\phi(\bm{x}_{s,1})-\phi(\bm{x}_{s,2})]\big)

\displaystyle+(1-y_{s})\log\mu\big(\bm{\theta}^{\top}[\phi(\bm{x}_{s,2})-\phi(\bm{x}_{s,1})]\big)\bigg)+\frac{\lambda}{2}\|\bm{\theta}\|_{2}^{2}\Bigg].

(A.306)

Setting its gradient to
0
, the following is satisfied:

\sum_{s\in[t-1]:\atop i_{s}=i}\left(\mu\left(\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t}^{\top}\widetilde{\phi}_{s}\right)-y_{s}\right)\widetilde{\phi}_{s}+\lambda\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t}=0,

(A.307)

which is used in the last step.

Now we have

\frac{1}{\kappa_{\mu}^{2}}\left\lVert G_{i,t}(\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t})\right\rVert_{\bm{V}_{i,t-1}^{-1}}^{2}\leq\frac{1}{\kappa_{\mu}^{2}}\left\lVert\sum_{s\in[t-1]:\atop i_{s}=i}\epsilon_{s}\widetilde{\phi}_{s}\right\rVert_{\bm{V}_{i,t-1}^{-1}}^{2}.

(A.308)

Denote
\bm{V}\triangleq\frac{\lambda}{\kappa_{\mu}}\mathbf{I}
.
Note that the sequence of observation noises
\{\epsilon_{s}\}
is
1
-sub-Gaussian.

Next, we can apply Theorem 1 from
[
1
]
, to obtain

\left\lVert\sum_{s\in[t-1]:\atop i_{s}=i}\epsilon_{s}\widetilde{\phi}_{s}\right\rVert_{\bm{V}_{i,t-1}^{-1}}^{2}\leq 2\log\left(\frac{\det(\bm{V}_{i,t-1})^{1/2}}{\delta\det(\bm{V})^{1/2}}\right),

(A.309)

which holds with probability of at least
1-\delta
.

Next, based on our assumption that
\left\lVert\widetilde{\phi}_{s}\right\rVert_{2}\leq 2
, according to Lemma 10 from
[
1
]
, we have that

\det(\bm{V}_{i,t-1})\leq\left(\lambda/\kappa_{\mu}+4T_{i,t}/d\right)^{d}\,,

(A.310)

where
T_{i,t}
denotes the number of rounds of serving user
i
in the first
t
rounds.
Therefore,

\sqrt{\frac{\det{\bm{V}_{i,t-1}}}{\det(V)}}\leq\sqrt{\frac{\left(\lambda/\kappa_{\mu}+4T_{i,t}/d\right)^{d}}{(\lambda/\kappa_{\mu})^{d}}}=\left(1+4T_{i,t}\kappa_{\mu}/(d\lambda)\right)^{\frac{d}{2}}

(A.311)

This gives us

\left\lVert\sum_{s\in[t-1]:\atop i_{s}=i}\epsilon_{s}\widetilde{\phi}_{s}\right\rVert_{\bm{V}_{i,t-1}^{-1}}^{2}\leq 2\log\left(\frac{\det(\bm{V}_{i,t-1})^{1/2}}{\delta\det(V)^{1/2}}\right)\leq 2\log(1/\delta)+d\log\left(1+4T_{i,t}\kappa_{\mu}/(d\lambda)\right)

(A.312)

Then, with the above reasoning, we have that with probability at least
1-\delta
for some
\delta\in(0,1)
, at any
t\in[T]
:

\left\lVert\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t}-\bm{\theta}^{j(i)}\right\rVert_{\bm{V}_{i,t-1}}\leq\frac{\sqrt{\lambda\kappa_{\mu}}+\sqrt{2\log(1/\delta)+d\log(1+4T_{i,t}\kappa_{\mu}/d\lambda)}}{\kappa_{\mu}}\,,

(A.313)

Taking a union bound over
u
users, we have that with probability at least
1-\delta
for some
\delta\in(0,1)
, at any
t\in[T]
:

\left\lVert\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t}-\bm{\theta}^{j(i)}\right\rVert_{\bm{V}_{i,t-1}}\leq\frac{\sqrt{\lambda\kappa_{\mu}}+\sqrt{2\log(u/\delta)+d\log(1+4T_{i,t}\kappa_{\mu}/d\lambda)}}{\kappa_{\mu}}\,,\forall i\in\mathcal{U}.

(A.314)

Then we have that with probability at least
1-\delta
for all
t\in[T]
and all
i\in\mathcal{U}

\displaystyle\left\lVert\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t}-\bm{\theta}^{j(i)}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\left\lVert\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t}-\bm{\theta}^{j(i)}\right\rVert_{\bm{V}_{i,t-1}}}{\sqrt{\lambda_{\text{min}(\bm{V}_{i,t-1})}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sqrt{\lambda\kappa_{\mu}}+\sqrt{2\log(u/\delta)+d\log(1+4T_{i,t}\kappa_{\mu}/d\lambda)}}{\kappa_{\mu}{\sqrt{\lambda_{\text{min}(\bm{V}_{i,t-1})}}}}.

(A.315)

∎

Then, we prove the following lemma, which gives a sufficient time
T_{0}
for the COLDB algorithm to cluster all the users correctly with high probability.

Lemma A.8.2
.

With the carefully designed edge deletion rule, after

\displaystyle T_{0}

\displaystyle\triangleq 16u\log(\frac{u}{\delta})+4u\max\{\frac{128d}{\kappa_{\mu}^{2}\tilde{\lambda}_{x}\gamma^{2}}\log(\frac{u}{\delta}),\frac{16}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}^{2}}\log(\frac{8ud}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}^{2}\delta})\}

\displaystyle=O\bigg(u\left(\frac{d}{\kappa_{\mu}^{2}\tilde{\lambda}_{x}\gamma^{2}}+\frac{1}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}^{2}}\right)\log\frac{1}{\delta}\bigg)

rounds, with probability at least
1-3\delta
for some
\delta\in(0,\frac{1}{3})
, COLDB can cluster all the users correctly.

Proof.

Then, with the item regularity assumption stated in Assumption
9.4
, Lemma J.1 in
[
236
]
, together with Lemma 7 in
[
139
]
, and applying a union bound, with probability at least
1-\delta
, for all
i\in\mathcal{U}
, at any
t
such that
T_{i,t}\geq\frac{16}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}^{2}}\log(\frac{8ud}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}^{2}\delta})
, we have:

\lambda_{\text{min}}(\bm{V}_{i,t})\geq 2\tilde{\lambda}_{x}T_{i,t}\,.

(A.316)

Then, together with Lemma
A.8.1
, we have: if
T_{i,t}\geq\frac{16}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}^{2}}\log(\frac{8ud}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}^{2}\delta})
, then with probability
\geq 1-2\delta
, we have:

\displaystyle\left\lVert\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t}-\bm{\theta}^{j(i)}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sqrt{\lambda\kappa_{\mu}}+\sqrt{2\log(u/\delta)+d\log(1+4T_{i,t}\kappa_{\mu}/d\lambda)}}{\kappa_{\mu}{\sqrt{\lambda_{\text{min}(\bm{V}_{i,t-1})}}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sqrt{\lambda\kappa_{\mu}}+\sqrt{2\log(u/\delta)+d\log(1+4T_{i,t}\kappa_{\mu}/d\lambda)}}{\kappa_{\mu}{\sqrt{2\tilde{\lambda}_{x}T_{i,t}}}}\,.

Now, let

\frac{\sqrt{\lambda\kappa_{\mu}}+\sqrt{2\log(u/\delta)+d\log(1+4T_{i,t}\kappa_{\mu}/d\lambda)}}{\kappa_{\mu}{\sqrt{2\tilde{\lambda}_{x}T_{i,t}}}}<\frac{\gamma}{4}\,,

(A.317)

Let
\lambda\kappa_{\mu}\leq 2\log(u/\delta)+d\log(1+4T_{i,t}\kappa_{\mu}/d\lambda)
, which typically holds (
\kappa_{\mu}
is typically very small), we can get

\frac{2\log(u/\delta)+d\log(1+4T_{i,t}\kappa_{\mu}/d\lambda)}{2\kappa_{\mu}^{2}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}T_{i,t}}}<\frac{\gamma^{2}}{64}\,,

(A.318)

and a sufficient condition for it to hold is

\displaystyle\frac{2\log(u/\delta)}{2\kappa_{\mu}^{2}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}T_{i,t}}}<\frac{\gamma^{2}}{128}

(A.319)

and

\frac{d\log(1+4T_{i,t}\kappa_{\mu}/d\lambda)}{2\kappa_{\mu}^{2}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}T_{i,t}}}<\frac{\gamma^{2}}{128}\,.

(A.320)

Solving Eq.(
A.319
), we can get

T_{i,t}\geq\frac{128\log(u/\delta)}{\kappa_{\mu}^{2}\tilde{\lambda}_{x}\gamma^{2}}\,.

(A.321)

Following Lemma 9 in
[
139
]
, we can get the following sufficient condition for Eq.(
A.320
):

T_{i,t}\geq\frac{128d}{\kappa_{\mu}^{2}\tilde{\lambda}_{x}\gamma^{2}}\log(\frac{512}{\lambda\kappa_{\mu}\tilde{\lambda}_{x}\gamma^{2}})\,.

(A.322)

Let
u/\delta\geq 512/\lambda\kappa_{\mu}\tilde{\lambda}_{x}\gamma^{2}
, which is typically held. Then, combining all together, we have that if

T_{i,t}\geq\max\{\frac{128d}{\kappa_{\mu}^{2}\tilde{\lambda}_{x}\gamma^{2}}\log(\frac{u}{\delta}),\frac{16}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}^{2}}\log(\frac{8ud}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}^{2}\delta})\},\forall i\in\mathcal{U}\,,

(A.323)

then with probability at least
1-2\delta
, we have

\left\lVert\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t}-\bm{\theta}^{j(i)}\right\rVert<\gamma/4,\forall i\in\mathcal{U}\,.

(A.324)

By Lemma 8 in
[
139
]
, and Assumption
9.3
of user arrival uniformness, we have that for all

\displaystyle T_{0}

\displaystyle\triangleq 16u\log(\frac{u}{\delta})+4u\max\{\frac{128d}{\kappa_{\mu}^{2}\tilde{\lambda}_{x}\gamma^{2}}\log(\frac{u}{\delta}),\frac{16}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}^{2}}\log(\frac{8ud}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}^{2}\delta})\}

\displaystyle=O\bigg(u\left(\frac{d}{\kappa_{\mu}^{2}\tilde{\lambda}_{x}\gamma^{2}}+\frac{1}{\tilde{\lambda}_{x}^{2}}\right)\log\frac{1}{\delta}\bigg)\,,

the condition in Eq.(
A.323
) is satisfied with probability at least
1-\delta
.

Therefore we have that for all
t\geq T_{0}
, with probability
\geq 1-3\delta
:

\left\lVert\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t}-\bm{\theta}^{j(i)}\right\rVert_{2}<\frac{\gamma}{4}\,,\forall{i\in\mathcal{U}}\,.

(A.325)

Finally, we only need to show that with
\left\lVert\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t}-\bm{\theta}^{j(i)}\right\rVert_{2}<\frac{\gamma}{4}\,,\forall{i\in\mathcal{U}}
, the algorithm can cluster all the users correctly. First, when the edge
(i,l)
is deleted, user
i
and user
j
must belong to different
ground-truth clusters
, i.e.,
\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{i}-\bm{\theta}_{l}\right\rVert_{2}>0
. This is because by the deletion rule of the algorithm, the concentration bound, and triangle inequality

\displaystyle\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{i}-\bm{\theta}_{l}\right\rVert_{2}=\left\lVert\bm{\theta}^{j(i)}-\bm{\theta}^{j(l)}\right\rVert_{2}

\displaystyle\geq\left\lVert\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t}-\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{l,t}\right\rVert_{2}-\left\lVert\bm{\theta}^{j(l)}-\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{l,t}\right\rVert_{2}-\left\lVert\bm{\theta}^{j(i)}-\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t}\right\rVert_{2}

\displaystyle\geq\left\lVert\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t}-\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{l,t}\right\rVert_{2}-f(T_{i,t})-f(T_{l,t})>0\,.

(A.326)

Second, we can show that if
\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{i}-\bm{\theta}_{l}\right\rVert>\gamma
, meaning that user
i
and user
l
are not in the same
ground-truth cluster
, COLDB will delete the edge
(i,l)
after
T_{0}
. This is because

\displaystyle\left\lVert\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t}-\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{l,t}\right\rVert

\displaystyle\geq\left\lVert\bm{\theta}_{i}-\bm{\theta}_{l}\right\rVert-\left\lVert\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{i,t}-\bm{\theta}^{j(i)}\right\rVert_{2}-\left\lVert\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{l,t}-\bm{\theta}^{j(l)}\right\rVert_{2}

\displaystyle>\gamma-\frac{\gamma}{4}-\frac{\gamma}{4}

\displaystyle=\frac{\gamma}{2}>f(T_{i,t})+f(T_{l,t})\,,

(A.327)

which will trigger the edge deletion rule to delete edge
(i,l)
. Combining all the reasoning above, we can finish the proof.
∎

Then, we prove the following lemmas for the cluster-based statistics.

Lemma A.8.3
.

With probability at least
1-4\delta
for some
\delta\in(0,1/4)
, at any
t\geq T_{0}
:

\left\lVert\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{t}-\bm{\theta}_{i_{t}}\right\rVert_{\bm{V}_{t-1}}\leq\frac{\sqrt{\lambda\kappa_{\mu}}+\sqrt{2\log(u/\delta)+d\log(1+4T\kappa_{\mu}/d\lambda)}}{\kappa_{\mu}}\,.

(A.328)

Proof.

First, by Lemma
A.8.2
, we have that with probability at least
1-3\delta
, all the users are clustered correctly, i.e.,
\overline{C}_{t}=C_{j(i_{t})},\forall t\geq T_{0}
.
Recall that
f_{i}(\bm{x})=\bm{\theta}_{i}^{\top}\phi(\bm{x})
. In iteration
s
, define
\widetilde{\phi}_{s}=\phi(\bm{x}_{s,1})-\phi(\bm{x}_{s,2})
.
And we define
\widetilde{f}_{i,s}=f_{i}(\bm{x}_{s,1})-f_{i}(\bm{x}_{s,2})=\bm{\theta}_{i}^{\top}\widetilde{\phi}_{s}
.

For any
\bm{\theta}_{f^{\prime}}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
, define

G_{t}(\bm{\theta}_{f^{\prime}})=\sum_{s\in[t-1]:\atop i_{s}\in\overline{C}_{t}}\left(\mu(\bm{\theta}_{f^{\prime}}^{\top}\widetilde{\phi}_{s})-\mu(\bm{\theta}_{i_{t}}^{\top}\widetilde{\phi}_{s})\right)\widetilde{\phi}_{s}+\lambda\bm{\theta}_{f^{\prime}}.

For
\lambda^{\prime}\in(0,1)
, setting
\bm{\theta}_{\bar{f}}=\lambda^{\prime}\bm{\theta}_{f^{\prime}_{1}}+(1-\lambda^{\prime})\bm{\theta}_{f^{\prime}_{2}}
.
and using the mean-value theorem, we get:

\displaystyle G_{t}(\bm{\theta}_{f^{\prime}_{1}})-G_{t}(\bm{\theta}_{f^{\prime}_{2}})

\displaystyle=\left[\sum_{s\in[t-1]:\atop i_{s}\in\overline{C}_{t}}\nabla\mu(\bm{\theta}_{\bar{f}}^{\top}\widetilde{\phi}_{s})\widetilde{\phi}_{s}\widetilde{\phi}_{s}^{\top}+\lambda\mathbf{I}\right](\bm{\theta}_{f^{\prime}_{1}}-\bm{\theta}_{f^{\prime}_{2}})

Define
\bm{M}_{t-1}=\left[\sum_{s\in[t-1]:\atop i_{s}\in\overline{C}_{t}}\nabla\mu(\bm{\theta}_{\bar{f}}^{\top}\widetilde{\phi}_{s})\widetilde{\phi}_{s}\widetilde{\phi}_{s}^{\top}+\lambda\mathbf{I}\right]
, and recall that
\bm{V}_{t-1}=\sum_{s\in[t-1]:\atop i_{s}\in\overline{C}_{t}}\widetilde{\phi}_{s}\widetilde{\phi}_{s}^{\top}+\frac{\lambda}{\kappa_{\mu}}\mathbf{I}
.
Then we have that
\bm{M}_{t-1}\succeq\kappa_{\mu}\bm{V}_{t-1}
and that
\bm{V}^{-1}_{t-1}\succeq\kappa_{\mu}\bm{M}^{-1}_{t-1}
. Then we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert G_{t}(\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{t})-\lambda\bm{\theta}_{i_{t}}\right\rVert_{\bm{V}_{t-1}^{-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\left\lVert G_{t}(\bm{\theta}_{i_{t}})-G_{t}(\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{t})\right\rVert_{\bm{V}_{t-1}^{-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\left\lVert\bm{M}_{t-1}(\bm{\theta}_{i_{t}}-\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{t})\right\rVert_{\bm{V}_{t-1}^{-1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\left(G_{t}(\bm{\theta}_{i_{t}})=\lambda\bm{\theta}_{i_{t}}\text{ by definition}\right)

\displaystyle=(\bm{\theta}_{i_{t}}-\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{t})^{\top}\bm{M}_{t-1}\bm{V}_{t-1}^{-1}\bm{M}_{t-1}(\bm{\theta}_{i_{t}}-\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{t})

\displaystyle\geq(\bm{\theta}_{i_{t}}-\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{t})^{\top}\bm{M}_{t-1}\kappa_{\mu}\bm{M}_{t-1}^{-1}\bm{M}_{t-1}(\bm{\theta}_{i_{t}}-\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{t})

\displaystyle=\kappa_{\mu}(\bm{\theta}_{i_{t}}-\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{t})^{\top}\bm{M}_{t-1}(\bm{\theta}_{i_{t}}-\overline{\bm{\theta}}_{t})
